# Recursive Self-Improvement (`RSI`) GPU Kernel Optimization Program (`PROGRAM.md`)

This specification defines the **autonomous Recursive Self-Improvement (`RSI`) kernel research, anti-reward-hacking verification, and vector-Pareto ratchet protocol** used in `AILab_CUDA_Kernel_Optimization_v1`.

Its architecture synthesizes three primary open-research patterns and adapts them to **multi-GPU SM80 (`8x NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` NVSwitch) LLM/SLM/Diffusion-LLM training kernels**:
1. **Declarative Goal + Immutable Evaluator + Keep/Revert Ratchet** (`autoresearch` `program.md` + `results.tsv`, cataloged in `usail-hkust/awesome-rsi-infra`).
2. **Hardware-Grounded Multi-Trial Kernel Evolution** (`NaiveAI` `Naive-N0.5-Flash` / `NaiveRT`: autonomous hypothesis -> custom CUDA/Triton mutation -> hardware profiling -> diagnostic self-repair).
3. **Multi-Stage Adversarial Numerical & Anti-Cheat Gatekeeping** (`AutoKernel`, `arXiv:2603.21331`, extended here with per-tensor `FP64` analytical oracles, unit-RMS `O(h^2)` Richardson extrapolation, L2-exceeding buffer rotation, and `FSDP2` multi-rank collective invariants).

---

## 1. Hardware Envelope & Measurement Contract

- **Target Node**: `8x NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`, `108` SMs, `166,912 B` configurable SRAM/SM, `40 MiB` L2 cache, `2,039 GB/s` HBM2e, `312 TFLOP/s` dense `BF16` Tensor Core, `NV12` `600 GB/s` bidirectional NVSwitch).
- **Clock & Cache Discipline**:
  - SM graphics clocks MUST be locked to **`1,410 MHz`** (`sudo nvidia-smi -lgc 1410,1410`) prior to any kept timing run and unlocked (`sudo nvidia-smi -rgc`) at cleanup.
  - Multi-rank runs MUST isolate compiler caches per rank (`TORCHINDUCTOR_CACHE_DIR`, `TRITON_CACHE_DIR`) to prevent concurrent SQLite/cubin corruption.
  - All publishable performance claims require **1 smoke pass + 3 independent OS-process `--mode full` repetitions (`rep1`, `rep2`, `rep3`)** summarized by median and interquartile range (`IQR`).

---

## 2. Mutable vs. Immutable Boundary

| Artifact | Mutability During RSI Loop | Role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `PROGRAM.md` | **Immutable** per experiment | Declares objective, target shape vector, numerical tolerances, HBM ceiling, and forbidden shortcuts. |
| `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py` | **Immutable** | Computes its own LF-normalized SHA-256 (`eval_harness_sha256`), generates hidden-seed inputs after candidate construction, enforces all correctness/anti-cheat gates, runs locked-clock rotating benchmarks, applies the Vector-Pareto Ratchet, and writes `rsi_ledger.tsv`. |
| Candidate Kernel Functions (`Trial 0..K`) | **Mutable** | Baseline (`Trial 0`) and iterative kernel mutations (`Trial 1..K`), each hashed via source AST/text SHA-256 (`candidate_sha256`). |
| `results/raw/<exp>/rsi_ledger.tsv` | **Append-Only** | Auditable history of every kept, reverted, or rejected trial, including compiler crashes and shape regressions. |
| `code/analyze_<exp>.py` | **Deterministic Validator** | Recomputes on-disk `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py` SHA-256 and rejects the experiment if `eval_harness_sha256` drifted or if any `KEEP` transition violated the ratchet invariants. |

---

## 3. Nine-Gate Anti-Reward-Hacking & Numerical Verification Protocol

Autonomous optimization loops fail silently when an agent improves wall-clock latency by weakening semantics, exploiting cache residency, or hiding regressions on unfavorable shapes. Every candidate in `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py` MUST pass all applicable gates before timing is eligible for `KEEP`:

1. **Global Precision & Autograd Flag Snapshot (`Gate G1`)**:
   - Snapshots `(allow_tf32_matmul, allow_tf32_cudnn, float32_matmul_precision, is_grad_enabled, are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled)` before and after every candidate invocation. Any mutation triggers immediate `REJECT_ANTI_CHEAT`.
2. **Post-Construction Fresh RNG & Dynamic Per-Step Inputs (`Gate G2`)**:
   - Inputs, masks, and upstream VJP cotangents (`dO`) are drawn from an evaluator-owned `torch.Generator` *after* candidate instantiation and rotated across timed steps, preventing memoization or pre-cached dynamic mask indices.
3. **Adversarial Shape, Ragged Boundary, Empty-Mask, Zero-Gate, & Stride Torture (`Gate G3`)**:
   - Attention kernels are tested on ragged document lengths `< W`, `== W`, `W + 1`, and partial trailing blocks (e.g., `[3, 4, 5, 10, 17]` for `W = 4`), plus non-contiguous sliced/transposed `Q/K/V` views (`stride(0) != H * D`).
   - Conditioning kernels (`adaLN-Zero`) are tested both at exact step-0 zero initialization (`gate == 0`, requiring `d_gate != 0`, `dx == dy`, and exact `d_shift == d_scale == 0`) and in the post-warmup nonzero regime (`gate != 0`).
   - Masked loss kernels are tested at `M = 0` (all tokens unmasked), `M = 1`, `M = N` (all tokens masked), and small diffusion times (`t = 1e-2`), preserving exact `1 / L` normalization and `1 / t` ELBO weighting.
4. **Input Immutability & Output Non-Aliasing (`Gate G4`)**:
   - Clones inputs prior to execution to verify candidates never corrupt caller tensors in-place, and executes two distinct inputs back-to-back to ensure returned tensors do not alias a static overwritten buffer.
5. **Per-Tensor `FP64` Analytical Oracle (`Gate G5`)**:
   - Compares *every* forward output and *every* input/parameter gradient tensor individually (`rel_l2 <= max_fp64_rel_l2` and `max_abs` guard when reference norm is zero) against an independent `FP64` reference implementation. Never pool small parameter/conditioning gradients (`[N_blks, D]`) into large activation norms (`[N, D]`).
6. **Supplementary `FP64` Unit-RMS `O(h^2)` Richardson Extrapolation (`Gate G6`)**:
   - On `FP64`-capable custom autograd operators, probes central finite differences along a unit-RMS perturbation (`||d||_rms = 1`) at `h = 2e-3` vs `h = 1e-3` (with curvature guard `|err(2h)| > 1e-10`), verifying error ratio `err(2h) / err(h) in [3.90, 4.10]` (`~4.000x`).
7. **L2-Exceeding Rotating Buffer Pool & Stream-Synchronized Timing (`Gate G7`)**:
   - Allocates a multi-slot input rotation ring whose aggregate footprint exceeds **`96 MiB` (`> 2.4x` A100's `40 MiB` L2 cache)** when benchmarking memory-bound operators, and synchronizes CUDA streams + records both `max_memory_allocated` and `max_memory_reserved` alongside locked-clock SM telemetry.
8. **Vector-Pareto Ratchet (`Gate G8`)**:
   - Each RSI track evaluates a **shape vector** $\mathcal{S} = \{s_1, \dots, s_M\}$ (e.g., diffusion block sizes $W \in \{4, 16, 64, 256\}$), never a cherry-picked scalar point.
   - Decision rule for candidate $c$ against baseline $b_0$ and current incumbent $c^\star$:
     - Any compile/runtime exception $\implies$ `REJECT_COMPILE_OR_RUNTIME`
     - Any failed `G1..G6` check $\implies$ `REJECT_CORRECTNESS` or `REJECT_ANTI_CHEAT`
     - Peak HBM $> \text{hbm\_cap\_mib}$ $\implies$ `REJECT_MEMORY`
     - $\max_{s \in \mathcal{S}} \frac{t_c(s)}{t_{b_0}(s)} > 1 + \tau_{\text{reg}}$ (`2.0%` default) $\implies$ **`REJECT_SHAPE_REGRESSION`** (exposing partial optimizations that win large tiles while crippling small tiles!)
     - $\text{geomean}_{s \in \mathcal{S}}\, t_c(s) \le (1 - \tau_{\text{gain}})\,\text{geomean}_{s \in \mathcal{S}}\, t_{c^\star}(s)$ (`1.0%` default) $\implies$ **`KEEP`** ($c^\star \leftarrow c$); else **`REVERT_SLOWER`**.
9. **Multi-Rank `FSDP2` Collective Symmetry (`Gate G9`)**:
   - End-to-end distributed trials must invoke every `fully_shard` module an identical deterministic number of times across all `8` ranks (even if a rank receives `M = 0` active masked tokens) and verify 8-GPU `FSDP2` loss and sharded `dW` parity against a 1-GPU oracle.

---

## 4. Iterative RSI Loop Execution Pattern

For each optimization track:
1. **Anchor (`Trial 0`)**: Implement the exact literature baseline (`FlexAttention`, eager PyTorch, or `torch.compile`), pass `G1..G7`, and establish $b_0 = c^\star$. If the literal paper snippet hits a framework/compiler defect (such as PyTorch `2.13` TorchInductor's SymPy `bool == bool` `InductorError` on `BD3-LM` Figure 4), record `Trial 0a` (`REJECT_COMPILE_OR_RUNTIME`) in `rsi_ledger.tsv` before recording repaired `Trial 0b` (`BASELINE`).
2. **Hypothesize & Mutate (`Trial k`)**: Formulate a concrete hardware hypothesis (CTA occupancy, register spills, SRAM reuse, HBM byte traffic, or dead-branch pruning) and register the candidate in the immutable harness.
3. **Verify, Profile, & Ratchet**: Run `RSIVectorRatchet.evaluate_candidate(...)`. Inspect per-shape latencies, register/spill counts, and HBM peaks. When a trial is rejected (e.g., `REJECT_SHAPE_REGRESSION` at `W = 4` or `W = 64`), record the failure and its hardware diagnosis in `rsi_ledger.tsv`—never delete failed trials—and design `Trial k + 1` specifically to eliminate the diagnosed bottleneck.
4. **Compose End-to-End (`Suite C`)**: Stack only `KEEP`-ratcheted winners into the 8-GPU NVSwitch `FSDP2` training ablation (`Rung 1 .. Rung K`) to verify additive system-level throughput and memory scaling.

---

## 5. Multi-Round Roofline RSI & Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` `autoresearch` Fairness Protocol (`E128+`)

When advancing from Round-1 bootstrap winners (`E127`) to Round-2 roofline convergence and end-to-end fixed-wall-clock model-quality (`val_bpb`) optimization (`E128`, co-audited with `GPT 6 Astra, Max`), five additional fairness and anti-reward-hacking invariants govern every generation:

1. **Unmodified Cryptographic Evaluator Continuity**:
   - `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py` remains byte-for-byte frozen (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f07831179f4665fb83fee3c2b11e3269ccf106c3f412336bf77a4925904e`) across rounds, and Round-2 `Trial 0` (`BASELINE`) starts directly at the promoted Round-1 incumbent while reporting both algorithmic minimal and physical realized HBM bandwidth (accounting for any split-reduction scratch writes/reads) plus cubin register-spill counts (`n_spills == 0`).
2. **Zero-Advantage Pre-Timer Compilation Warmup**:
   - Steady-state fixed-wall-clock training trials warm representative forward/backward/optimizer/collective specializations *outside* the wall-clock timer, then discard all warmed state and restore the **exact canonical initial weights, fresh `FP32` master-weight `AdamW` state, data cursor `0`, and deterministic RNG seeds** prior to `dist.barrier(); torch.cuda.synchronize()`.
3. **Rank-0 Authoritative Synchronized Deadline**:
   - After each completed optimizer step and `torch.cuda.synchronize()`, Rank 0 checks `elapsed_s >= T_budget` and broadcasts `[stop_flag, elapsed_s]` across all `8` ranks so every rank executes the **exact same integer number of completed updates**.
4. **Wall-Clock-Parameterized Schedule & Preserved Optimizer Semantics**:
   - Warmup and cosine learning-rate decay are parameterized by synchronized wall-clock fraction $u = \min(\text{elapsed\_s} / T_{\text{budget}}, 1.0)$ rather than fixed step horizons, while `FP32` master-weight `AdamW` (`betas`, `eps`, `weight_decay`) and exact 1-`AllReduce` global `clip_grad_norm_` remain identical across kernel generations.
5. **Immutable Multi-Timestep Quadrature `val_bpb` + Untouched `2x`-Finer `test_bpb` Confirmation**:
   - Promoted generations must strictly improve deterministic `8`-node midpoint-quadrature corpus `val_bpb` (`total_weighted_nelbo_nats / (ln(2) * total_utf8_bytes)`), automatically roll back destabilizing mutations (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), and confirm generalization on a disjoint untouched `test` corpus with fresh corruption draws and `16`-node (`2x`-finer) quadrature (`untouched_test_bpb_16node`).

---

## 6. Round-3 Multi-Stream Residual Topology RSI (`DeepSeek mHC`, `arXiv:2512.24880`) & Architecture-Kernel Co-Design Protocol (`E129`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from intra-block operators to **macro-architectural multi-stream residual highways** (`n`-stream `Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections`, co-audited with `GPT 6 Astra, Max`), four domain-specific numerical and fairness gates are enforced:

1. **Division-Free Log-Domain Sinkhorn-Knopp (`20` Iterations) & `+115` Row-Offset Underflow Audit**:
   - Column-max shifting $\exp(Z - \max_{\text{col}} Z)$ guarantees nonzero columns, *not* nonzero rows: a `+115` offset on row `0` flushes rows `1..n-1` to `0.0` in `FP32`, triggering `0 / 0 = NaN` in the first row normalization $\mathcal{T}_r$. Log-domain alternating LSE projection ($L \leftarrow L - \text{LSE}_{\text{col}}(L)$, $L \leftarrow L - \text{LSE}_{\text{row}}(L)$) with `5 x 4` on-chip checkpointed backward ($dL = dL' - \exp(L')\sum dL'$) eliminates all divisions and underflow (`0` `NaN`s, `0` cubin spills) and enforces DeepSeek Eq. 9's exact finite-`20` `Col -> Row` operator order (`rt4` `Row -> Col` shift bias caught by `Gate G5`).
2. **Exact `Norm-After-Matmul` Denominator VJP (`dr` Gate) & Superfused `Pre+RMSNorm` `BF16` Boundary**:
   - Absorbing RMSNorm weight $\gamma$ into $A = \text{diag}(\gamma)\Phi$ (`Single-Scan Tensor-Core Proj+RMS`) avoids materializing $[N, nC]$ normalized states, and backward MUST retain the exact denominator gradient $-X \odot \frac{\sum_j q_j u_j}{nC \cdot r^3}$ (`rt3` omission rejected by `Gate G5` + `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`). Superfused `Pre + SublayerRMSNorm` preserves the exact `FP32 -> BF16 -> FP32` boundary and divides RMS strictly by true $C$ (never padded `BLOCK_C`).
3. **Selective Stream-Only Checkpointing (`mHC-SAC`, Eq. 20 $L_r^* = \sqrt{nL / (n+2)}$) & `FSDP2` Uniform-Dtype Collective Order**:
   - Storing only boundary `n`-stream states $X_{k L_r}$ while replaying lightweight stream merges inside active $L_r$ segments (`0` extra Attention/MLP FLOPs and `0` extra `FSDP2` `AllGather` collectives) empirically achieves its global HBM minimum at closed-form $L_r^* = 4$ (`n = 4, L = 24`), while keeping `FP32` gating scalars (`alpha`, `b`, `stream_emb`) in deterministic name-ordered `ignored_params` (never sorted by Python object `id(p)`!).
4. **Equal-Stream Symmetry-Saddle Audit (`dZ_res == 0`) & Matched-Step Width-Fair `1-Stream` Control**:
   - Identical stream initialization (`stream_emb = 0, phi = 0, b_res = 0`) traps `n`-stream `mHC` on a collapsed `stream_pairwise_cos = 1.0000` saddle (`Gen 3` -> `REVERT_VAL_BPB`), whereas broken-symmetry Hadamard/Birkhoff initialization + Dynamic `H_final^pre` readout (`Gen 4`) shatters stream symmetry while benchmarking fairly against an oracle-LR-tuned width-matched `1-Stream` baseline with **strictly more parameters** (`params_1stream > params_mhc`).

---

## 7. Round-4 Conditional Memory & Scalable $O(1)$ Lookup RSI (`DeepSeek Engram`, `arXiv:2601.07372`) & Sparsity-Allocation U-Curve Protocol (`E130`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from multi-stream residual routing (`mHC`) to **massive-table $O(1)$ N-gram Conditional Memory** (`Engram: Conditional Memory via Scalable Lookup`, `arXiv:2601.07372`, paired with `M = 4` `mHC`), four domain-specific numerical, communication, and scaling-law gates are enforced:

1. **Bitwise Surjective Tokenizer + Prime Multiplicative-XOR Hash & Packed-Document Boundary Isolation**:
   - NFKC/lowercase/whitespace surjective projection $P: V \to V'$ (`129,280 -> 99,546`, `-23.00%` effective vocabulary) and bitwise `uint64` multiplicative-XOR prime modulo hashing across $(n \in \{2, 3\}, k \in \{0..7\})$ MUST match DeepSeek's reference `engram_demo_v1.py` with **`0` hash mismatches** while clamping both N-gram lookbacks and dilated causal `ShortConv` taps ($w = 4, \delta = 3$) at `doc_start[t]` (`0.0` cross-document forward or gradient leakage).
2. **Zero-`DtoH`-Sync Tile-Fused Sorted Segmented Backward + Exact `JIT-Lazy-Adam(lr = 5x, wd = 0)`**:
   - Under Zipfian N-gram skew (`~2.87%` active rows/step), `torch.optim.SparseAdam` drops momentum drift on inactive steps (`> 51%` full-table divergence vs dense `Adam`), while `torch.unique_consecutive` forces a blocking GPU->CPU synchronization and `u = 0` serial hot-row tail. Pre-forward run-head `JIT-Lazy-Adam` catch-up + two-stage tile-segmented backward reduction (`num_warps = BLOCK_D // 32`, `0` cubin spills, `0` `DtoH` syncs, `0` HBM atomics) preserves exact dense `Adam(wd = 0)` trajectories (`~2.0e-6` `rel_l2`, `> 250,000x` closer than `SparseAdam`).
3. **Smooth `Eq. 6` Branch-Specific Context Gate + Zero-Transpose Split-CTA `ShortConv` & Forward-Only Demo Pitfall Audit**:
   - Quantifies two forward-only quirks in `engram_demo_v1.py` (`nn.RMSNorm(D)` `eps=None -> 0.0078125` in `BF16`, and `gate.abs().clamp_min(1e-6).sqrt() * gate.sign()` creating a `125.0 -> 0.0` backward Jacobian cliff with `8.72%` `BF16` zero-crossing `dH` jitter), enforces `arXiv:2601.07372` `Eq. 6` smooth $\sigma\big(\frac{\text{RMS}(q)^\top \text{RMS}(k)}{\sqrt{d}}\big)$ (`<= 1.67e-3` `BF16` `dH` parity, `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`), and fuses dual-RMSNorm + `M = 4` gating + `[B, T, M, D]`-native depthwise dilated causal `ShortConv` (`Split-64` `dConvW` across all `108` SMs, zero `.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()` copies).
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Zipf-Deduplicated `AllToAllv` + Layer-2 Async Stream Prefetch (`Gate G9`) & Iso-Param / Iso-FLOPs Sparsity-Allocation U-Curve**:
   - Sharding prime tables across 8 ranks with per-destination sorted unique `int64` row deduplication + `AllToAllv` + Layer-2 `prefetch_stream` overlap (`Blocks 0..1` hiding NVSwitch lookup before `Block 2`) MUST match a 1-GPU unsharded oracle across multi-step `JIT-Lazy-Adam` updates (`reassembled_8shard_3step_master_table_rel_l2 < 1e-5`), while matched-step `val_bpb` scaling across $\rho \in \{1.00, 0.75, 0.25, 0.00\}$ (using `100%` dropless `torch._grouped_mm` MoE dispatch) + `Iso-Active-FLOPs Dense` + `Iso-Total-Param Wider Dense` verifies `arXiv:2601.07372` Section 3's interior hybrid optimum ($\rho^* \approx 0.75$).

## 8. Round-5 Nonlinear Fast-Weight Test-Time Training RSI (`LaCT`, `arXiv:2505.23884`, ICLR 2026) & Within-Chunk `CP = 8` Protocol (`E131`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from static $O(1)$ lookup memory (`Engram`) to **online in-context nonlinear `SwiGLU` fast-weight Test-Time Training** (`LaCT: Test-Time Training Done Right`, `arXiv:2505.23884`, official repo `a1600012888/LaCT`), four domain-specific numerical, compiler, distributed, and scaling-law gates are enforced:

1. **Zero-Spill Split-`H` Lock-Free `Fwd-of-Bwd` + `out_dtype=float32` SM80 Tensor-Core Epilogue & Official 3-Accumulator Spill Audit**:
   - Holding 3 simultaneous `FP32` Tensor-Core accumulators inside a single Triton CTA (`ttt_operation_fused_kernel.py`) spills `4,582` (`128x128`) to `4,024` (`128x256`) registers to local HBM on SM80 and crashes with `OutOfResources: 229,376 > 166,912` at `BLOCK_K = 64, num_stages = 3`. Decoupling vendor `cuBLAS` `torch.bmm(..., out_dtype=torch.float32)` Tensor-Core contractions (`7,055x` more accurate across 8-rank `AllReduce` shards than `BF16`-truncated epilogues) from 9 zero-spill (`0` cubin spills, `<= 48` regs) Triton epilogue and split-`H` lock-free 2nd-order gradient kernels satisfies strict `FP64` and `O(h^2)` Richardson (`~4.000x`) gates across all 10 inputs (`W0, W1, W2, Q, K, V, lr0, lr1, lr2, momentum`).
2. **Independent 3-Matrix Batched `FP32` `Muon-NS5` (`[3*B_h, R, K]`) + Analytical Fréchet VJP & Official Concatenated `[dW0; dW2]` Bug Audit**:
   - Orthogonalizing concatenated `[dW0; dW2]` jointly (`ttt_operation_fused_kernel.py` L164/268) couples singular spectra across gate and value projections (`> 78%` `rel_l2` corruption and `0.226x` singular-value collapse when `||dW0|| != ||dW2||`), while `BF16` 5-step cubic Newton-Schulz amplifies mantissa rounding `~41x` (`~3.1e-2` error) and `TorchInductor` folds away naive Ozaki `BF16` splits (`(A - A.bfloat16().float()).bfloat16()`). Batching `(dW0^T, dW2^T, dW1)` independently as `[3*B_h, R, K]` (`R = \min(H, D)`) in `FP32` with `cuBLAS` `baddbmm` epilogues + checkpointed analytical Fréchet VJP achieves `< 2.0e-6` `FP64` parity (`> 15,000x` closer than eager `BF16`) in `15` forward / `25` backward launches (`vs 40 / 65`).
3. **Division-Free `L2Norm` Sphere Retention + Compact Chunk-Boundary State Checkpointing & Official `0 / 0 = NaN` Audit**:
   - Reconstructing $\hat{w} = y / \text{tgt\_scale}$ in backward (`l2norm_triton_kernels.py`) yields `0.0 / 0.0 = NaN` on zero-initialized fast-weight rows; storing normalized unit vectors $\hat{u} = w_{\text{cand}} / (\|w_{\text{cand}}\|_2 + \epsilon)$ directly guarantees `0` `NaN`s (`Gate G5`), while checkpointing only compact `[num_transitions, B_h, ...]` fast-weight boundary states (recomputing per-chunk activations on chip) beats `torch.compile(dynamic=False)` in both step latency and peak HBM.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Within-Chunk Sub-Chunk `CP = 8` + 1-Hop P2P Halo `SWA` (`Gate G9`) & Beyond-Window Associative Recall Scaling Law**:
   - Exploiting chunk-level linearity $\nabla_W \mathcal{L}(K_{1:C}, V_{1:C}) = \sum_{r=0}^{P-1} \nabla_W \mathcal{L}(K^{(r)}, V^{(r)})$ replaces `O(S_global)` `Q/K/V` `AllGather` with `O(H_{\text{fw}} D_{\text{ttt}})` `FP32` fast-weight `AllReduce` (`1` per chunk transition, keeping base-weight gradients un-summed across ranks until outer `DDP` to prevent `P`-fold gradient inflation) + 1-hop NVSwitch `batch_isend_irecv` `[B, W_{\text{swa}}, H, D]` `SWA` boundary halos (`Gate G9` parity `<= 6e-3` vs 1-GPU full-sequence oracle). In fixed-wall-clock `val_bpb` + matched-step iso-parameter ablations, `LaCT(SwiGLU + Muon + Momentum + L2Norm)` must vault beyond-window (`>= 3x W_swa`) associative recall from `~6.25%` chance (`SWA-only`) and `~7%` (`unorthogonalized SwiGLU-GD`) to **`> 98%`** while auto-reverting `L2Norm` removal (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and small-chunk `C = 32` over-synchronization (`REVERT_SLOWER`).

## 9. Round-6 Depth-Wise Selective Residual Aggregation RSI (`Moonshot Kimi Attention Residuals`, `arXiv:2603.15031`) & Interleaved `1F1B` Cross-Stage Caching Protocol (`E132`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from sequence-axis fast-weight memory (`LaCT`) to **depth-axis content-dependent residual routing** (`Attention Residuals` & `Block AttnRes`, Moonshot Kimi Team, `arXiv:2603.15031`, official repo `MoonshotAI/Attention-Residuals` + community Triton reference `Hydra-2P` `manishklach/attnres-kernel-lab`), four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/atomic, distributed pipeline, and PreNorm-dilution gates are enforced:

1. **Query-Scale `RMSNorm` Absorption (`q_eff = w_s * gamma_key_s`) + Closed-Form Radial Logit-Adjoint Identity (`0` Atomics, `14/14` `0`-Spill Cubins)**:
   - Community `Hydra-2P` (`manishklach/attnres-kernel-lab`) drops per-layer key `RMSNorm` scale `gamma_key`, caps `D <= 256`, and uses `(N*M)`-way global `FP32` `tl.atomic_add` across `dq` and `dv` (`> 36,000` atomic writers/element at `N = 8, M = 4,096`), regressing slower than eager PyTorch at `D = 2,048` (`REJECT_SHAPE_REGRESSION`), while naive 1-pass `BD = 4096, num_warps = 16` hits SM80's `65,536 / 512 = 128` register/thread ceiling (`28` cubin spills). Absorbing $\gamma_{\text{key}, s}$ into $q_{\text{eff}, s} = w_s \odot \gamma_{\text{key}, s}$ (`0` extra HBM bytes, exact at mandatory step-0 $w_s = 0$ init) and applying the closed-form radial identity $\langle \sum_s dx_{m,s,n} q_{\text{eff}, s}, \hat{v}_{n,m} \rangle = \sum_s dx_{m,s,n} x_{m,s,n}$ eliminates all cross-`D` reductions in 2D Split-`M` backward (`0` global atomics, `0` cubin spills across all `14` kernels up to `D = 4,096`, `FP32 rel_l2 <= 2e-5`, `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `Fig. 2` `i = 0` duplicate-source & missing-radial-Jacobian exploits).
2. **Zero-`O1_bar`-Retention Two-Node Unbind + Superfused `Phase 2 Online-Softmax Merge + Sublayer Pre-RMSNorm`**:
   - Retaining Phase-1 `[S, M, D]` `FP32` inter-block outputs across sequential Transformer blocks wastes gigabytes of HBM, while Python slicing `o1_bar[s]` spawns `S` separate `[S, M, D]` `SelectBackward0` zero-fill allocations per block. Using `dlse1_s = -dx_part_s` and $\langle d\bar{o}^{(1)}_s, \bar{o}^{(1)}_s \rangle = \sum_n \tilde{\alpha}_{s,n} \langle d\bar{o}^{(1)}_s, v_n \rangle$ (`0` `O1_bar` bytes saved), `RSIPhase1UnbindInterBlockFn`, and superfusing `Phase 2` online-softmax merge directly with sublayer `Pre-RMSNorm` (exploiting `AttnRes`'s no-skip pre-norm topology so `h_s` is never materialized) must beat both Moonshot `Fig. 2` Eager and `fullgraph=True` `torch.compile` in latency and peak HBM across both batched (`Track 1`) and sequential multi-block (`Track 2`) stacks.
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Interleaved `1F1B` (`P = 8, V = 4`, `32` Virtual Stages, `142`-Tick Megatron-LM Causal Schedule) + Cross-Stage Virtual-Rank Block Cache (`arXiv:2603.15031` Sec. 4.1 `Eq. 7-8` & `Fig. 3`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Caching completed block representations locally across virtual chunks $v \in \{0..V-1\}$ on each physical rank reduces steady-state hop payloads from `125` tensors (`Full AttnRes`) and `32` tensors (`Naive Block AttnRes`, `Eq. 7`) to a constant `P - 1 = 7` incremental blocks (`Eq. 8`, `9.926x` total P2P reduction vs `Full AttnRes` and `2.596x` vs Naive `Block AttnRes`), while its exact adjoint accumulates cross-virtual-chunk `db_k` locally in HBM before returning `7` tensors along the entry hop (`Gate G9` `FP32` parity `< 2e-5` vs 1-GPU monolithic 32-stage oracle, catching missing cross-chunk gradient accumulation at `100%` `rel_l2` corruption).
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop (`Table 4` `w_l = 0` & Key-`RMSNorm` Guard) + Matched-Step Deep-24-Layer (`48`-Sublayer) PreNorm-Dilution Law**:
   - Under a fixed wall-clock `8xA100 DDP` budget, Round-6 RSI `Block AttnRes (N = 8)` must maximize completed steps and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting `Table 4` invariant violations (`w_l != 0` / disabled key `RMSNorm` -> `REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and per-sublayer `Full AttnRes (N = 48)` compute overhead (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`). At matched steps across `5` residual topologies (`Standard PreNorm`, `DenseFormer`, `Highway`, `Block AttnRes N=8`, `Full AttnRes N=48`), `Block AttnRes` must beat `Standard PreNorm`, `DenseFormer`, and `Highway` in `val_bpb` while suppressing `Standard PreNorm`'s `> 300x` hidden-norm depth explosion (`||h_L||_rms / ||h_1||_rms`, `Fig. 5(b)`).

## 10. Round-7 Tokenizer-Free End-to-End Dynamic Chunking RSI (`H-Net`, `arXiv:2507.07955`) & NVSwitch Cost-LPT `AllToAllv` Protocol (`E133`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from fixed-token depth-axis residual routing (`AttnRes`) to **tokenizer-free content-dependent sequence compression & hierarchical UTF-8 byte modeling** (`H-Net: Dynamic Chunking for End-to-End Hierarchical Sequence Modeling`, Hwang, Wang & Gu, `arXiv:2507.07955`, official repo `goombalab/hnet`), four domain-specific numerical, SM80 cubin/grid, distributed load-balancing, and compression-contract gates are enforced:

1. **`FP32`-Accumulating Merged-`QK` Cosine Router (`Eq. 4`) + Radial-Jacobian Adjoint + Unbounded `gridDim.x` (`17/17` `0`-Spill Cubins)**:
   - Official `goombalab/hnet` (`hnet/modules/dc.py` L93–97) computes `F.normalize(q)` and `einsum` in `BF16` (`ULP ~ 0.0039` near `p_t = 0.5`), flipping discrete boundaries $b_t = \mathbb{I}[p_t > 0.5]$ and causing `> 20%` forward/backward corruption (`REJECT_CORRECTNESS`), while `ChunkLayer` (`dc.py` L178/185) calls host-synchronizing `int(num_tokens.max())` (breaking `torch.compile(fullgraph=True)`) plus two $O(BL \log L)$ `torch.argsort` radix sorts, and `DeChunkLayer` (`dc.py` L257–293) delegates scalar-gate EMA $\bar{z}_j = P_j \hat{z}_j + (1 - P_j)\bar{z}_{j-1}$ to `mamba_ssm` via `BF16` `dt = -log(1 - p)` & `x = z / dt` quantization. Round-7 RSI replaces these with `FP32`-accumulating merged-`QK` cosine routing (`b_0 = 1` per packed `cu_seqlens` document, zero cross-document leakage, exact radial VJP $(I - \hat{u}\hat{u}^\top)/\|u\|$), zero-sort prefix-sum `cu_seqlens` compaction, 2-level Blelloch associative scalar-gate affine EMA scan + reverse adjoint scan, and lock-free Split-Span upsample backward + superfused `STE(c_t) == 1` residual (`17/17` Triton kernels at `0` cubin spills, `max 32 regs` vs `72` spills for naive 1-pass `[8, BD=4096], nw=16`, with unbounded sequence axes placed on `gridDim.x` supporting $T > 65,535$, `FP32 rel_l2 <= 1e-5`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Isolated Dynamic-Chunking Operators (`Track 1`) & Ragged `Varlen FlashAttention-2` Hierarchical Stage (`Track 2`)**:
   - Superfused Round-7 RSI DC operators (`Track 1`) and full `Encoder -> DC -> Varlen Main Network -> DeChunk -> Decoder` stages (`Track 2`, promoting `w_res` from `goombalab/hnet`'s `FP32` SIMT matmul to `BF16` Tensor Cores with `FP32` epilogue accumulation) must strictly dominate Official `goombalab/hnet`, Packed Eager PyTorch, and `torch.compile(dynamic=True)` in both latency and peak HBM while rejecting `BF16` router boundary-flip, omitted radial-Jacobian, and omitted EMA-adjoint exploits.
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Whole-Document Cost-LPT `AllToAllv` + Exact Global Token-Weighted Ratio Loss (`Eq. 10`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Natural variable-entropy UTF-8 shards (high-entropy code/math `~1.7` bytes/chunk vs repetitive logs/tables `~11` bytes/chunk) create `~3x` Main-Transformer quadratic compute imbalance across DDP ranks, while naive rank-local `load_balancing_loss` (`hnet/utils/train.py` L19–40) introduces $\mathrm{Cov}_r(F_r, G_r) \neq 0$ router gradient bias (`> 70%` `dw_qk` error on heterogeneous shards). Whole-document Longest-Processing-Time (`Cost-LPT`, $c_d = 12 D_{\text{main}} M_d + 4 M_d^2$) `AllToAllv` dispatches already-compressed chunks (`> 3x` smaller NVSwitch payload than raw-byte redistribution) while exact global $(F_{\text{glob}}, G_{\text{glob}})$ `AllReduce` preserves 3-step multi-update equivalence against a monolithic global-batch oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss rel_diff <= 1e-5`, `param rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `grad rel_l2 <= 5e-4`).
4. **Compression-Governed Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Byte-Level Phase-Shift & Noise Robustness Law**:
   - Under a fixed wall-clock `8xA100 DDP` budget with `target_ratio_n = 4.0`, the Pareto ratchet enforces the hierarchical compression contract (`bytes_per_chunk >= 2.0` and non-regressing throughput `>= 0.88x` incumbent steps) so removing Eq. (10) Ratio Loss (`alpha = 0` dense boundary collapse `bytes_per_chunk -> 1.02`, `2x` throughput collapse) is caught and reverted (`REVERT_SLOWER_COMPRESSION_COLLAPSE`) alongside removing Eq. (5) EMA smoothing (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), while Round-7 RSI + Stage LR Modulation maximizes updates and improves both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb`. At matched steps across `5` byte-level architectures (`Isotropic Byte SWA`, `Static Stride-4`, `SpaceByte Delimiter`, `Unsmoothed DC`, `Full H-Net RSI`), learned `H-Net` dynamic chunking must achieve the lowest clean and 1-byte phase-shifted + character-noisy `test_bpb` while aligning boundaries with latent morphemes.

## 11. Round-8 Data-Dependent Non-Commutative Householder Reflections & Forgetting-Gated Attention RSI (`PaTH-FoX`, `arXiv:2505.16381v2` & `arXiv:2503.02130`) & Compact WY `Ring PaTH-CP = 8` Protocol (`E134`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from hierarchical byte compression (`H-Net`) to **data-dependent non-commutative orthogonal state transitions in softmax attention** (`PaTH Attention: Position Encoding via Accumulating Householder Transformations`, Yang, FX-Team, Kim & Rush, `arXiv:2505.16381v2`, official repo `fla-org/flash-linear-attention` `fla/ops/path_attn/` paired with `FoX: Forgetting Transformer`, Lin et al., `arXiv:2503.02130`), four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed context-parallel, and state-tracking extrapolation gates are enforced:

1. **Block-Recurrent `[BK=64, 64]` `UT`-Transform WY Representation (`Eq. 3–6`) + Zero-Spill Two-Phase Split-`K` `64+64` Backward (`22/22` `0`-Spill Cubins vs `3,732` Spills in `fla-org`)**:
   - Cumulative Householder products $P_{ij} = \prod_{m=i+1}^{j}(I - \beta_m w_m w_m^\top)$ (`\|w_m\|_2 = 1`, $\beta_m \in (0, 2)$) are non-commutative and cost $O(T^2 K)$ if materialized sequentially. While `fla-org/flash-linear-attention` (`fla/ops/path_attn/`) introduces the chunkwise compact WY `UT` representation $\prod_{m=1}^{C}(I - \beta_m w_m w_m^\top) = I - W T W^\top$ where $T = (D_{\beta}^{-1} + \text{strictLower}(W W^\top))^{-1}$, its `chunk_cumprod_householder_bwd_kernel` (`chunk_cumprod_householder_bwd.py`) holds four `[BT=64, K=128]` matrices (`b_k, b_v, b_dk, b_dh`) simultaneously in `FP32` registers, causing **`3,732` bytes of cubin register spills** (`1888` stores / `1844` loads) on SM80 at `K = 128` (`5.13x` slowdown), while `intra_chunk_preprocess_bwd_fn` (`intra_chunk_preprocess_bwd.py`) invokes `@input_guard(contiguous=True)` triggering `4` separate `.contiguous()` HBM copies on `[B, T, H, K]` inputs. Round-8 RSI resolves both via: (a) **Block-Recurrent `[BK=64, 64]` `UT`-Transform WY Prep** accumulating $W W^\top$ over `K_SLICES` before solving $T$ on-chip in registers; (b) **Two-Phase Split-`K` `64+64` Inter-Chunk Householder Backward** (`Phase 1` computes chunk scalar dot summaries $M = W^\top K_{\text{next}}$, $S_m = W^\top dK_{\text{adj}}$, and $dK_{\text{adj}} \leftarrow P_{\text{chunk}}^\top dK_{\text{adj}}$ via `64`-slice streaming; `Phase 2` emits $d\tilde{k}, dw_{\text{inter}}, dT_{\text{inter}}$ per `BK = 64` slice with `0` spills); and (c) **Superfused `ShortConv(3) + SiLU + Safe-L2Norm + 2*sigmoid(beta)` Frontend + Analytical Backward** (`22/22` Triton kernels across `40` compiled specializations at **`0` cubin spills**, `FP32 max_rel_l2 = 9.95e-7`, `BF16 = 0.0079`, `8/8` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: commutative additive rotation, omitted $dT = -T^\top dA_{\text{strictLower}} T^\top$ inverse-triangular chain rule, `allow_tf32` mutation, and un-decayed `FoX` online softmax).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `PaTH-FoX` Attention (`Track 1`) & End-to-End `ShortConv+L2Norm+PaTH-FoX` Block (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`K \in \{64, 128\}`, `T \in \{1024..4096\}`, `GQA 16Q/4KV` & `MHA`), Round-8 RSI must strictly dominate `Eager`, `torch.compile`, and `Official fla-org/flash-linear-attention` `parallel_path_attn` in both latency and peak HBM across both core `PaTH-FoX` attention (`Track 1`) and full `ShortConv3 + SiLU + L2Norm + BetaSigmoid + PaTH-FoX` blocks (`Track 2`), while automatically rejecting `K = 128` full-width register-spill regressions (`REJECT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Compact WY `Ring PaTH-CP = 8` Context Parallelism (`Gate G9`)**:
   - Because $P_{ij} = P_{i,\text{split}} P_{\text{split},j}$ does not commute across sequence shards, shard-local Householder reset without inter-rank reflection handoff corrupts cross-shard queries/keys (`0.3105` parameter drift), while naive dense $[K, K]$ orthogonal matrix ring handoff incurs $O(P \cdot N_{\text{chunks}} K^3)$ matmul overhead. Passing compact WY factors $(W_{\text{rank}}, T_{\text{rank}})$ + cumulative `FoX` log-decay across ranks (`Ring PaTH-CP = 8`) preserves 3-step multi-update equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `max_param_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-5`) while beating All-Gather Eager, All-Gather Official `fla-org`, and Dense-Matrix Ring Handoff at `T_global = 8,192, K = 128`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Position-Encoding & `2x` Length-Extrapolation Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `15.0s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on interleaved language modeling + non-commutative cyclic `S_4` state-tracking (`SWAP_01`, `SWAP_12`, `SWAP_23`, `ROT_L`, `ROT_R`, `REV`), Round-8 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and single-touch `test_bpb` over `Eager`, `torch.compile`, and `Official fla-org` while reverting `L2Norm` removal (`\|w\|_2 \neq 1` non-orthogonal explosion -> `REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and small-chunk `C = 16` over-chunking (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`260` steps) across `5` position encodings (`P0_nope`, `P1_rope`, `P2_alibi`, `P3_fox`, `P4_path_fox_rsi`), `PaTH-FoX RSI` must achieve the lowest clean and `2x` length-extrapolated (`T_{\text{train}} = 256 \to T_{\text{extrap}} = 512`) `test_bpb` while vaulting non-commutative `S_4` state-tracking accuracy from `~45%–61%` (`NoPE`/`RoPE`/`ALiBi`/`FoX`) to **`~90%+`**.

## 12. Round-9 Recursive Latent-Depth Transformers & Dynamic Token-Level Routing RSI (`Mixture-of-Recursions`, `arXiv:2507.10524v2`, NeurIPS 2025) & Recursion-Pinned `FSDP2` + `MoR-LPT AllToAllv` Protocol (`E135`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from sequence-axis orthogonal state transitions (`PaTH-FoX`) to **weight-tied vertical latent-depth reasoning & adaptive token-level recursion** (`Mixture-of-Recursions: Learning Dynamic Recursive Depths for Adaptive Token-Level Computation`, Bae, Kim, Bayat, Kim, Ha, Schuster, Fisch, Harutyunyan, Ji, Courville & Yun, `arXiv:2507.10524v2`, NeurIPS 2025; official repo `raymin0223/mixture_of_recursions` commit `82c97f29cd140efa01f5cc4fe0de73a4c3b6ffd6`), four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed tied-weight collective, and causal latent-depth scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Asymmetric Position-Indexed Rectangular Causal-Gather `Flash-MoR` (`Q_active[K_r] x KV_shared[T]`) + Zero-Spill Varlen Gather-`RMSNorm`-`RoPE` & Gated Scatter-Residual (`0` Cubin Spills, `0` Atomics, Upstream `raymin0223/mixture_of_recursions` Forensics)**:
   - Official `raymin0223/mixture_of_recursions` suffers from six concrete training-kernel pathologies: (i) when `kv_sharing.enable=True` (`expert_choice_router.py:225-240`, `token_choice_router.py:222-245`), it runs the **entire** `[B, T, D]` QKV + Attention + SwiGLU block on all $T$ tokens at every recursion $r = 2 \dots N_r$ and discards inactive rows via `torch.gather`, saving `0%` compute FLOPs because standard `FlashAttention` `is_causal=True` aligns unequal $Q/K$ lengths to the bottom-right diagonal (`q_idx - k_idx == k_r - T`) rather than original coordinates `n <= pos_q[m]`; (ii) `token_choice_router.py:75-164` executes $B$ host-blocking `torch.where` `DtoH` syncs per recursion, `rnn_utils.pad_sequence`, and a dense 4D `[new_bs, 1, S_max, S_max]` mask (`> 1 GiB` at `T = 8,192`); (iii) `token_choice_router.py:293-300` drops `g_t^r` on unfinished transitions (`r < i`, violating Eq. 2.2) and aliases `updates[batch_idx] = ...; x = updates`, mutating `x` in-place via `CopySlices` on $r \ge 1$; (iv) `expert_choice_router.py:289` computes `torch.logsumexp(_router_weights, dim=-1).square()` on shape `[bs, k, 1]`, collapsing identically to `_router_weights ** 2` and penalizing negative unselected logits against `BCEWithLogitsLoss`; (v) `expert_choice_router.py:252` & `token_choice_router.py:147` slice `position_ids[:, :top_k]` / broadcast `emb[0]`; and (vi) `modeling_llama.py:232` leaves `# TODO: support MoRLlamaDecoderLayer` broken under `gradient_checkpointing=True`. Round-9 RSI resolves all six via: (a) **Position-Indexed Rectangular Causal-Gather `Flash-MoR`** (`BM=64, BN=64` at `D_h=64`; `BM=64, BN=32` fwd / `BM=32, BN=32` 2-pass key-parallel GQA-native bwd at `D_h=128` with **`0` cubin spills** and **`0` global atomics**, pruning 100% of future KV tiles `n_start > pos_q[m_end-1]` in fwd/dQ and jumping via on-chip binary search to the first eligible query `pos_q[m_first] >= n_start` in dKV while writing exact `0.0` on trailing zero-consumer key tiles); and (b) **Fused Gather-`PreRMSNorm` + Position-Indexed `RoPE(pos_q[m])` + `Eq. 2.1/2.2` Gated Scatter-Residual** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` margin-verified `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: bottom-right naive `FA2` causal alignment error on `Q[K_r] x KV[T]`, omitted `dK_shared/dV_shared` cross-recursion gradient accumulation, `allow_tf32` mutation, and omitted `dg_t^r` router gate VJP).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Isolated Rectangular `Flash-MoR` (`Track 1`) & End-to-End `Middle-Cycle` ($N_r = 3$) Recursive `MoR` Stacks (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`D_h \in \{64, 128\}`, `T \in \{2048..8192\}`, `GQA 16Q/4KV` & `32Q/8KV`, active capacities $k_r / T \in \{2/3, 1/3\}$), Round-9 RSI must strictly dominate Official `raymin0223/mixture_of_recursions` (`kv_sharing` full-sequence waste & `pad_sequence` 4D-mask Token-Choice), Exact Masked Eager/SDPA, and `torch.compile` in both latency and peak HBM while rejecting `D_h = 128` monolithic `64x128` `dKV` register-spill regressions (`REJECT_SHAPE_REGRESSION` / `REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Recursion-Pinned Tied-Weight `FSDP2` (`1` `AllGather` + `1` `ReduceScatter` vs `2 N_r` `AllGather`s) + Dimensionally Exact Whole-Document `MoR-LPT AllToAllv` + Global `(P_global, f_global)` Balancing (`Gate G9`)**:
   - Calling a `fully_shard` block $N_r = 3$ times with default `reshard_after_forward=True` triggers `2 N_r = 6` `AllGather`s (`3` fwd + `3` bwd), whereas pinning unsharded tied parameters across the $N_r$-step recursion window executes **`1` `AllGather` + `1` `ReduceScatter`** (`6x` fewer `AllGather` bytes, matching §2.1's $N_r$-iter/gather goal). On heterogeneous reasoning/boilerplate shards, whole-document `MoR-LPT AllToAllv` using exact width-aware FLOPs $\sum_{r=2}^{N_r} [2 k_{d,r}(2D^2 + 3 D D_{\text{ff}}) + 4 D \sum_m (p_{d,m}+1)]$ + token-weighted global balancing loss $\mathcal{L}_{\text{bal}}^{(p)} = W \sum_j f_j^{\text{glob}} (\frac{1}{N_{\text{glob}}}\sum_{t \in \mathcal{T}^{(p)}} p_{t,j})$ must preserve 3-step multi-update equivalence against a 1-GPU monolithic global-batch oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-5`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4` vs massive rank-local `P_i * f_i` router gradient bias).
4. **Strict-Causal Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Parameter-Sharing & Multi-Hop Latent-Depth Reasoning Scaling Law**:
   - All validation/test evaluations enforce **strict prefix-invariant causal routing** (`prefix_invariance_max_abs_diff == 0.0`, auditing train-time batch top-$k$ future-token leakage separately). Under a fixed `12.0s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on interleaved language modeling + `1/2/3`-hop modular composition tokens, Round-9 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting upstream `[bs, k, 1]` `logsumexp` / unregularized depth-collapse mutations (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and over-recursive $N_r = 6$ throughput collapse (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`240` steps) across `5` architectures (`Iso-Param Shallow Vanilla`, `Fixed Middle-Cycle N_r=3`, `MoR Token-Choice Rec-Cache`, `MoR Expert-Choice Aux-BCE Rec-Cache`, `MoR Causal Recursive-KV-Share RSI`), `MoR Causal Recursive-KV-Share RSI` must achieve the best strict-causal `val_bpb` / `test_bpb` and `3`-hop latent reasoning accuracy while adaptively allocating deeper mean recursion depth to `3`-hop tokens than `1`-hop tokens ($\mathbb{E}[r \mid \text{3-hop}] > \mathbb{E}[r \mid \text{1-hop}]$).

## 13. Round-10 Common-Mode Attention-Noise Cancellation & Paired-Head Differential FlashAttention RSI (`Differential Transformer V1 & V2`, `arXiv:2410.05258`, ICLR 2025 + Jan 2026 `DIFF V2`) & Dual-LSE `Ring DiffAttn-CP = 8` Protocol (`E136`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from vertical latent-depth token routing (`Mixture-of-Recursions`) to **common-mode attention-noise cancellation via paired differential softmax streams** (`Differential Transformer`, Ye, Dong, Xia, Sun, Zhu, Huang & Wei, Microsoft Research, `arXiv:2410.05258`, ICLR 2025 + January 2026 `Differential Transformer V2`; official repo `microsoft/unilm/tree/master/Diff-Transformer` & `Diff-Transformer-V2`), four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed context-parallel, and multi-needle distractor-cancellation gates are enforced:

1. **Co-Scheduled Same-GQA-Group Paired-Query `Flash-DiffAttn-V2` & Paired-Stream `Flash-DiffAttn-V1` + Fused `SubLN` Epilogue (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `microsoft/unilm/Diff-Transformer` V1 & V2 Forensics)**:
   - Official `microsoft/unilm/Diff-Transformer` (`V1` & `V2`) suffers from five concrete training-kernel and mathematical pathologies: (i) `multihead_diffattn.py:80,86,101` reshapes `k` to `(bsz, src_len, 2 * num_kv_heads, head_dim)` and calls `repeat_kv(k.transpose(1, 2), n_rep)` before `.view(bsz, num_heads, 2, tgt_len, src_len)`, which for any GQA configuration (`n_rep >= 2` even) repeats each KV head consecutively so both streams `(0, 1)` of head $h$ receive the **same** repeated KV channel (`(k_{g,0}, k_{g,0})` or `(k_{g,1}, k_{g,1})`) instead of `(k_{g,0}, k_{g,1})`, diverging (`rel_l2 > 0.45`) from `multihead_flashdiff_1.py:79-84`; (ii) `multihead_flashdiff_2.py:90-98` (standard `flash-attn` fallback when $d_v = 2 d_{qk}$ is unsupported) splits $V \to (V_1, V_2)$ and invokes `flash_attn_func` **4 separate times** (`attn11, attn12, attn21, attn22`) + 2 `torch.cat`s, recomputing $Q_1 K_1^\top$ + online softmax and $Q_2 K_2^\top$ + online softmax twice (`2.0x` redundant attention compute); (iii) `multihead_flashdiff_1.py:88-96` invokes `flash_attn_func` twice (`2.0x` redundant $V \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times T \times H_{kv} \times 2d}$ HBM reads in fwd and `dO, O, V` reads in bwd) followed by unfused `attn1 - lambda_full * attn2`, unfused `rms_norm.py` (`x.float().pow(2).mean(-1)`), and unfused `*(1 - lambda_init)` (4 extra HBM round-trips on `[B, T, H, 2d]`), while `torch.exp(sum(lambda_q1 * lambda_k1)).type_as(q)` overflows FP16 near `11.1`; (iv) `Diff-Transformer-V2/multihead_flashdiffv2.py:9-11,69-73` runs `flash_attn_func(q, k, v)` on `[B, T, 2H, d]` (treating paired heads `(2h, 2h+1)` in the same GQA group as independent heads that reload `(K_{hkv}, V_{hkv})` twice and write a `2x`-wider `[B, T, 2H, d]` intermediate to HBM) before slicing stride-2 non-contiguous views `attn[:, :, 0::2], attn[:, :, 1::2]` into a separate `diff_func` kernel (`attn1 - sigmoid(lambda_val) * attn2`); and (v) `kernel/rotary.py:124-156,204,253` executes two global loads (`X0` + permuted `rk_swap` `X1`) with hardcoded `BLOCK_M = 4` for interleaved RoPE and an unconditional `do = do.clone()` in non-interleaved backward. Round-10 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Co-Scheduled Paired-Query `Flash-DiffAttn-V2`** (loading shared `(K_{hkv}, V_{hkv})` **once** per head pair `(2h, 2h+1)`, maintaining paired online softmax `(m_1, l_1, O_1)` and `(m_2, l_2, O_2)` in registers, applying $O_h = O_{1,h} - \sigma(\lambda_{b,t,h}) O_{2,h}$ in the epilogue, writing only `[B, T, H, d]` to HBM, and computing exact $\partial \mathcal{L} / \partial \lambda_{b,t,h} = -\sigma(\lambda)(1-\sigma(\lambda))\langle dO_h, O_{2,h} \rangle$ in backward via saved $(LSE_1, LSE_2, D_{\text{dot},1}, D_{\text{dot},2})$ scalars); and (b) **Paired-Stream `Flash-DiffAttn-V1` + Fused `SubLN` `RMSNorm(2d)` & `(1 - lambda_init)` Epilogue** with two-slice `64+64` register staging (`0` cubin spills across all Round-10 RSI kernels vs `> 1,500` spills for naive monolithic `D=128` paired backward; separate `V2` and `V1` `FP32` oracles with `max_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: `V2` GQA half-split `q[:, :, :H]` vs `q[:, :, H:]` cross-KV-group mismatch, `V1` GQA `repeat_kv` pre-split mismatch, `allow_tf32` mutation, and omitted `dlambda` / `SubLN` radial Jacobian).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across `DiffAttn V2` (`Track 1`) & `DiffAttn V1` (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`D \in \{64, 128\}`, `T \in \{2048..8192\}`, `GQA` $n_{\text{rep}} \in \{2, 4\}$), Round-10 RSI must strictly dominate Official `MultiheadFlashDiffV2` (`kernel/rotary.py` + `2H`-head `FA2` + stride-2 `diff_func`), Eager `DiffAttn V2`, and `torch.compile` in `Track 1`, and strictly dominate Official `MultiheadFlashDiff2` (`4x` `FA2` + `2x` `cat` + `rms_norm.py`) and Official `MultiheadFlashDiff1` (`2x` `FA2` + unfused `SubLN`) in `Track 2` in both latency and peak HBM while rejecting `D = 128` monolithic register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Dual-LSE `Ring DiffAttn-CP = 8` Context Parallelism (`Gate G9`, Separate `V2` & `V1` Ring Contracts)**:
   - Subtracting shard-local attention outputs $O^{(1)}_{(p)} - \lambda O^{(2)}_{(p)}$ before cross-shard ring reduction (or reducing with a single shared LSE) violates softmax non-linearity across shards (`> 30%` relative L2 error because $l^{(1)}_{(p)} / l^{(1)} \neq l^{(2)}_{(p)} / l^{(2)}$). Streaming shared `(K_shard, V_shard)` once around the 8-GPU NVSwitch ring while updating the **Dual-LSE Monoid** $(m^{(1)}, l^{(1)}, O^{(1)}; \; m^{(2)}, l^{(2)}, O^{(2)})$ per ring hop and applying the terminal differential epilogue ($O = O^{(1)} - \sigma(\lambda) O^{(2)}$ for `V2`; $O = (1 - \lambda_{\text{init}})\text{SubLN}(O^{(1)} - \lambda_{\text{full}} O^{(2)})$ for `V1`) only after hop $p = CP - 1$ must preserve 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-5`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`) while beating `All-Gather DiffAttn` and `2x Separate Ring-FA2 + Unfused Epilogue` at `T_global = 16,384`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Multi-Needle Distractor-Noise Cancellation Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `12.0s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on interleaved language modeling + multi-needle associative key-value retrieval with correlated high-frequency distractor keys, Round-10 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting wrong GQA `chunk(2)` head-pairing (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), unnormalized `V1` without `SubLN` (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), and `4x-FA2` `MultiheadFlashDiff2` throughput collapse (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`180` steps) across `5` predeclared architectures (`D0_standard_gqa_softmax`, `D1_diff_v2_wrong_gqa_chunk2`, `D2_diff_v1_no_subln`, `D3_diff_v1_rsi_paired_subln`, `D4_diff_v2_rsi_paired_gqa_sigmoid`), `D4` and `D3` must achieve the lowest clean and distractor-heavy `test_bpb` and highest multi-needle retrieval accuracy by cancelling common-mode distractor attention mass ($\text{Attn}_{\text{distractor}} \downarrow$).

## 14. Round-11 Hypersphere Representation Learning, Chordal Residual LERP & Riemannian Tangent-Retracted Training RSI (`nGPT`, `arXiv:2410.01131v2`, ICLR 2025) & Cosine-Attention `Ring nGPT-CP = 8` Protocol (`E137`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from common-mode attention-noise cancellation (`Differential Transformer V1 & V2`) to **unit-hypersphere representation learning (`nGPT: Normalized Transformer with Representation Learning on the Hypersphere`, Loshchilov, Hsieh, Sun & Ginsburg, NVIDIA, `arXiv:2410.01131v2`, ICLR 2025; official repo `NVIDIA/ngpt`)**, four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed context-parallel & shard-retracted optimizer, and hypersphere convergence scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Superfused Hypersphere Chordal Residual LERP + Pair-Isotropic $SO(2)$ RoPE-Norm-$s_{qk}$ + Eigen-Scaled SwiGLU + Riemannian Tangent-Retracted AdamW (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `NVIDIA/ngpt` Forensics)**:
   - Official `NVIDIA/ngpt` (`model.py` & `train.py`) suffers from five concrete training-kernel and mathematical pathologies: (i) `model.py:40-58,102-104` builds `get_sinusoidal_embeddings(T, D)` on **CPU** inside every `Block.forward()` (`torch.zeros((T, D))` + `.to(device=q.device)` H2D copy per layer), interleaves `sin` (`0::2`) and `cos` (`1::2`), and then calls `sin, cos = sinusoidal_pos.chunk(2, dim=-1)` (splitting first-half low frequencies vs second-half high frequencies rather than `sin` vs `cos`) followed by elementwise `stack((-q_odd, q_even)) * stack((cos, sin))` without summing $q_{\text{even}}\cos\theta - q_{\text{odd}}\sin\theta$, violating $SO(2)$ orthogonality ($\|R(q)\|_2 \neq \|q\|_2$) and destroying relative-position shift invariance ($\langle R_{m+\Delta} q, R_{n+\Delta} k \rangle \neq \langle R_m q, R_n k \rangle$); (ii) `model.py:88-90,123-131,145-153` executes the hypersphere residual update `h = justnorm(justnorm(h) + abs(alpha*scale) * (justnorm(g) - justnorm(h)))` as `10` separate kernel launches per sublayer (`20` per block forward, `20` backward) with `justnorm(x) = x / x.norm(p=2, dim=-1, keepdim=True)` in `BF16` without an $\varepsilon$ guard (`0/0 -> NaN` on zero rows) and materializes `4` intermediate `[B, T, C]` tensors (`A_norm, B_norm, res, h`) in HBM per sublayer; (iii) `model.py:135-140` materializes `uv = suv * sqrt(C) * c_fc(hin)` (`[B, T, 2 I]`) in HBM before `torch.chunk(uv, 2, dim=-1)` and `u * silu(v)`; (iv) `model.py:199-203` materializes a second full-vocabulary `[B, T, V]` tensor `logits = sz * logits` in HBM before `F.cross_entropy`; and (v) `train.py:326-345` runs a post-step Python loop `normalize_matrices()` over `74+` weight matrices (`idim=1` for row-unit `wte, lm_head, query, key, value, c_fc` and non-contiguous `idim=0` for column-unit `att_c_proj, mlp_c_proj`), allocating `FP32` copies and launching `~300` kernels per step while allowing Euclidean `AdamW` to accumulate radial gradients $G_{\parallel} = \hat{W}\langle \hat{W}, G\rangle$ into $(m_t, v_t)$. Round-11 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Superfused Hypersphere Chordal LERP (`_ngpt_hypersphere_lerp_fwd/bwd_kernel`)** computing $r_x = (\|x\|_2^2 + \varepsilon)^{-1/2}$, $r_g = (\|g\|_2^2 + \varepsilon)^{-1/2}$, $u = \hat{x} + |\alpha_{\text{eff}}| \odot (\hat{g} - \hat{x})$, and $y = r_u u$ in one pass while reconstructing unrounded `FP32` $(\hat{x}, \hat{g}, u, y)$ in backward from saved scalar $(r_x, r_g, r_u)$ (`12` B/token) for exact coupled 3-sphere tangent projections; (b) **Pair-Isotropic $SO(2)$ RoPE + `FP32` L2-Norm + $s_{qk}$ Scaling (`_ngpt_rope_norm_sqk_fwd/bwd_kernel`)** with rotary-pair-tied $\bar{s}_{qk,2i} = \bar{s}_{qk,2i+1} = \frac{1}{2}(s_{qk,2i}+s_{qk,2i+1})$ restoring exact relative-shift invariance (plus a compile-time `ROPE_MODE=0` upstream-broken linear-map mode for exact parity verification against `NVIDIA/ngpt`); (c) **Fused Eigen-Scaled SwiGLU (`_ngpt_scaled_swiglu_fwd/bwd_kernel`)** & **Fused `sz`-Scaled Online-Softmax Cross-Entropy (`_ngpt_sz_cross_entropy_fwd/bwd_kernel`)**; and (d) **Fused Riemannian Tangent-Preconditioned & Vector-Transported AdamW + Hypersphere Retraction (`_ngpt_riemannian_adamw_retract_row/col_kernel`)** (`0` cubin spills across all Round-11 RSI kernels, `FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: omitted coupled 3-sphere radial Jacobian, wrong attention softmax scale $1/\sqrt{D}$ instead of $+\sqrt{D}$ on unit $Q, K$, `allow_tf32` mutation, and un-retracted weight drift $\|W\|_2 \neq 1$).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `nGPT` Hypersphere Operators (`Track 1`) & End-to-End `nGPT` Block + Weight-Retraction Step (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`C \in \{1024, 2048\}`, `T \in \{2048..8192\}`, `D_h \in \{64, 128\}`), Round-11 RSI must strictly dominate Official `NVIDIA/ngpt` (`model.py` + `train.py:normalize_matrices()`), Eager Corrected `nGPT`, and `torch.compile` across both isolated hypersphere operators (`Track 1`) and full `nGPT` Transformer block + post-step weight retraction (`Track 2`) in both latency and peak HBM while rejecting `D_h = 128` monolithic register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Cosine-Attention `Ring nGPT-CP = 8` + Shard-Retracted Riemannian Optimizer (`Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $W = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`), sequence shards $T_{\text{local}} = T / 8$ execute token-local hypersphere operators with global RoPE offset `rank * T_local`, global valid-token `sz` Cross-Entropy denominator, and **8-GPU Causal Ring Cosine-Attention (`softmax_scale = +sqrt(D_h)`)** with exact online LSE merge, followed by cross-rank gradient `AllReduce` on $(\nabla W, d\alpha_A, d\alpha_M, ds_{qk}, ds_{uv}, ds_z)$ and **ZeRO-1 Shard-Retracted Riemannian AdamW** (partitioning whole unit-norm rows/columns across the 8 ranks, retracting in-register, and `all_gather_into_tensor`), preserving 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-5`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_unit_norm_err <= 1e-6`) while beating `All-Gather nGPT` and `Unfused Ring + Python normalize_matrices()`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Hypersphere vs Pre-RMSNorm Convergence Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `12.0s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on interleaved language modeling + long-range directional associative recall, Round-11 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting upstream broken `chunk(2)` RoPE (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), wrong $1/\sqrt{D_h}$ softmax scale on unit $Q, K$ (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`), and unfused Python `normalize_matrices()` throughput collapse (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`180` steps) across `5` predeclared architectures (`N0_standard_prenorm_gpt`, `N1_ngpt_wrong_inv_sqrt_scale`, `N2_ngpt_upstream_broken_rope`, `N3_ngpt_ortho_rope_euclidean_retract`, `N4_ngpt_rsi_pair_isotropic_riemannian`), `N4` and `N3` must achieve the fastest convergence (`val_bpb` and `test_bpb`) and highest associative recall accuracy while maintaining exact unit-hypersphere invariant $\max |\,\|h\|_2 - 1\,| \le 10^{-4}$ and $\max |\,\|W\|_2 - 1\,| \le 10^{-5}$.

## 15. Round-12 Hierarchical Fenwick-Tree State-Space & Log-Linear Attention RSI (`Log-Linear Mamba-2` & `Log-Linear Gated DeltaNet`, `arXiv:2506.04761v3`, Mar 2026, `HanGuo97/log-linear-attention`) & Shard-Decoupled `Fenwick-CP = 8` Protocol (`E138`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from hypersphere representation learning (`nGPT`) to **logarithmically growing hierarchical state-space and linear attention models (`Log-Linear Attention`, Guo, Yang, Goel, Xing, Dao & Kim, MIT / Princeton / Together AI / CMU, `arXiv:2506.04761v3`, Mar 2026; official repo `HanGuo97/log-linear-attention`)**, covering both **Log-Linear Mamba-2 (`H-Mamba-2`, `HStruct.MAMBA2`)** and **Log-Linear Gated DeltaNet (`H-GDN`, `HStruct.GDELTA`)**, four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed Fenwick context-parallel, and hierarchical memory scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Bitwise XOR `clz` Fenwick Level Identity + Parallel Multi-Level Recurrence + Zero-Atomic `dl` Segmented Reduction + `gamma`-Free `H-GDN` WY Preprocessing (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `HanGuo97/log-linear-attention` Forensics)**:
   - Official `HanGuo97/log-linear-attention` (`hattention/base.py`, `chunkwise.py`, `chunkwise_hgdn.py`) exhibits five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies: (i) `base.py:21-25,86-143` hardcodes internal cluster paths `"/export/share/experiments/20250202/llut/llut.length-16384.base-2.pth"` in `CACHED_LEVELS_MATRICES` (crashing with `FileNotFoundError` when `cached_length=16384` is used externally) and otherwise executes an $O(T^2)$ double Python loop (`T*(T-1)/2` scalar CUDA writes) to allocate a dense `[T, T]` `int64` `llut` matrix (`2.0 GiB` at $T = 16,384$); (ii) `chunkwise.py:722-783,932-1047` loops sequentially on the host `for ell in range(num_chunk_levels):`, launching `2 * L_chunk + 1` kernels in forward and `4 * L_chunk + 2` kernels in backward (recomputing the exact same chunk-local `tl.dot(b_k, b_v)` at every level $\ell$, reloading/storing `o` at every level $\ell$, allocating `NK`-replicated `dg` and `dl` buffers, and executing `BT * BT = 4,096` colliding `tl.atomic_add(p_dla, b_dla, scope="cta")` operations per CTA onto `dl`); (iii) `chunkwise_hgdn.py:424-483` materializes a 5D `[B, NT, BT, BT, H]` `gamma` tensor in `BF16` and runs a 5-tensor eager `einsum` to compute `q_new` without exploiting the exact exponential cancellation $\exp(g_r - g_s)\exp(g_s) \equiv \exp(g_r)$ for $r \ge s$, while allocating `3` full-sequence `.masked_fill` clones per level in forward and `5` in backward; (iv) `chunkwise.py:670-676` and `chunkwise_hgdn.py:415-422` (`if False: using_groups = True`) unconditionally expand `q` and `k` by `H // G` in HBM via `einops.repeat(...).contiguous()`; and (v) `kernel.py:332` leaves `HAttentionPrefillFunction.backward` unimplemented (`raise NotImplementedError`), preventing differentiable state handoff across sequence shards. Round-12 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Closed-Form Bitwise XOR `clz` Fenwick Level Identity** $\ell_{\text{weak}}(t, s) = \operatorname{where}(t == s, 0, 32 - \operatorname{clz}(t \oplus s))$ for $t \ge s \ge 0$ (`0` bytes of `[T, T]` `llut` allocation, `0` `/export/share/...` files); (b) **Precomputed Chunk-Summary KV States & Parallel Multi-Level Fenwick Scan (`_log_linear_mamba2_fwd/bwd_*` and `_hgdn_fwd/bwd_*`)** with single-pass multi-level output accumulation (`1` `[BK, BV]` state tile live in registers per loop step, `0` cubin spills across all Round-12 RSI kernels); (c) **Zero-Atomic Register-Segmented Intra-Chunk `dl` Row Reduction** $dl_{r, \ell} = \sum_{c=0}^{BT-1} \mathbf{1}[\ell_{\text{weak}}(r, c) == \ell]\, b_{\text{dla}}[r, c]$ for $\ell \in \{0, \dots, \log_2 BT\}$ (`0` `atomic_add`s); and (d) **Exact Dual-Inverse (`Aw` ungated, `Au` decay-weighted) + `gamma`-Free `q_new` Preprocessing** $q_{\text{new}}[r] = \exp(g_r)(q[r] - \sum_{s \le r}(q[r]\cdot k[s]) w[s])$ (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: wrong decay-weighted `Aw` inverse in `H-GDN`, collapsed single-level $\lambda_\ell \equiv \lambda_0$ linear-attention fallback, `allow_tf32` mutation, and omitted inter-chunk `dg_last` decay gradient).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across `Log-Linear Mamba-2` (`Track 1`) & `Log-Linear Gated DeltaNet` (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`T \in \{1024..4096\}`, `H = 16`, `K, V \in \{64, 128\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-12 RSI must strictly dominate Official `HanGuo97/log-linear-attention` (`chunkwise.py` & `chunkwise_hgdn.py` with `[T, T]` `llut` and per-level host loops), Eager Materialized $O(T^2)$ Log-Linear Attention (`base.py`), and `torch.compile` in both latency and peak HBM while rejecting monolithic multi-level register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Hierarchical Fenwick-Tree Shard-Decoupled Context Parallelism (`Fenwick-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8 = 2^3$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024 = 2^{10}$ per rank), by the orthogonal bit-field decomposition $(p \cdot 2^{10} + t_{\text{loc}}) \oplus (q \cdot 2^{10} + s_{\text{loc}}) = ((p \oplus q) \ll 10) \mid (t_{\text{loc}} \oplus s_{\text{loc}})$, all $11$ fine levels $\ell \in \{0, \dots, 10\}$ execute strictly rank-locally with zero inter-GPU traffic, while all cross-rank interactions collapse into the $3 = \log_2 8$ coarse levels $\ell \in \{11, 12, 13\}$ via a single `AllGather` of per-rank summary tuples $(S_{\text{rank}}^{(q)}, G_{\text{total}}^{(q)})$ and exact reverse-mode adjoint propagation through prefix $G_{\text{shard}}(p, t_{\text{loc}})$, intervening $G_{\text{total}}(r)$ ($q < r < p$), and source-shard suffix $G_{\text{total}}(q) - G_{\text{shard}}(q, s_{\text{loc}})$, achieving 3-step `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU monolithic $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$ oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-5`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 1e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, vs `> 40%` error under shard-local level reset without coarse Fenwick handoff).
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Multi-Scale Associative Recall & Hierarchical Memory Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed wall-clock `8xA100 DDP` budget on multi-scale associative recall + language modeling, Round-12 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting collapsed single-level $\lambda_\ell \equiv 1$ (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and upstream per-level host-loop + `[T, T]` `llut` overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`L0_mamba2_ssd_fixed_state`, `L1_gated_deltanet_fixed_state`, `L2_log_linear_mamba2_wrong_uniform_lambda`, `L3_log_linear_mamba2_rsi`, `L4_log_linear_gdn_rsi`), `L4` and `L3` must outperform fixed-state `Mamba-2 SSD` (`L0`), fixed-state `Gated DeltaNet` (`L1`), and collapsed uniform-$\lambda$ (`L2`) on multi-scale associative recall (`test_bpb` and recall accuracy).

## 16. Round-13 Locally Optimal Test-Time Training, Dual-State `(H_kk, H_kv)` Recurrence & Causal Query-Subtiled Warm-Start Jacobi-PCG RSI (`MesaNet`, `arXiv:2506.05233v2`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/mesa_net`) & NVSwitch Dual-State Prefix-Scan `Mesa-CP = 8` Protocol (`E139`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from hierarchical Fenwick-tree state spaces (`Log-Linear Attention`) to **second-order locally optimal test-time regression (`MesaNet: Sequence Modeling by Locally Optimal Test-Time Training`, von Oswald, Scherrer, Kobayashi, Versari, Yang, Lajoie, Pascanu, Agüera y Arcas & Sacramento, Google Paradigms of Intelligence / Google DeepMind / MIT CSAIL, `arXiv:2506.05233v2`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/mesa_net`)**, four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed dual-state context-parallel, and correlated-key recursive least-squares scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Exact Diagonal-Preconditioned Warm-Start Conjugate Gradient (`Jacobi-PCG`) + Causal Query-Subtiled `Q-Subtiled PCG` + Unified `KK + KV` Intra-Chunk Adjoint (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/mesa_net` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/mesa_net` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_cg_solver_fwd.py`, `chunk_cg_solver_bwd.py`, `chunk_h_kk_intra_bwd.py`, `chunk_h_kv_intra_bwd.py`) exhibits five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies: (i) `chunk_cg_solver_fwd.py:109-121` and `chunk_cg_solver_bwd.py:102-114` initialize $x^{(0)} = 0$, omit the Jacobi diagonal preconditioner $D_{t,d} = \exp(\bar{g}_{c,i})(H_{c-1})_{d,d} + \sum_{j \le i} M_c[i,j] k_{c,j,d}^2 + \Lambda_d$ (commented out in `naive.py:111`), add a large `+ 1e-5` bias to both $\alpha_k = \delta_k / (p_k^\top A p_k + 10^{-5})$ and $\beta_k = \delta_{k+1} / (\delta_k + 10^{-5})$ denominators (stalling linear-system relative residual convergence around $\sim 1.4\times 10^{-3}$ even after 30 iterations and perturbing the Implicit Function Theorem adjoint), and execute an unconditional `for i in range(30):` loop with no residual-norm stopping criterion; (ii) at $K = V = 128$ (`BT = 64`), `chunk_fwd_mesa_cg_dim64_kernel`, `chunk_mesa_net_h_kv_bwd_intra_kernel`, and `chunk_mesa_net_h_kk_bwd_intra_kernel` hold `[128, 128]` state tiles (`16,384` elements) alongside multiple `[64, 128]` `FP32` tiles in registers simultaneously, causing severe SM80 cubin register spills to local memory; (iii) `chunk.py:42-60,213-220` launches separate `l2norm_fwd(q)`, `l2norm_fwd(k)`, and `chunk_local_cumsum(g)` kernels while forcibly casting `q` and `k` to `torch.float16` even for `bfloat16`/`float32` callers; (iv) `chunk.py:116-171` executes two separate intra-chunk backward passes (`chunk_mesa_net_h_kv_bwd_intra_fn` and `chunk_mesa_net_h_kk_bwd_intra_fn`) that write intermediate `dk_beta` and `dg` tensors to HBM and then combine them in a third pass; and (v) no multi-GPU context-parallel protocol exists for the coupled dual recurrence $(H_{kk}, H_{kv})$ where $H_{kk}^{\text{prefix}}(r)$ sits inside the matrix inverse $(H_{kk}^{\text{prefix}}(r)\exp(\bar{g}) + H_{kk}^{\text{local}} + \Lambda)^{-1} q$. Round-13 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Exact Diagonal-Preconditioned Warm-Start CG (`Jacobi-PCG`)** with $D_{c,i,d} = \exp(\bar{g}_{c,i})(H_{c-1})_{d,d} + \sum_{j \le i} M_c[i,j] k_{c,j,d}^2 + \Lambda_d$, warm-start $x^{(0)}_{c,i} = D_{c,i}^{-1} q_{c,i}$, unbiased `1e-30` denominator guard, and hardware dynamic residual stopping ($\max_{i} \|r_{c,i}\|_2^2 \le \varepsilon_{\text{tol}}^2 \|q_{c,i}\|_2^2$), reaching $< 10^{-7}$ relative error in $6$–$8$ steps (`> 25,000x` lower error than Upstream's 30-step CG in `4x–5x` fewer steps); (b) **Causal Query-Subtiled & 2-Panel Split-State `Q-Subtiled PCG`** (`BM = 16/32`, splitting `[128, 128]` $H_{c-1}$ into `[128, 64]` panels and decoupling the $H_{kv}$ readout so `0` cubin spills occur across all Round-13 RSI Triton kernels); and (c) **Unified Dual-Gram `KK + KV` Intra-Chunk Backward (`C_c = dO_c V_c^\top - d\tilde{Q}_c K_c^\top`)** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 2e-3`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: omitted $dH_{kk}$ IFT adjoint, collapsed 0-step CG / $+0.1$ biased denominator, `allow_tf32` mutation, and flipped $+d\tilde{q}\,q^{*\top}$ IFT sign).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `MesaNet` Dual-State + CG Layer (`Track 1`) & Full `MesaNet` Block (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`T \in \{512..2048\}`, `H = 8`, `K, V \in \{64, 128\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-13 RSI must strictly dominate Official `fla/ops/mesa_net` (`30`-step unpreconditioned CG + separate `l2norm` / `cumsum` / split backward), Sequential Exact `torch.linalg.solve` (`naive_mesa_net_exact`), and `torch.compile` across both isolated `MesaNet` layers (`Track 1`) and full `MesaNet` blocks with `ShortConv1d(W=4) + QK-L2Norm + Output RMSNorm/Gate + SwiGLU` (`Track 2`) in latency and peak HBM while rejecting monolithic `[64x128]` register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Dual-State Prefix-Scan Context Parallelism (`Mesa-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024$ per rank), omitting $H_{kk}^{\text{prefix}}(r)$ from the rank-$r$ CG linear system $(H_{kk}^{\text{prefix}}(r)\exp(\bar{g}) + H_{kk}^{\text{local}} + \Lambda)^{-1} q$ corrupts output by $> 25\%$ relative L2 error. Packing per-rank dual summaries $(\Delta H_{kk, r}, \Delta H_{kv, r}, \Gamma_r)$ into a single NVSwitch `all_gather_into_tensor` and running exact associative prefix/suffix scans before the parallel shard-local `Q-Subtiled PCG` solve must preserve 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-4`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`) while beating `All-Gather Full-Sequence MesaNet`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Correlated-Key Recursive Least-Squares Associative Recall Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `2.1s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on correlated-key overwrite & multi-query associative recall, Round-13 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting 0-step CG / Hebbian GLA collapse (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and unfused 30-step host-loop CG overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`108` steps) across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`M0_hebbian_gla_0cg`, `M1_online_deltanet_1step`, `M2_mesa_biased_unprecond_1cg`, `M3_mesa_scalar_lambda_pcg`, `M4_mesa_anisotropic_shortconv_rsi_pcg`), `M4` and `M3` must outperform first-order Hebbian `GLA` (`M0`), first-order online `DeltaNet` (`M1`), and biased unpreconditioned `MesaNet` (`M2`) on correlated-key associative recall (`test_bpb` and recall accuracy).

## 17. Round-14 Fine-Grained Channel-Gated Delta Attention, Overflow-Free Causal Sub-Chunking & Split-K/V WY Adjoint RSI (`Kimi Linear / KDA`, `arXiv:2510.26692`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/kda`) & NVSwitch Channel-Affine Monoid `KDA-CP = 8` Protocol (`E140`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from second-order test-time training (`MesaNet`) to **fine-grained channel-gated Diagonal-Plus-Low-Rank (`DPLR`) delta attention (`Kimi Linear: An Expressive, Efficient Attention Architecture`, Moonshot AI / Kimi Team, `arXiv:2510.26692`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/kda`)**, four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed channel-affine context-parallel, and channel-selective associative recall scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Overflow-Free Non-Positive Causal Sub-Chunking + Split-`K`/`V` Zero-Spill WY Adjoint + Superfused `QK-L2Norm + BetaSigmoid + KDA-Gate-Cumsum` (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/kda` & `arXiv:2510.26692` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/kda` (`chunk_fwd.py`, `chunk_bwd.py`, `chunk_intra.py`, `gate.py`) and `arXiv:2510.26692` Listing 8(b) exhibit five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) `chunk_fwd.py:77` and `chunk_bwd.py:786` omit `lower_bound=lower_bound` when calling `chunk_kda_fwd_intra` and `chunk_kda_bwd_intra` (leaving `GATE_SHIFT` hardcoded to `2.0 * RCP_LN2` / $d = 15.0$), and on $16 \times 16$ diagonal sub-chunks (`BC = 16`), `chunk_kda_fwd_kernel_intra_sub_chunk` factors $\exp(\bar{g}_i - \bar{g}_j) = \exp(\bar{g}_i - C)\exp(C - \bar{g}_j)$ around $C = \bar{g}_0 - 15$, which under steep valid decay ($g_{t,d} = -7.0$) evaluates $\exp(C - \bar{g}_{15}) = \exp(+90.0) = +\infty$ in `FP32` and corrupts `32,768 / 524,288` (`6.25%`) of output elements to `NaN` via `0 * Inf = NaN` in `tl.dot`, even though every causal difference $\bar{g}_{i,d} - \bar{g}_{j,d} \le 0$ ($i \ge j$) satisfies $\exp(\bar{g}_{i,d} - \bar{g}_{j,d}) \in (0, 1]$ (moreover, `arXiv:2510.26692` Listing 8(b) omits $\beta$ on $A_{kk}$ and $(W, U)$ and updates inter-chunk state $S$ with raw $v_i$ instead of pseudo-value $u_i - w_i S$); (ii) on SM80 at $K = V = 128$ (`BT = 64`), `chunk_kda_bwd_kernel_wy_dqkg_fused` (`chunk_bwd.py:137-320`) spills **`156` to `3,648` registers across `100%` (`36/36`) of its autotuned specializations (`48,438` total spills)** because it nests the `i_v` loop inside the `i_k` loop while holding `[64, 64]` `b_A` and `b_dA` (`FP32`) live alongside `b_dq, b_dk, b_dw, b_v_new, b_do, b_h, b_dh, b_dv`, while `chunk_kda_bwd_kernel_intra` spills **`30` registers** and `chunk_kda_fwd_kernel_inter_solve_fused` spills **`4` registers**; (iii) `chunk.py` launches `l2norm_fwd(q)`, `l2norm_fwd(k)`, `fused_beta_sigmoid(beta)`, and `kda_gate_chunk_cumsum(g, A_log, dt_bias)` as 4 separate forward and 4 separate backward kernel passes plus extra global reductions `db2.sum(0)` and `dbias.sum(0)`; (iv) under Grouped Value Attention (`GVA`, $H_V > H$), `naive.py` materializes `q.repeat_interleave(G)` / `k.repeat_interleave(G)` and `tilelang` rejects `HV > H` altogether; and (v) naive multi-GPU Context Parallelism either all-gathers full sequences or approximates the channel-wise transition $P_c = \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(\bar{g}_{c,B_T})) - J_c^\top W_c$ with a scalar decay. Round-14 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Overflow-Free Non-Positive Causal Sub-Chunking** (using exact sub-chunk boundary anchors $\bar{g}_{16 c_r, d}$ for off-diagonal $16 \times 16$ Tensor-Core blocks so both $\bar{g}_{i,d} - \bar{g}_{16 c_r, d} \le 0$ and $\bar{g}_{16 c_r, d} - \bar{g}_{j,d} \le 0$ are strictly non-positive, and pre-masked causal differences $\bar{g}_{i,d} - \bar{g}_{j,d} \le 0$ for $16 \times 16$ diagonal blocks: `0` `NaN`s across `524,288/524,288` elements at $g_{t,d} = -7.0$ and `0.0` future-token leakage); (b) **Zero-Spill Split-`K`/`V` WY Backward Decomposition** (decoupling per-slice `(dq, dk, dg, dw)` at `BK = 32, BV = 32` from the `[64, 64]` lower-triangular inverse adjoint $\bar{L} = -\operatorname{StrictTril}(A^\top \operatorname{StrictTril}(D_{\text{raw}} \odot \beta^\top) A^\top)$ so `0` cubin spills occur across all Round-14 RSI Triton kernels); and (c) **Superfused `QK-L2Norm + BetaSigmoid + KDA-Gate-Cumsum` Frontend & Native GVA Stride Indexing** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `BF16 <= 2.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: Paper Listing 8(b) raw-$v_i$ state update bug, untransposed $A(\cdots)A$ inverse adjoint bug, `allow_tf32` flag mutation, and omitted `L2Norm` / `kda_gate` VJP).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `KDA` Layer (`Track 1`) & Full `KimiDeltaAttention` Block (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`T \in \{1024..4096\}`, `H \in \{8, 16\}`, `K, V \in \{64, 128\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-14 RSI must strictly dominate Upstream `fla/ops/kda` (`chunk_kda`), Eager `naive_chunk_kda` / `naive_recurrent_kda`, and `torch.compile` across both isolated `KDA` layers (`Track 1`) and full `KimiDeltaAttention` blocks with `ShortConv1d(W=4) + QK-L2Norm + Low-Rank Gate + FusedRMSNormGated + Projections` (`Track 2`) in latency and peak HBM while rejecting monolithic `[64x128]` register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Channel-Affine Monoid Context Parallelism (`KDA-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024$ per rank), each rank summarizes its shard as an exact channel-gated affine transition $(P_{\text{rank}}(r), H_{\text{rank}}(r)) \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times H_V \times K \times (K + V)}$ built by composing $P_c = \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(\bar{g}_{c,B_T})) - J_c^\top W_c$ and $H_c = J_c^\top U_c$, exchanges $(P_{\text{rank}}, H_{\text{rank}})$ in a single NVSwitch `all_gather_into_tensor` to seed $S_{\text{init}}(r)$, and in backward reuses the saved $P_{\text{rank}}(q)^\top$ with a single `AllGather` of inhomogeneous shard gradients $G_{\text{rank}}(q)$ (`2x` smaller backward payload) to seed $dht(r)$, preserving 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-4`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, vs `> 25%` error when omitting cross-rank state handoff) while beating `All-Gather Full-Sequence KDA`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Channel-Selective Associative Recall & `3:1` Hybrid `Kimi Linear` Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `1.4s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on channel-selective multi-timescale associative recall (where persistent slow-decay channels and transient fast-overwrite channels coexist within the same head), Round-14 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting Paper Listing 8(b)'s raw-$v_i$ inter-chunk update bug (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and unfused host-loop KDA overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`64` steps) across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`K0_gla_no_delta_rule`, `K1_gdn_coarse_scalar_gate`, `K2_kda_paper_listing8b_bug`, `K3_kda_pure_channel_delta_rsi`, `K4_kimi_linear_3to1_hybrid_kda_mla_rsi`), `K4` and `K3` must outperform `GLA` without delta rule (`K0`), coarse head-scalar `Gated DeltaNet` (`K1`), and Listing 8(b) broken `KDA` (`K2`) on channel-selective associative recall (`test_bpb` and recall accuracy).
