## 18. Round-15 Generalized Diagonal-Plus-Low-Rank (`DPLR`) Delta Rule & `RWKV-7 "Goose"` Dynamic State Evolution RSI (`arXiv:2503.14456` / `arXiv:2506.02475`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/generalized_delta_rule/dplr` & `fla/ops/rwkv7`) & NVSwitch Dual-Source Channel-Affine Monoid `DPLR-CP = 8` Protocol (`E141`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from symmetric rank-1 channel-gated delta attention (`Kimi Linear / KDA`, where $(I - \beta_t k_t k_t^\top)\operatorname{Diag}(\exp(g_t))$ ties the erase direction to the write key $k_t$ and multiplies the rank-1 update by step-$t$ decay) to **asymmetric Generalized Diagonal-Plus-Low-Rank (`DPLR`) state evolution (`RWKV-7 "Goose" with Expressive Dynamic State Evolution`, Peng, Zhang, Goldstein et al., `arXiv:2503.14456`, COLM 2025; `Comba: Improving Bilinear RNNs with Closed-loop Control`, Hu et al., `arXiv:2506.02475`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/generalized_delta_rule/dplr` & `fla/ops/rwkv7`)**, where the column-state transition is
$$S_t = \left(\operatorname{Diag}(\exp(w_t)) + b_t a_t^\top\right) S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top, \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot S_t^\top q_t$$
(with RWKV-7 parameterization $q_t = r_t$, $a_t = -\hat{\kappa}_t$, $b_t = \hat{\kappa}_t \odot \alpha_t$, $\tilde{k}_t = k_t \odot (1 + (\alpha_t - 1)\odot k_a)$, and output correction $y_t = (\operatorname{GroupNorm}(o_t) + \langle r_t, \tilde{k}_t \odot r_k \rangle v_t) \odot g_t$), four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed dual-source channel-affine context-parallel, and asymmetric state-evolution scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Overflow-Free Non-Positive Dual-Decay (`gi` Inclusive / `ge` Exclusive) Sub-Chunking + Zero-Spill Split-`K`/`V` DPLR WY Adjoint + Superfused `RWKV-7` Frontend & Epilogue (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/generalized_delta_rule/dplr` & `fla/ops/rwkv7` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/generalized_delta_rule/dplr` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_A_fwd.py`, `chunk_A_bwd.py`, `wy_fast_fwd.py`, `wy_fast_bwd.py`) and `fla/ops/rwkv7` (`gate_output_correction.py`, `fused_k_update.py`, `fused_addcmul.py`) exhibit five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) `chunk_A_fwd.py:216-252` (`chunk_dplr_fwd_A_kernel_intra_tensorcore`, `safe_gate=True`) shifts cumulative log2-decays by the mid-chunk row `b_offset = gi[i_t*BT + 32, :]`, computing `inv_exp_gi = exp2(b_offset - b_gi_val)` and factoring `b_kg_g = (b_k * exp2(b_offset - b_gi_val)) * exp2(b_g_last - b_offset)`, which under steep valid decay ($w_{t,d} = -4.1$ bits/token) overflows `exp2(b_offset - b_gi_val) = +Inf` for rows $r > 32$ and multiplies by `exp2(b_g_last - b_offset) = 0.0` to corrupt **`1,620` output elements (`Aqk=128, Aqb=128, Aab=192, Aak=192, kg=499, bg=481`) to `NaN`**, even though $g_{i,B_T-1,d} - g_{i,r,d} \le 0$ and every causal difference $g_{i,r,d} - g_{i,j,d} \le 0$ ($r \ge j$) and $g_{e,r,d} - g_{i,j,d} \le 0$ ($r > j$) are strictly non-positive; meanwhile `wy_fast_fwd.py:72-81,118-174` (`prepare_wy_repr_fwd_kernel_safe`) downcasts `float32` $A_{ab}$ to `tl.float16` (`FP16`) inside `_inv_diag16`, truncating `FP32` accuracy and overflowing `FP16` (`65,504`) when $\|a_t\|\|b_t\| > 1$; (ii) on SM80 at $K = V = 128$ (`BT = 64`), **`100%` (`22/22`) of upstream's autotuned TensorCore specializations across `chunk_dplr_fwd_A_kernel_intra_tensorcore` (`2,840–3,416` spills across `4/4` configs = `12,512` spills), `chunk_dplr_bwd_kernel_intra_tensorcore` (`1,902–2,312` spills across `6/6` `BK=64` configs = `12,642` spills; `2,100–3,114` spills at `BK=128`), and `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` (`72–262` spills across `12/12` configs = `1,998` spills) spill registers to local HBM (`27,152` total spills across `22/22` configs)** because they hold `4` `[64, 128]` `FP32` gated tiles alongside `4–6` `[64, 64]` `FP32` matrices in a single CTA; (iii) `chunk.py:258-315` (`ChunkDPLRDeltaRuleFunction.backward`) by default (`disable_recompute=False`) re-executes 100% of forward (`chunk_rwkv6_fwd_cumsum`, `chunk_dplr_fwd_intra`, `prepare_wy_repr_fwd`, and sequential `chunk_dplr_fwd_h`) inside `backward()` while still allocating all intermediate tensors simultaneously; (iv) `gate_output_correction.py:194` (`dr_k = torch.empty_like(r, dtype=torch.float32)`) and `fused_k_update.py:254` (`dka_tmp = torch.empty_like(k, dtype=torch.float32)`) allocate full `[B, T, H, K]` `FP32` buffers in HBM before calling `.sum(dim=(0, 1))`, while `RWKV7Attention.forward` launches `16` separate non-GEMM frontend/epilogue kernels per layer; and (v) transposed rank-1 orientation $a_t b_t^\top S_{t-1}$ vs $b_t a_t^\top S_{t-1}$ or conflating exclusive $g_{e,t}$ with inclusive $g_{i,t}$ on $a_t$ silently corrupts asymmetric DPLR state evolution. Round-15 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Overflow-Free Non-Positive Dual-Decay (`gi` / `ge`) Sub-Chunking (`BC = 16`)** (evaluating diagonal $16 \times 16$ sub-chunks via pre-masked non-positive differences $g_{i,r,d} - g_{i,c,d} \le 0$ for $r \ge c$ and $g_{e,r,d} - g_{i,c,d} \le 0$ for $r > c$, off-diagonal $16 \times 16$ TensorCore blocks via sub-chunk boundary anchors $g_{\text{anchor}}(r_b) = g_{i, 16 r_b - 1, d}$ so $g_{i,r,d} - g_{\text{anchor}} \le 0$, $g_{e,r,d} - g_{\text{anchor}} \le 0$, and $g_{\text{anchor}} - g_{i,c,d} \le 0$, direct non-positive $k_g = k \odot \exp_2(g_{i,B_T-1} - g_i)$, $b_g = b \odot \exp_2(g_{i,B_T-1} - g_i)$, and `FP32` $(I - A_{ab})^{-1}$ inversion: **`0` `NaN`s across `100%` of outputs** and `0.0` future-token leakage); (b) **Zero-Spill Sub-Chunk / Split-`K`/`V` DPLR Kernels** (subtiling `BT=64` into `BM=16` row panels and `BK=32, BV=32` channel slices so **`0` cubin spills occur across all Round-15 RSI Triton kernels**); and (c) **Superfused RWKV-7 `TokenShift+6xAddcmul`, `KK-L2Norm+KUpdate+DPLR-AB+Cumsum`, and `GroupNorm+BonusCorrection+Gate` Kernels** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `BF16 <= 2.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `4/4` red-team exploits: transposed $a_t b_t^\top$ vs $b_t a_t^\top$ orientation bug, wrong inclusive `gi` instead of exclusive `ge` on $a_t$, `allow_tf32` flag mutation, and flipped negative sign on $dA_{ab} = +\operatorname{StrictTril}(M^\top dM M^\top)$).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `Generalized DPLR / RWKV-7` Layer (`Track 1`) & Full `RWKV-7 Goose` Block (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` (`T \in \{512..2048\}`, `H \in \{4, 8\}`, `K, V \in \{64, 128\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-15 RSI must strictly dominate Upstream `fla/ops/generalized_delta_rule/dplr` (`chunk_rwkv7` / `chunk_dplr_delta_rule` spill & recompute pattern), Eager Chunk `DPLR`, Sequential Recurrent `DPLR`, and `torch.compile` across both isolated `DPLR / RWKV-7` layers (`Track 1`) and full `RWKV7Attention` blocks (`Track 2`) in latency and peak HBM while rejecting upstream `[64x128]` register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Dual-Source Channel-Affine Monoid Context Parallelism (`DPLR-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024$ per rank), each rank summarizes its shard as an exact DPLR affine transition $(P_{\text{rank}}(r), H_{\text{rank}}(r)) \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times H \times K \times (K + V)}$ built by composing $P_c = \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(g_{i,c,B_T-1})) + B_{g,c}^\top W_c$ and the dual-source offset $H_c = K_{g,c}^\top V_c + B_{g,c}^\top U_c$, exchanges $(P_{\text{rank}}, H_{\text{rank}})$ in a single NVSwitch `all_gather_into_tensor` to seed $S_{\text{init}}(r)$, and in backward reuses saved $P_{\text{rank}}(q)^\top$ (`2x` smaller backward payload) to seed $dht(r)$, preserving 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-4`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, vs `> 25%` error when omitting cross-rank state handoff) while beating `All-Gather Full-Sequence DPLR / RWKV-7`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Asymmetric State-Evolution & Non-Commutative Permutation-Tracking Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `1.4s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on asymmetric key-redirected state-tracking & associative recall (where a transition instruction redirects an existing key $k_{\text{src}}$ to a new key $k_{\text{dst}}$ via asymmetric $b_t \not\propto a_t$ and decoupled $k_t \not\propto a_t$), Round-15 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting the transposed $a_t b_t^\top$ / wrong-`gi`-on-$a_t$ bug (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and upstream spill/recompute overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`64` steps) across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`R0_gla_diagonal_decay_only`, `R1_kda_symmetric_rank1_erase`, `R2_dplr_wrong_inclusive_ge_bug`, `R3_rwkv7_dplr_pure_rsi`, `R4_rwkv7_goose_hybrid_mla_rsi`), `R4` and `R3` must outperform diagonal-only `GLA` (`R0`), symmetric rank-1 `KDA` (`R1`, which ties removal and replacement directions $b_t \propto a_t \propto k_t$), and broken inclusive-`gi` `DPLR` (`R2`) on asymmetric state-evolution & permutation tracking (`test_bpb` and accuracy).

## 19. Round-16 Curvature-Aware Preconditioned Delta Attention (`PrecondKDA` / `PrecondDeltaNet`) & Adaptive Diagonal Curvature Preconditioning (`ATK`) RSI (`arXiv:2604.21100`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/precond_kda` & `fla/ops/atk`) & NVSwitch Two-Stage Coupled Curvature-Matrix `PKDA-CP = 8` Protocol (`E142`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from first-order channel-gated delta rules (`KDA` / `RWKV-7 DPLR`, which use isotropic scalar or fixed-vector step sizes along $k_t$ and suffer under anisotropic power-law key curvature) to **hierarchically coupled second-moment curvature-preconditioned delta attention (`Preconditioned DeltaNet: Curvature-aware Sequence Modeling for Linear Recurrences`, Moonshot AI / Kimi Team & FLA Team, `arXiv:2604.21100`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/precond_kda`, `fla/ops/atk`, & `fla/layers/precond_kda.py`)**, where Stage 1 tracks a per-channel second-moment key curvature state $X_{t+1} = \exp(g^{\text{atk}}_t) X_t + \beta^{\text{atk}}_t (k_t \odot k_t) \in \mathbb{R}_{\ge 0}^K$ and applies a bounded symmetric log-squash preconditioner
$$\ell_t = \log(X_{t+1} + \epsilon), \qquad r_t = \ell_t - c_h, \qquad s_t = \frac{r_t}{1 + |r_t|} \in (-1, 1), \qquad M_t = \exp(-\log(x)\,s_t) \in [1/x, x], \qquad \tilde{k}_t = k_t \odot M_t$$
to modulate the write key $\tilde{k}_t$ inside Stage 2's **asymmetric** channel-gated KDA recurrence
$$S_{t+1} = \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(\gamma_t)) S_t + \beta_t \tilde{k}_t \left(v_t^\top - k_t^\top \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(\gamma_t)) S_t\right), \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot S_{t+1}^\top q_t,$$
four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed two-stage coupled context-parallel, and anisotropic-curvature associative recall scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Zero-Spill `16x16` Sub-Chunked `ATK` Curvature Preconditioner + Overflow-Free Causal Asymmetric Sub-Chunking + Split-`K`/`V` Asymmetric WY Adjoint (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/precond_kda` & `fla/ops/atk` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/precond_kda` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_intra.py`, `chunk_bwd.py`, `wy_fast.py`) and `fla/ops/atk` (`chunk_atk_fwd.py`, `chunk_atk_bwd.py`) exhibit four concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) on SM80 at $K = V = 128$ (`BT = 64`), upstream accumulates **`50,812` total cubin register spills across its autotuned specializations (`318–322` cubin spills per step even in the winning autotuned configs)** — specifically: `chunk_precond_kda_bwd_kernel_wy_dqkg` (`chunk_bwd.py:72-270`) spills **`168` to `3,972` registers across `100%` (`36/36`) of its autotuned configs (`49,824` total spills, `255` max regs)** because it holds both raw $k$ and preconditioned $\tilde{k}$ plus both output gradients $\mathrm{d}k$ and $\mathrm{d}\tilde{k}_g$ alongside `[64, 64]` `b_A`, `b_dA`, `b_v`, `b_vn`, `b_h`, `b_dh`; `chunk_precond_kda_fwd_kernel_inter_solve_fused` (`chunk_intra.py:42-382`) spills **`62` to `246` registers across `100%` (`3/3`) of its autotuned configs (`430–438` total spills, `255` max regs)** because it computes all 6 off-diagonal `[16, 16]` blocks of $A_{qk}$ and all 6 off-diagonal `[16, 16]` blocks of $A_{kk}$ while inverting $(I + A_{kk})^{-1}$ in one kernel; `_atk_backward_chunk_out` (`chunk_atk_bwd.py:21-497`) spills **`88` registers across `100%` (`1/1`) of its configs (`255` max regs)** because it materializes dense `[64, 64]` matrices `M`, `gM`, and `roll_mat` in registers to differentiate a 1D scalar-gated diagonal EMA and executes contended global `tl.atomic_add` across all $B \times \text{NT}$ chunks; `recompute_w_u_fwd_kernel` (`wy_fast.py:29-121`) spills **`44` to `78` registers across `6/18` configs (`364` total spills)**; and `chunk_precond_kda_bwd_kernel_intra` (`chunk_intra.py:553-812`, `safe_gate=True`) spills **`32` to `34` registers across `3/12` configs (`98` total spills)**; (ii) in `chunk_precond_kda_fwd_kernel_intra_sub_chunk` (`chunk_intra.py:442-455`) and `chunk_precond_kda_bwd_kernel_intra` (`chunk_intra.py:678-698, 756-778`), upstream shifts the sub-chunk anchor by `b_gn = g[i_ti] - 0.5 * (BC - 1) * GATE_SHIFT` and evaluates `exp2(-b_gm)` before causal masking, while `chunk_precond_kda` (`chunk.py:552`) fails to validate `lower_bound` when `use_gate_in_kernel=False` (the default path called by `PrecondKDA.forward` in `fla/layers/precond_kda.py:296`), overflowing `exp2(-b_gm)` to `+Inf` and corrupting **`401,408` elements (`o=8,192, dq=131,072, dk=131,072, dg=131,072` at `B=2, T=128, H=4, K=V=128`) to `NaN`** at steep decay ($g = -7.2$ nats with `lower_bound=None` or $g = -9.0$ nats with `lower_bound=-5.0`); (iii) `chunk_precond_kda` launches **9 separate ATK kernels per fwd+bwd step** (`3` in `chunk_atk_fwd`, `3` in `recompute_atk_fwd` because forward discards the tiny per-chunk boundary states `ac`, and `3` in `chunk_atk_bwd`), while `PrecondKDA` launches unfused `3x ShortConvolution`, unfused KDA/ATK gate projections, separate `l2norm`, and separate `FusedRMSNormGated`; and (iv) `chunk_precond_kda` (`chunk.py:540-545`) explicitly raises `NotImplementedError("Context parallelism is not yet supported for chunk_precond_kda")`. Round-16 RSI resolves all four via: (a) **Zero-Spill Sub-Chunked (`BC = 16`) `ATK` Forward & Backward Kernels** (`_atk_fused_subchunk_state_fwd_kernel`, `_atk_subchunk_squash_out_fwd_kernel`, `_atk_subchunk_squash_bwd_kernel`, `_atk_reverse_subchunk_scan_bwd_kernel` with saved per-subchunk boundary states $X_{\text{sub},0}$ — `0` `recompute_atk_fwd` relaunches, `[16, 16]` decay tiles instead of `[64, 64]` matrices, and **`0` cubin spills**); (b) **Pre-Masked Non-Positive Causal Asymmetric Sub-Chunking (`BC = 16`) & Decoupled Off-Diagonal `Aqk` / `Akk`-Solve Kernels** (`0` `NaN`s across all non-positive decay regimes and **`0` cubin spills**); (c) **Row-Subtiled (`BM = 16`) Asymmetric WY Forward & 3-Stage Split Asymmetric WY + Intra-Subchunk Backward Kernels** with outer-and-inner strict lower-triangular masking $\mathrm{d}A_{kk} = \operatorname{strictTril}(-M_{\text{inv}}^\top \operatorname{strictTril}(\mathrm{d}M_{\text{inv}}) M_{\text{inv}}^\top)$ and exact asymmetric Euler gate identity $\mathrm{d}g_{\text{sum},t} = q_t \odot \mathrm{d}q_t + k_t \odot \mathrm{d}k^{\text{stage2}}_t - \tilde{k}_t \odot \mathrm{d}\tilde{k}_t$; and (d) **Superfused `PrecondKDA` Frontend (`3x ShortConv1d+SiLU`, `QK-L2Norm+Beta/BetaATK-Sigmoid+KDA/ATK-Gates+Cumsum`) & Epilogue (`FusedRMSNormGated`)** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `BF16 <= 2.0e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `5/5` red-team exploits: symmetric $k_t k_t^\top$ instead of asymmetric $k_t \tilde{k}_t^\top$ in $A_{kk}$/WY, omitted outer `strictTril` mask on $\mathrm{d}A_{kk}$, wrong negative sign on $\mathrm{d}\log s_{\text{atk}}$, omitted Stage-1 ATK gradient $\mathrm{d}k^{\text{atk}}$ into $\mathrm{d}k$, and `allow_tf32` mutation).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `PrecondKDA` Layer (`Track 1`) & Full `PrecondKDA` Block (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` and `b1..b4` (`T \in \{512..2048\}`, `H \in \{4, 8\}`, `K, V \in \{64, 128\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-16 RSI must strictly dominate Upstream `fla/ops/precond_kda` (`chunk_precond_kda` spill & 9-launch ATK recompute pattern), Eager Chunk `PrecondKDA`, Sequential Recurrent `PrecondKDA`, and `torch.compile` across both isolated `PrecondKDA` layers (`Track 1`) and full `PrecondKDA` blocks (`Track 2`) in latency and peak HBM while rejecting upstream `[64x64]` ATK and `[64x128]` WY register-spill regressions (`REVERT_SLOWER`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Two-Stage Hierarchically Coupled Curvature-State + Asymmetric Matrix-Monoid Context Parallelism (`PKDA-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024$ per rank), resolving upstream's `NotImplementedError`: Stage 1 summarizes each rank's curvature recurrence $X_{t+1} = \exp(g^{\text{atk}}_t) X_t + \beta^{\text{atk}}_t k_t^2$ as a scalar-vector affine pair $(p^{\text{atk}}_r, a^{\text{atk}}_r) \in \mathbb{R}_{>0} \times \mathbb{R}^K$, all-gathers across 8 GPUs to seed exact $X_{r,0}$, and materializes exact local preconditioned keys $\tilde{k}_{r,t} = k_{r,t} \odot M(X_{r,t+1})$; Stage 2 summarizes each rank's asymmetric matrix recurrence as $(P_r, H_r) \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times K} \times \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ built from $P_c = \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(g_{c,\text{last}})) - \tilde{K}_{g,c}^\top W_c$ and $H_c = \tilde{K}_{g,c}^\top U_c$, all-gathers to seed exact $S_{r,0}$, and in backward executes the coupled two-stage reverse adjoint scan (Stage 2 reverse matrix scan $\mathrm{d}S_{r,T_{\text{loc}}} = \sum_{q > r} (P_{r+1 \to q-1})^\top G_q + (P_{r+1 \to R-1})^\top \mathrm{d}S_T \implies \mathrm{d}\tilde{k}_{r,t}$, followed by Stage 1 reverse scalar-vector curvature scan $\mathrm{d}X_{r,T_{\text{loc}}} = \sum_{q > r} (\prod_{m=r+1}^{q-1} p^{\text{atk}}_m) \gamma^{\text{atk}}_q + (\prod_{m=r+1}^{R-1} p^{\text{atk}}_m) \mathrm{d}X_T \implies \mathrm{d}k_{r,t}, \mathrm{d}g^{\text{atk}}_{r,t}, \mathrm{d}\beta^{\text{atk}}_{r,t}, \mathrm{d}c_h$), preserving 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-4`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, vs `> 25%` error when omitting cross-rank state handoff) while beating `All-Gather Full-Sequence PrecondKDA`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Anisotropic Power-Law Curvature & Rare-Channel Associative Recall Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `1.4s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on anisotropic power-law key-spectrum associative recall (where dominant high-variance key channels coexist with low-energy rare informative channels that unpreconditioned delta rules under-fit or overwrite), Round-16 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting symmetric unpreconditioned $k_t k_t^\top$ $A_{kk}$ corruption (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and upstream spill/recompute overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`64` steps) across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`P0_gla_no_delta_no_precond`, `P1_deltanet_scalar_gate_no_precond`, `P2_kda_channel_gate_no_atk_precond`, `P3_precond_kda_symmetric_akk_bug`, `P4_precond_kda_full_atk_block_rsi`), `P4` must outperform `GLA` (`P0`), scalar `DeltaNet` (`P1`), unpreconditioned `KDA` (`P2`), and symmetric-$A_{kk}$-bug `PrecondKDA` (`P3`) on anisotropic-curvature associative recall (`test_bpb` and recall accuracy).

## 20. Round-17 Triadic Gated DeltaNet (`Triadic-GDN`) & Triadic Kimi Delta Attention (`Triadic-KDA`): Kronecker Tensor-Product State Expansion & Hadamard-Factored UT/WY Linear Recurrences RSI (`arXiv:2609.36529`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/triadic_gdn` & `fla/ops/triadic_kda`) & NVSwitch Parallel-WY Triadic State Context Parallelism `Triadic-CP = 8` Protocol (`E143`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from single-matrix $[K, V]$ state recurrences (`GDN` / `KDA` / `RWKV-7 DPLR` / `PrecondKDA`, which store $O(K V)$ state per head and suffer capacity saturation under high-density multi-item associative recall) to **Kronecker tensor-product state expansion (`Triadic Gated DeltaNet` & `Triadic Kimi Delta Attention`, `arXiv:2609.36529`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/triadic_gdn` & `fla/ops/triadic_kda`)**, where each head maintains $E$ state slices $S_e \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ ($S \in \mathbb{R}^{E \times K \times V}$, expanding associative state capacity by $E\times$) addressed by the Kronecker / Khatri-Rao joint key and query
$$\kappa_t = k^{(2)}_t \otimes k_t \in \mathbb{R}^{E \times K}, \qquad \rho_t = q^{(2)}_t \otimes q_t \in \mathbb{R}^{E \times K}$$
with slice-wise log-decay $g_{t,e} \le 0$ (`Triadic-GDN`) or Kronecker-sum log-decay $g^{(2)}_{t,e} + g^{(k)}_{t,d} \le 0$ (`Triadic-KDA`) and within-chunk Hadamard Gram factorization
$$A = \operatorname{strictTril}\!\bigl(\operatorname{Diag}(\beta)\bigl(L^{(k)} \odot R\bigr)\bigr), \qquad A_{qk} = \text{scale} \cdot \operatorname{tril}\!\bigl(M^{(qk)} \odot R^{(q)}\bigr), \qquad A_i = (I + A)^{-1},$$
four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed multi-slice context-parallel, and Kronecker state-capacity scaling gates are enforced:

1. **Dual-Sphere $\mathbb{S}^{K-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{E-1}$ Contraction Stability + Zero-Spill L2-Staged Sub-Tiled `[BK=32, BV=32]` Triadic State & Hadamard-Gram Kernels + First-Ever Zero-Spill Triton `Triadic-KDA` & Superfused `TriadicGDNBlock` (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/triadic_gdn` & `fla/ops/triadic_kda` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/triadic_gdn` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_fwd.py`, `chunk_bwd.py`, `naive.py`) and `fla/ops/triadic_kda` (`naive.py`) exhibit five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) because $\|\kappa_t\|_2^2 = \|k^{(2)}_t\|_2^2 \|k_t\|_2^2$, the Householder transition $I - \beta_t \kappa_t \kappa_t^\top$ is contractive ($\beta_t \|\kappa_t\|_2^2 \le 2$) when $(k_t, k^{(2)}_t) \in \mathbb{S}^{K-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{E-1}$, yet upstream `fla/ops/triadic_gdn/chunk.py` omits `use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel` completely, so unnormalized standard-normal projections have $\mathbb{E}[\|\kappa_t\|_2^2] = E K = 256$, causing $A_i = (I + A)^{-1}$ to explode to **`1.019e+35`** within one 64-token chunk and corrupting **`32,768 / 32,768` (`100%`) of outputs in `o` to `NaN` (`115,796` `NaN`/`Inf` gradients across `dq, dk, dv, dk2, dq2, dg, dbeta`)**; (ii) on SM80 across $K, V \in \{64, 128\}$ and $E \in \{2, 4, 8\}$ (`BT = 64`), upstream `fla/ops/triadic_gdn` compiles **`5/6` kernels to the `255`-register SM80 ceiling (`36/42` specializations at `248–255` regs) and spills `1,698` total registers across `20/42` (`47.6%`) specializations**: `chunk_triadic_gdn_bwd_kernel_intra` (`chunk_bwd.py:251-355`) holds 10 simultaneous `[64, 64]` `FP32` matrices (`b_dA, b_dAqk, b_kk, b_qk, b_R, b_Rq, b_dP, b_dPq, b_dR, b_dRq`), spilling **`180–206` registers across `6/6` (`100%`) specializations (`1,144` spills)**; `chunk_triadic_gdn_bwd_kernel_inter` (`chunk_bwd.py:165-248`) loads full `BV = V = 128` without subtiling `V`, spilling **`24–58` registers across `6/6` (`100%`) specializations (`240` spills)**; `chunk_triadic_gdn_bwd_kernel_dhu` (`chunk_bwd.py:18-162`) and `chunk_triadic_gdn_fwd_kernel_h` (`chunk_fwd.py:173-274`) unroll `tl.static_range(E)` keeping all $E$ state slices `[BV, BK=128]` in registers simultaneously (`214` and `86` spills); and `chunk_triadic_gdn_fwd_kernel_intra` (`chunk_fwd.py:92-156`) inline-expands `triadic_intra_r` (`14` spills); (iii) upstream hardcodes `DOT_PRECISION = 'tf32'` (`chunk_fwd.py:18`) and uses default `input_precision='tf32'` even when `q.dtype == torch.float32`, degrading `FP32` relative $L_2$ error to **`2.213e-3` on `o` and `1.848e-3` on `dq`** (`~1,000x` worse than IEEE `FP32`); (iv) by default (`disable_recompute=False`), `chunk_triadic_gdn_bwd` (`chunk_bwd.py:424-436`) re-executes `chunk_triadic_gdn_fwd_h` from scratch while allocating 5D `[NK, B, T, H, E]` `FP32` buffers `qs, ks, kd, ss` and 4D `[B, T, H, BT]` `dR, dRq`, `fla/ops/triadic_kda/` has **only `naive.py` (`FP64` sequential Python loop) and zero Triton kernels**, and `fla/layers/` has no `TriadicGDNBlock` module; and (v) upstream has no multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`CP`) for Triadic state $S \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times H \times E \times K \times V}$. Round-17 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Fused Dual-Sphere $\mathbb{S}^{K-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{E-1}$ $L_2$-Norm + Gate Cumsum Frontend & Epilogue (`ShortConv1d+SiLU`, `DualSphere-L2Norm+BetaSigmoid+GateCumsum`, `FusedRMSNormGated`)**; (b) **Decoupled Zero-Spill Intra-Chunk Routing (`R, Rq`), Hadamard-Gram (`A, Aqk`), Split Block-Triangular Solve (`Ai`), and L2-Staged Sub-Tiled `[BK=32, BV=32]` Triadic State Forward & Backward Kernels** (eliminating all `1,698` spills to **`0` cubin spills and `max_regs <= 168` across `100%` of Round-17 RSI kernels**, with `IEEE` `FP32` precision `max_rel_l2 <= 5e-5` vs `2.21e-3` in upstream); and (c) **Zero-Spill Kronecker-Sum `Triadic-KDA` Forward & Backward Triton Kernels** (`FP32 max_rel_l2 <= 5e-5`, `BF16 <= 1.5e-2`, `7/7` `FP64` `O(h^2)` Richardson `~4.000x`, rejecting `5/5` red-team exploits: `RT1` unnormalized Kronecker spectral explosion `NaN`, `RT2` omitted Hadamard factor $R_{ij}$ in $A$, `RT3` wrong sign $+\operatorname{strictTril}(\mathrm{d}x\,u^\top)$ on $\mathrm{d}A$, `RT4` omitted column-transpose $\mathrm{d}k^{(2)}$ / reverse-cumsum $\mathrm{d}g$, and `RT5` `allow_tf32` flag mutation + omitted dual-sphere radial `L2Norm` VJP).
2. **Vector-Pareto Ratchets Across Core `Triadic-GDN / Triadic-KDA` Layer (`Track 1`) & Full `TriadicGDNBlock` (`Track 2`)**:
   - Across `s1..s4` and `b1..b4` (`T \in \{1024, 2048\}`, `H = 8`, `K = V \in \{64, 128\}`, `E \in \{4, 8\}`, `BT = 64`), Round-17 RSI must strictly dominate Upstream Spill (`chunk_triadic_gdn` with recompute & `255`-reg spills / `naive_chunk_triadic_kda`), Eager Chunk `Triadic-GDN`, Sequential Recurrent `Triadic-GDN`, and `torch.compile` across both isolated layers (`Track 1`) and full `TriadicGDNBlock` modules (`Track 2`) in latency and peak HBM while demonstrating a strictly monotonic 5-stage progressive fusion ablation ladder (`A0..A4`).
3. **8-GPU NVSwitch Parallel-WY Triadic State Context Parallelism (`Triadic-CP = 8`, `Gate G9`)**:
   - Across $P = 8$ GPUs (`CP = 8`, $T_{\text{global}} = 8,192$, $T_{\text{local}} = 1,024$ per rank), every rank computes its intra-chunk Dual-Sphere $L_2$-Norm, cumulative gates, Hadamard Gram matrices $(R, R^{(q)}, A, A_{qk})$, and triangular inverse $A_i = (I + A)^{-1}$ **in parallel across all 8 GPUs**, propagates the exact coupled $E$-slice Triadic boundary state $S_{\text{rank}} \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times H \times E \times K \times V}$ (and reverse adjoint $\mathrm{d}S_{\text{rank}}$ in backward) across NVSwitch without decoupling the $E$ slices, and executes all intra/inter/gate backward kernels in parallel across all 8 GPUs, preserving 3-step multi-update `FP32` equivalence against a 1-GPU full-sequence oracle (`Gate G9`: `loss_rel_diff <= 1e-4`, `max_param_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, `max_grad_rel_l2 <= 5e-4`, vs `> 25%` error when omitting cross-rank Triadic state handoff) while beating `All-Gather Full-Sequence Triadic-GDN`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock `val_bpb` RSI Loop + 5-Arm Kronecker State-Capacity Expansion Scaling Law**:
   - Under a fixed `1.4s` `8xA100 DDP` budget on high-density multi-slice associative recall (where each head must store and retrieve $4\times$ more key-value pairs than a single $[K, V]$ state slice can separate without interference), Round-17 RSI must maximize completed updates and improve both `val_bpb` and untouched single-touch `test_bpb` while automatically reverting omitted Hadamard routing factor $R_{ij}$ (`REVERT_VAL_BPB`) and upstream spill/recompute overhead (`REVERT_SLOWER`). At matched steps (`64` steps) across `5` predeclared sequence-modeling architectures (`R0_single_slice_gdn_e1`, `R1_collinear_unrouted_triadic_e4`, `R2_omitted_hadamard_r_bug_e4`, `R3_pure_triadic_gdn_e4_rsi`, `R4_triadic_kda_full_block_e4_rsi`), `R4` and `R3` must outperform single-slice `GDN (E=1)` (`R0`), collinear unrouted `Triadic (E=4, k2=1)` (`R1`), and broken Hadamard-$R$ `Triadic` (`R2`) on high-density associative recall (`test_bpb` and recall accuracy).

## 21. Round-18 Momentum DeltaNet: Coupled Dual-State $[S, M]$ Heavy-Ball Momentum Test-Time Gradient Descent, Zero-Spill 3-RHS Coupled WY Forward & Adjoint Kernels, Algebraic Symplectic Gate Fusion & 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses-WY `Momentum-CP = 8` Protocol (`E144`, `arXiv:2605.05838`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/momentum_delta_rule` & `fla/layers/momentum_deltanet.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from first-order online gradient descent (`Gated DeltaNet` / `KDA` / `Triadic-GDN`) to **second-order heavy-ball momentum test-time gradient descent over a coupled dual matrix state $(S_t, M_t) \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V} \times \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ (`Momentum DeltaNet`, `arXiv:2605.05838`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/momentum_delta_rule` & `fla/layers/momentum_deltanet.py`)**, where each token updates the momentum matrix $M_t$ and associative memory matrix $S_t$ via
$$\begin{aligned}
v^{\text{new}}_t &= v_t - S_{t-1}^\top \tilde{p}_t, \\
M_t &= \mu_t M_{t-1} - \tilde{k}_t \bigl(v^{\text{new}}_t\bigr)^\top, \\
S_t &= \alpha_t S_{t-1} - \beta_t M_t = \alpha_t S_{t-1} - \beta_t \mu_t M_{t-1} + \beta_t \tilde{k}_t \bigl(v^{\text{new}}_t\bigr)^\top, \\
o_t &= S_t^\top \tilde{q}_t,
\end{aligned}$$
with chunkwise two-timescale Green's function $\Gamma_q = D_\alpha (\operatorname{Diag}(\beta) D_\mu)$, boundary momentum coupling vector $b = D_\alpha (\beta \odot \bar{\mu})$, row-shifted strictly lower-triangular Green's function $\Gamma_{i, j} = \mathbf{1}_{i > j} [\Gamma_q]_{i-1, j}$, system matrix $A = (\tilde{P} \tilde{K}^\top) \odot \Gamma$, inverse $A_i = (I + A)^{-1}$, and **3-RHS Coupled WY Basis Tensors**
$$U_c = A_i V, \qquad Y_c = A_i (\tilde{\alpha} \odot \tilde{P}), \qquad Z_c = A_i (\tilde{b} \odot \tilde{P}), \qquad V_{\text{new}} = U_c - Y_c S_{\text{pre}} + Z_c M_{\text{pre}},$$
four domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, distributed coupled dual-state context-parallel, and second-order momentum convergence scaling gates are enforced:

1. **First-Ever Zero-Spill NVIDIA CUDA Triton Forward & Analytical Adjoint Kernels + Exact Green's Function $b_t$ + Overflow-Safe Algebraic Trigonometric Symplectic Gate Fusion (`0` Cubin Spills, Upstream `fla/ops/momentum_delta_rule` & `fla/layers/momentum_deltanet.py` Forensics)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/momentum_delta_rule` (`chunk.py`, `naive.py`, `fused_recurrent.py`, `backends/triton_ascend/`) and `fla/layers/momentum_deltanet.py` exhibit five concrete mathematical and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) **Zero NVIDIA CUDA Triton Kernels & Zero Backward Triton Kernels on ANY Platform**: on NVIDIA GPUs, `chunk_momentum_delta_rule` falls back to `chunk_momentum_delta_rule_ref` (`naive.py`), executing a 63-step sequential Python loop for $(I+A)^{-1}$ and an $N_T$-step sequential Python loop over chunks, and in `backward` re-executing the entire forward pass inside `with torch.enable_grad():` via `torch.autograd.grad(...)` (`~195 ms` per step at $B=2, T=2048, H=8, K=64, V=128$); meanwhile the Huawei Ascend backend (`backends/triton_ascend/`) has **zero backward Triton kernels**, runs a sequential chunk loop inside `chunk_momentum_delta_fwd_o_kernel_npu`, and when its 4 forward kernels are compiled on `SM80` at $(K=64, V=128)$ they spill **`4,624` total registers (`234 + 2,432 + 1,624 + 334`, `100%` of kernels spill)** and at $(K=128, V=128)$ (`223,768 > 166,912` B SMEM) and the layer default $(K=256, V=256)$ (`420,376 > 166,912` B SMEM) they **crash hard on `SM80` with `triton.runtime.errors.OutOfResources`**; (ii) **Hard `AssertionError` Refusing `FP32` Training (`chunk.py:92`)**: `assert q.dtype != torch.float32`; (iii) **`beta + 1e-6` Log-Cumsum-Exp Bias (`triton_ascend/chunk_momentum_delta.py:85`) & Redundant $O(T)$ Non-Chunk-Multiple Fallback (`naive.py:187`)**: `log_beta = (beta_p + 1e-6).log()` injects spurious momentum drift even when $\beta_t = 0$, while `naive.py:187` discards chunk final states whenever $T \bmod 64 \ne 0$ and re-runs an $O(T)$ sequential token loop; and (iv) **14-Launch Unfused Trigonometric Symplectic Gate & Output-Correction Pipeline (`layers/momentum_deltanet.py:239-265`)**: launching separate `arctan`, `sin**2`, `cos**2`, `log(cos**2)`, `softplus`, `tanh`, `sigmoid`, `clamp_min_`, `q - exp(D_log)*k`, and `l2norm` kernels. Round-18 RSI resolves all via: (a) **Zero-Spill 3-RHS Coupled WY Forward & Analytical Adjoint (`dX = A_i^T dV_new`) Kernels** (proving $dV = dX$, $dA = -\operatorname{strictTril}(dX\,V_{\text{new}}^\top)$, $dP_y = -dX\,S_{\text{pre}}^\top$, and $dP_z = +dX\,M_{\text{pre}}^\top$ without materializing $dY_c$ or $dZ_c$, with in-register `[K=64, BV=32]` dual-state recurrence and L2-streamed cross-slab synchronized `[BK=64, BV=32]` recurrence for $K \in \{128, 256\}$ achieving **`0` SM80 cubin register spills and `max_regs <= 168` across `100%` of Round-18 RSI kernels**); (b) **Exact Bias-Free Green's Function $b = D_\alpha (\beta \odot \bar{\mu})$ & Exact Zero-Padded Non-Chunk-Multiple Boundary Handling**; and (c) **Overflow-Safe Algebraic Symplectic Gate Fusion (`1/(1+r^2)` & `r^2/(1+r^2)` piecewise reciprocal for $r = \eta e^{\gamma}$) + Output Correction + Triple `L2Norm` + `3x ShortConv1d+SiLU` + `FusedRMSNormGated`**.
2. **Oracle Parity Across 5 Stress Regimes & Frozen-Evaluator Red-Team (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-5` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 2e-2` across 5 stress regimes (`T=137` non-chunk-multiple, `T=256`, `K=128, V=128`, decoupled $p \ne k$, and high-momentum $\mu \to 0.99, \beta \in [1.5, 1.99]$) against an independent `FP64` recurrent oracle across all 10 forward/backward outputs and gradients (`o, S_T, M_T, dq, dk, dv, dp, dlog_alpha, dlog_mu, dbeta, deta, dS0, dM0`), and reject `3/3` negative-control ablations (`omit_momentum_z_c`, `broken_ut_solve`, `corrupt_bwd_dx`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Kernel Sweep & Cumulative Block Ablation Ladder (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_momentum_delta_rule_rsi` across shapes (`K=64, V=128`, `K=128, V=128`, `K=256, V=256`, `T=2048, 4096`) against upstream `chunk_momentum_delta_rule` (`naive.py` + autograd replay) and the adapted Ascend forward kernel path, and demonstrate a strictly monotonic cumulative block speedup across `Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4` for `MomentumDeltaNetBlockRSI`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock (`val_bpb`) Autoresearch Loop (`Track 4`)**:
   - Under an identical `25.0s` wall-clock training budget on a 4-layer `MomentumDeltaNet` language model, compare `Arm 1` (`Stage A0`: upstream eager chunk + autograd replay), `Arm 2` (`Stage A2`: zero-spill 3-RHS WY + analytical backward, unfused block periphery), and `Arm 3` (`Stage A4`: full Round-18 RSI superfused `MomentumDeltaNetBlockRSI`), measuring completed optimizer steps, tokens/sec, and final validation `val_bpb`.
5. **8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses-WY Coupled Dual-State Context Parallelism (`Momentum-CP = 8`, `Track 5`)**:
   - Partition long sequences ($T_{\text{global}} \in \{16384, 32768, 65536\}$) across 8 A100 GPUs via single-call NVSwitch `dist.all_to_all_single` head-partitioned Ulysses-WY `Momentum-CP = 8`, preserving exact `0.000e+00` dual-state $(S, M)$ output and gradient parity vs 1-GPU full-sequence execution (and quantifying the boundary error when cross-shard dual-state continuity $(S_{\text{pre}}, M_{\text{pre}})$ is omitted) while outperforming 1x A100 and 8-GPU sequential dual-state ring handoff.

## 22. Round-19 Gated KalmaNet (`GKA`): Fading-Memory Test-Time Ridge Regression via Inner-Product-Free Chebyshev Iteration, Implicit Differentiation, Zero-Spill Query-Subtiled `[BM=16]` Runtime-Loop Solver & 8-GPU NVSwitch Coupled Dual-State `[H_kk, U_kv]` Context Parallelism (`E145`, `arXiv:2511.21016`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) from inner-product-dependent conjugate-gradient test-time training (`MesaNet`) and first/second-order online gradient descent (`Gated DeltaNet` / `Momentum DeltaNet`) to **exact fading-memory test-time ridge regression solved via inner-product-free Chebyshev polynomial iteration and implicit differentiation (`Gated KalmaNet: A Fading Memory Layer Through Test-Time Ridge Regression`, `arXiv:2511.21016`; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py`)**, where at every token $t$ the layer maintains the coupled Gram and cross-covariance states $(H_t, U_t) \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times K} \times \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$:
$$\begin{aligned}
H_t &= e^{g^{(k)}_t} H_{t-1} + k_t k_t^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times K}, \qquad U_t = e^{g_t} U_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}, \\
\lambda_t &= a \|H_t\|_F, \qquad A_t = H_t + \lambda_t I_K, \qquad x_t = A_t^{-1} q_t, \\
\tilde{q}_t &= q_t + \alpha_t (x_t - q_t), \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot U_t^\top \tilde{q}_t,
\end{aligned}$$
with guaranteed spectral interval $[\mu_t, L_t] = [\lambda_t, \lambda_t + \|H_t\|_F]$ (bounding $\kappa(A_t) \le (a+1)/a$), inner-product-free Chebyshev recurrence
$$\omega_0 = 2, \quad \xi_{-1} = 0, \quad \xi_0 = \frac{2}{2\lambda_t + \|H_t\|_F} q_t, \quad \omega_n = \frac{1}{1 - (\rho^2/4)\omega_{n-1}}, \quad \xi_n = \omega_n \xi_{n-1} - \left(\frac{2\omega_n}{2\lambda_t + \|H_t\|_F}(A_t \xi_{n-1} - q_t) + (\omega_n - 1)\xi_{n-2}\right),$$
and implicit ridge adjoint $\mathrm{d}q^{\text{solve}}_t = A_t^{-1} \mathrm{d}x_t$, $\mathrm{d}H_t = -\frac{1}{2}(\mathrm{d}q^{\text{solve}}_t x_t^\top + x_t (\mathrm{d}q^{\text{solve}}_t)^\top) - a \frac{\langle \mathrm{d}q^{\text{solve}}_t, x_t \rangle}{\|H_t\|_F} H_t$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, compilation-latency, distributed coupled dual-state context-parallel, and fixed-wall-clock convergence gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill `[BM=16]` Runtime-Loop Round-19 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/gka` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_solve_fwd.py`, `chunk_solve_bwd.py`) and `fla/layers/gka.py` exhibit five concrete mathematical, compilation, and GPU-kernel pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`SM80`): (i) **Severe SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`193/219` = `88.1%` of Specializations Spilling, `1,519,648` B Stack Spills, `ptxas` `regs=32` Allocator Collapse & `262,144 > 166,912` B SMEM Overflow)**: `chunk_gka_solve_fwd_kernel` spills registers across **`75/75` (`100%`)** of compiled specializations (`255` regs, up to `2,536` B stack/thread, `61,248` B total stack) because it holds all `BT=64` query rows `[64, K]` AND the dense `[K, K]` state `b_h` in registers simultaneously while compile-time unrolling `NUM_ITER: tl.constexpr = 30` into a **`1.7 MB` PTX file (`82.3–200.1 s` cold compilation per shape)**; `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_intra` spills registers across **`72/72` (`100%`)** of specializations, triggering catastrophic `ptxas` register-allocator collapse to `regs=32` with up to **`47,864` B stack/thread (`1,227,136` B total stack)** and up to **`262,144` B shared memory (`> 166,912` B `SM80` ceiling, forcing a 2-pass `PART=1, 2` split hack)**; and `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_inter` spills registers across **`46/72` (`63.9%`)** of specializations (`max_stack = 19,904` B/thread, `231,264` B total stack); (ii) **Zero-Key / Masked-Token `NaN` Contamination (`32,768 / 65,536` `dk` `NaN`s from a Single $k_0 = 0$ Token)**: when $\|H_t\|_F = 0$, upstream `b_step = 2 / (2 * b_lamb + b_fro)` and `b_w = 2 * b_tmp / b_fro` divide `0.0 / 0.0 = NaN`, and `tl.dot(b_m * b_w[None, :], tl.trans(b_m))` propagates that single `NaN` across all 64 tokens of the chunk (`32,768 / 65,536` `dk` `NaN`s, `512` `o/dq/dg` `NaN`s); (iii) **`TF32` Truncation in `FP32` Mode (`dg rel_l2 = 2.81e-3`, `dk rel_l2 = 2.20e-3`)**: upstream omits `input_precision='ieee'` on `FP32` inputs and casts `(b_kk * b_kk).to(b_q.dtype)` to `BF16` inside $\|H_t\|_F^2$; (iv) **Redundant Backward State Recomputations (`2x chunk_fwd_h`), Duplicate `chunk_local_cumsum`, & 11-Launch Unfused `GatedKalmaNet` Periphery**; and (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`CP`)** for the coupled dual state $(H_t, U_t)$. Round-19 RSI resolves all five via: (a) **Decoupled `FP32` Frobenius-Norm Kernel (`_gka_fro_fwd_kernel`) + Query-Subtiled (`BM = 16`) Runtime-Loop Chebyshev Solver (`_gka_chebyshev_solve_fwd_kernel`)** exploiting the mathematical independence of token rows $t \in [0, 64)$ in $A_t x_t = q_t$ with slab-streamed $H_{[c]}$ (`[BS=32, K]`), runtime `int32` loop `for _ in range(0, num_iter)` (`< 1 s` compile time vs `82–200 s`), safe zero-history guard `fro_safe = max(fro, 1e-12)`, and fused $\alpha$-mixing epilogue; (b) **4-Kernel `[32, 32]`/`[16, 32]` Slab-Streamed Implicit Ridge Backward** eliminating `[128, 128]` register residency and achieving **`0` SM80 cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of Round-19 RSI kernels**; and (c) **Saved Chunk States $(H_{[c]}, U_{[c]})$ + Superfused `GatedKalmaNetBlockRSI` Frontend & Epilogue**.
2. **Oracle Parity Across 5 Stress Regimes & Frozen-Evaluator Red-Team (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-5` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 2e-2` across 5 stress regimes (`T=137` non-chunk-multiple, `T=256`, `K=V=128`, decoupled $g^{(k)} \ne g$, and zero-key $k_0 = 0$ token robustness) against an independent `FP64` recurrent oracle across all outputs and gradients (`o, H_T, U_T, dq, dk, dv, dg, dgk, dalpha, dH0, dU0`), and reject `3/3` negative-control ablations (`omit_fro_lamb_bwd`, `zero_iter_gla_collapse`, `corrupt_chebyshev_sign`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Kernel Sweep & Cumulative Block Ablation Ladder (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_gka_rsi` across shapes (`K=64, V=64`, `K=64, V=128`, `K=128, V=128`, `T=2048, 4096, 8192`) against upstream `chunk_gka` (`193/219` spilling specs + backward state recomputation) and vectorized eager chunk `GKA`, and demonstrate a strictly monotonic cumulative block speedup across `Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4` for `GatedKalmaNetBlockRSI` vs upstream `GatedKalmaNet`.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock (`val_bpb`) Autoresearch Loop (`Track 4`)**:
   - Under an identical `25.0s` wall-clock training budget on a 4-layer `GatedKalmaNet` language model, compare `Arm 1` (`Stage A0`: vectorized eager chunk `GKA`), `Arm 2` (`Stage A1`: upstream `fla` `chunk_gka` with `1.52 MB` cubin spills + unfused block periphery), and `Arm 3` (`Stage A4`: full Round-19 RSI zero-spill superfused `GatedKalmaNetBlockRSI`), measuring completed optimizer steps, tokens/sec, and final validation `val_bpb`.
5. **Zero-Copy `B=1` 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses Coupled Dual-State Context Parallelism (`GKA-CP = 8`, `Track 5`)**:
   - Partition long sequences ($T_{\text{global}} \in \{8192, 16384, 32768\}, H = 128$) across 8 A100 GPUs via zero-copy `B=1` NVSwitch `dist.all_to_all_single` head-partitioned Ulysses `GKA-CP = 8` propagating coupled $(H_t, U_t)$ and adaptive Frobenius ridge $\lambda_t = a \|H_t\|_F$, preserving exact `0.000e+00` output and gradient parity vs 1-GPU full-sequence execution (and quantifying the boundary error when cross-shard $(H_{\text{pre}}, U_{\text{pre}})$ continuity is omitted) while outperforming 1x A100 and 8-GPU sequential dual-state ring handoff.

## 23. Round-20 Gated DeltaNet-2 (`GDN-2`), Gated Oja Rule 2 (`Oja-2`), & Gated Slot Attention 2 (`GSA-2`): Left/Right Decoupled Channel-Wise Erase-Write Matrix & Hierarchical Two-Level Slot Memory, Span-Checked Midpoint-Anchored `16x16` Tensor Core Sub-Chunking, State-Invariant `BK * M = 4096` Zero-Spill Recurrence & 8-GPU NVSwitch Hierarchical `GSA2-CP = 8` (`E146`, `NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2`, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/gdn2`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule2`, `fla/layers/gdn2.py` & `fla/layers/gsa2.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to the **unified Two-Level Decoupled Channel-Wise Erase-and-Write Memory family (`GDN-2`, `Oja-2`, and `GSA-2`)** in `fla-org/flash-linear-attention` (`fla/ops/gdn2`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule2`, `fla/layers/gdn2.py`, and `fla/layers/gsa2.py`), which unifies:
1. **Gated DeltaNet-2 (`GDN-2`, `NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2`, `fla/ops/gdn2`)**: Left-acting matrix recurrence on $S_t^{\text{GDN2}} \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ with channel-wise erase gate $b_t \in (0, 1)^K$, channel-wise write gate $w_t \in (0, 1)^V$, and channel-wise log-decay $g_t \in (-\infty, 0]^K$:
   $$S_t^{\text{GDN2}} = \left(I_K - k_t (b_t \odot k_t)^\top\right) \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(g_t)) S_{t-1}^{\text{GDN2}} + k_t (w_t \odot v_t)^\top, \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot (S_t^{\text{GDN2}})^\top q_t,$$
   with chunkwise strictly lower-triangular key-Gram matrix $L_{ij}^{\text{GDN2}} = \mathbf{1}_{i > j} \sum_{d=1}^K b_{i,d} k_{i,d} k_{j,d} 2^{\bar{g}_{i,d} - \bar{g}_{j,d}}$, inverse $A_{kk} = (I_{B_T} + L^{\text{GDN2}})^{-1}$, and channel-wise decoupled WY factors $W_{\text{wy}} = A_{kk} (b \odot 2^{\bar{g}} \odot k) \in \mathbb{R}^{B_T \times K}$ and $U_{\text{wy}} = A_{kk} (w \odot v) \in \mathbb{R}^{B_T \times V}$;
2. **Gated Oja Rule 2 (`Oja-2`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule2`)**: Exact right-acting slot-memory dual on $S_t^{\text{Oja2}} \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times M}$ ($M \in \{64, 128, 256\}$ slots) with per-slot erase gate $b_t \in (0, 1)^M$, per-channel write gate $c_t \in (0, 1)^K$, $L_2$-normalized slot code $v_t \in \mathbb{R}^M$, and per-slot decay $g_t^{(v)} \in [-5, 0]^M$:
   $$S_t^{\text{Oja2}} = S_{t-1}^{\text{Oja2}} \operatorname{Diag}(\exp(g_t^{(v)})) \left(I_M - (b_t \odot v_t) v_t^\top\right) + (c_t \odot k_t) v_t^\top, \qquad o_t^{(1)} = \text{scale}_1 \cdot (S_t^{\text{Oja2}})^\top q_t \in \mathbb{R}^M,$$
   with strictly lower-triangular slot-Gram matrix $L_{ij}^{\text{Oja2}} = \mathbf{1}_{i > j} \sum_{m=1}^M b_{i,m} v_{i,m} v_{j,m} \exp(\bar{g}_{i,m}^{(v)} - \bar{g}_{j,m}^{(v)})$, inverse $A_{vv} = (I_{B_T} + L^{\text{Oja2}})^{-1}$, and WY factors $W_{\text{oja}} = A_{vv} (b \odot v \odot \exp(\bar{g}^{(v)})) \in \mathbb{R}^{B_T \times M}$ and $U_{\text{oja}} = A_{vv} (c \odot k) \in \mathbb{R}^{B_T \times K}$; and
3. **Gated Slot Attention 2 (`GSA-2`, `fla/layers/gsa2.py`)**: Hierarchical two-level cascade feeding the activated Level-1 `Oja-2` slot readout $q_t^{(2)} = \phi(o_t^{(1)})$ and slot code $k_t^{(2)} = v_t$ (with optional slot projection when $M \ne D_2$) into Level-2 `GDN-2` value memory $S_t^{(2)} \in \mathbb{R}^{D_2 \times V}$,

five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, sub-chunk Tensor Core, superfused layer periphery, and 8-GPU NVSwitch hierarchical context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/gdn2`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule`, `fla/ops/gated_oja_rule2`, `fla/layers/gdn2.py` & `fla/layers/gsa2.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-20 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla` implementations exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Severe SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`255` Registers, `1,008` Total Spill Bytes Across Winning Configs)**: `chunk_gdn2_bwd_kernel_wy_dqkg_fused` hits `255` registers (`1` CTA/SM) and spills `206` registers/thread (`406` total spills, `1.189 ms` at $K=128, V=128$); `chunk_gdn2_fwd_kernel_inter_solve_fused` (`255` regs, `14` spills/thread) and `chunk_gdn2_bwd_kernel_intra` (`255` regs, `4` spills/thread, `1.308 ms`) spill because they hold 12 `[16, 16]` FP32 accumulators + `[16, 64]` FP32 tiles simultaneously and promote off-diagonal `b_kg, b_qg, b_kbg` to `FP32` instead of `BF16` (`312 TFLOPS` Tensor Cores); `oja2_bwd_kernel_wy` (`255` regs, `132` spills/thread, `530` total spills) and `oja2_fwd_kernel_wy` (`255` regs, `12` spills/thread) spill across all winning specializations; and `chunk_oja_fwd_kernel_h_blockdim64` (`255` regs, `163,840` B SMEM) and `chunk_oja_bwd_kernel_dhu_blockdim64` (`255` regs, `16` spills/thread, `131,072` B SMEM) hold all 4 `[BK, 64]` FP32 state tiles (`b_h1..b_h4` for $M=256$) simultaneously in registers, causing `bwd_dhu` to jump `2.75x` (`0.448 -> 1.231 ms`) at $M=256$;
     - (ii) **Five 16-Step Scalar SIMT Diagonal Sub-Chunk Loops (`for j in range(16)`)**: `chunk_gdn2_bwd_kernel_intra` (when `safe_gate=False`), `chunk_oja2_fwd_kernel_kkt_intra`, `chunk_oja2_bwd_kernel_kkt` (`1.274 ms` at $M=256$), `chunk_oja_fwd_intra`, `chunk_oja_bwd_kernel_dA` (`0.524 ms`), and `chunk_oja_bwd_kernel_dv_o` (`0.561 ms`) execute 16-step scalar SIMT loops issuing 160+ uncoalesced 1D global memory loads and 32 vector `exp`/`exp2` evaluations instead of $16\times 16$ Tensor Core `tl.dot`;
     - (iii) **Redundant 5D `[NK, B, T, H, ...]` Allocations & Global `.sum(0)` Reductions + Duplicate Backward `fwd_h` / `wy_fwd` Recomputations**: `chunk_oja_bwd_dA`, `chunk_oja_bwd_dqk`, `chunk_oja2_bwd_kkt`, and `chunk_gdn2_bwd_intra` allocate `[NK, B, T, H, ...]` scratch buffers and launch separate `.sum(0)` reduction kernels, while both `chunk_oja2_bwd` and `chunk_gdn2_bwd` (by default) recompute `wy_fwd` and `fwd_h` from scratch;
     - (iv) **`GSA-2` `packed_dense` Cold-Autotune Explosion (`> 100 s`) & 34-Launch Unfused Two-Level Periphery**: `GatedSlotAttention2.forward` forcibly reshapes dense batches (`B > 1`) into `IS_VARLEN=True` (`[1, B*T, D]` with `cu_seqlens`), triggering a `> 100 s` cold autotune storm and launching 4 separate `ShortConvolution` calls (`q, k, v, w`), separate FP32 `w / (w.norm(p=2) + eps)`, separate `_compute_oja_decay`, 4 `.sigmoid()` launches (`b1, c1, b2, w2`), and separate inter-level `SiLU`; and
     - (v) **Missing Hierarchical Two-Level Context Parallelism (`GSA2-CP = 8`)**: upstream `fla/ops/cp` supports single-level `GDN` state handoff but has zero Context Parallel (`CP`) support for `Oja-2` or the coupled two-level `GSA-2` state $(S_t^{(1)} \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times M}, S_t^{(2)} \in \mathbb{R}^{D_2 \times V})$.
   - Round-20 RSI resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Span-Checked Midpoint-Anchored `16x16` Sub-Chunk Tensor Core `tl.dot`**: for each $16\times 16$ diagonal sub-chunk $[i_{\text{ti}}, i_{\text{last}}]$, anchoring to the midpoint $g_{\text{mid}} = \frac{1}{2}(g_{i_{\text{ti}}} + g_{i_{\text{last}}})$ bounds every exponent $|g_r - g_{\text{mid}}| \le \delta = \frac{1}{2}(g_{i_{\text{ti}}} - g_{i_{\text{last}}})$ (`\le 37.5` in Oja-2 where $g_t^{(v)} \in [-5, 0]$), replacing all 5 scalar `j`-loops with a single $16\times 16$ Tensor Core `tl.dot` (with an automatic per-tile span check $\max_d \delta_d \le 60$ falling back to causal pairwise evaluation for extreme unbounded decay);
     - (b) **Two-Pass State-Split WY Adjoints (`_gdn2_bwd_wy_dA_dv_dw_kernel` + `_gdn2_bwd_wy_dq_dk_db_dg_kernel` and `_oja2_bwd_wy_dA_dv_db_dgv_kernel` + `_oja2_bwd_wy_dk_dc_kernel`) + State-Invariant `BK * M = 4096` Oja-2 Recurrence (`BK = 4096 // M`)**: keeping the per-CTA FP32 state register footprint strictly invariant at $4,096$ floats (`32` regs/thread at `num_warps=4`) across $M \in \{64, 128, 256\}$ (`BK = 64, 32, 16`) so every single `GDN-2`, `Oja-2`, and `GSA-2` kernel compiles with **`0` SM80 cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`)**; and
     - (c) **In-Kernel $K$/$M$-Reduced `dA`/`db`/`Aqk` + Saved Forward States + Superfused `GDN2BlockRSI` & `GSA2BlockRSI` (`Stage A0..A4`)** preserving the exact upstream numerical contracts (`w / (w.norm(p=2) + eps)`, optional `slot_proj` when $M \ne D_2$).
2. **Exact `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `FP32` and `BF16` forward/state/gradient parity against independent `FP64` recurrent oracles for both `GDN-2` (`o, S_T, dq, dk, dv, db, dw, dg, dS0`) and `Oja-2` / `GSA-2` (`o, S_T, dq, dk, dv, db, dc, dgv, dS0` across $M \in \{64, 128, 256\}$ and non-chunk-multiple $T = 137$), and kill `3/3` negative-control mutations (`collapse_gdn2_b_eq_w`, `omit_oja2_erase_b_gate`, `corrupt_gsa2_slot_l2norm_contract`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Kernel Matrix (`GDN-2` & `Oja-2` Across $K, V, M \in \{64, 128, 256\}$) & 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_gdn2_rsi` vs `fla.ops.gdn2.chunk_gdn2` and `chunk_gated_oja_rule2_rsi` vs `fla.ops.gated_oja_rule2.chunk_gated_oja_rule2` across $M \in \{64, 128, 256\}$ and $T \in \{2048, 4096\}$, and demonstrate a strictly monotonic 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for both `GDN2BlockRSI` and `GSA2BlockRSI` (`M=128` and `M=256`).
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock (`val_bpb`) Autoresearch Language Modeling Loop (`Track 4`)**:
   - Under an identical `15.0s` wall-clock training budget on a 4-layer hierarchical `GSA-2` language model, compare `Stage A0` (upstream `GatedSlotAttention2` baseline), `Stage A1` (native dense batching without `packed_dense` varlen detour), and `Stage A4` (full Round-20 RSI zero-spill superfused `GSA2BlockRSI`), measuring completed optimizer steps, tokens/sec, and final validation bits-per-byte (`val_bpb`).
5. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`GDN2-CP = 8` & Hierarchical Two-Level `GSA2-CP = 8`, `Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{16384, 32768\}, H = 16$), benchmark Zero-Copy `B=1` Head-Partitioned Ulysses `GSA2-CP = 8` (jointly executing Level-1 `Oja-2` state $S_t^{(1)} \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times M}$ + inter-level readout activation/projection + Level-2 `GDN-2` state $S_t^{(2)} \in \mathbb{R}^{D_2 \times V}$ on $H_{\text{local}} = 2$ heads/GPU in 4 NVSwitch `all_to_all_single` collectives) against 8-GPU Sequential Dual-State Ring (`GSA2-Ring-CP = 8`) and 1x A100 full-sequence execution, verifying `0.000e+00` relative $L_2$ parity.

## 24. Round-21 RSI — E147: `Gated DeltaProduct (GDP): Multi-Householder Product Linear Recurrences for Rotational & Parity State Tracking` (`arXiv:2502.10297`, Siems et al., `fla/ops/gated_delta_product` & `fla/layers/gated_deltaproduct.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Gated DeltaProduct (`GDP`, `arXiv:2502.10297`, Siems, Carstensen, Zela, Hutter, Pontil, & Grazzi; official repository `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/gated_delta_product` & `fla/layers/gated_deltaproduct.py`)**, where each token $t \in \{0, \dots, T-1\}$ first decays the recurrent state $S \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ by $\exp(g_t)$ and then applies $N_h = \text{num\_householder} \in \{1, 2, 3, 4\}$ sequential generalized Householder updates before a single end-of-token query readout:
$$S_{t, 0} = \exp(g_t) S_{t-1, N_h}, \qquad S_{t, j} = \left(I_K - \beta_{t,j} k_{t,j} k_{t,j}^\top\right) S_{t, j-1} + \beta_{t,j} k_{t,j} v_{t,j}^\top \quad (j = 1, \dots, N_h), \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot S_{t, N_h}^\top q_t,$$
five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, rectangular query-compacted Tensor Core, superfused layer periphery, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/gated_delta_product` & `fla/layers/gated_deltaproduct.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-21 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/gated_delta_product` exhibits five concrete SM80 hardware, memory, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Unimplemented Native Backward (`# TODO: optimize the backward pass like the forward pass` in `chunk.py:204` — $N_h \times$ Zero-Padded Backward Expansion & Full Forward Recomputation)**: `ChunkGatedDeltaProductFunction.backward` allocates `q_new` (`[B, T, N_h, H, K]`) and `do_new` (`[B, T, N_h, H, V]`) filled with $\frac{N_h - 1}{N_h}$ literal zeros (`50%` zeros at $N_h=2$, `66.7%` at $N_h=3$, `75%` at $N_h=4$), flattens them to length $T \cdot N_h$, calls `chunk_gated_delta_rule_bwd` which recomputes `w, u = recompute_w_u_fwd` and `h, v_new = chunk_gated_delta_rule_fwd_h` from scratch in backward, executes full `[64, 64]` Tensor Core MMAs across the zero-padded `q_new` and `do_new` rows, and discards $\frac{N_h - 1}{N_h}$ of `dq` and `dg` via `.contiguous()` slicing;
     - (ii) **Redundant Forward `g_interleaved` HBM Expansion & Dual Cumsum (`chunk.py:42-60`)**: `chunk_gated_delta_product_fwd` allocates `g_interleaved` (`[B, T * N_h, H]`, $\frac{N_h - 1}{N_h}$ zeros) and runs `chunk_local_cumsum` twice, while `chunk_fwd_kernel_o` (`chunk_deltaproduct_o.py:109-123`) loops `for i_dp in range(num_householder):` over `[64, 64]` square attention tiles, reloading `b_q` from HBM $N_h + 1$ times and performing $N_h \times$ redundant intra-chunk readout FLOPs;
     - (iii) **SM80 Cubin Register Spills (`10` Spilling Specializations, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 46`, `total_spills = 136`)**: `chunk_fwd_kernel_o` spills up to `46` registers/thread (`n_regs = 255`), `chunk_bwd_kernel_dv` spills up to `22` registers/thread (`n_regs = 255`), `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` spills `12` registers/thread (`n_regs = 255`), and `chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg` spills `6` registers/thread (`n_regs = 255, shared = 98,320 B`);
     - (iv) **`AssertionError` on `torch.float32` (`chunk.py:310`), `TF32` Precision Truncation (`rel_l2 = 1.42e-3..2.33e-3`), & Unfused Layer Periphery (`layers/gated_deltaproduct.py`)**: `chunk_gated_delta_product` rejects `torch.float32` inputs, truncates `FP32` dots to `TF32`, and launches 3 separate `ShortConvolution` calls (`q_conv1d`, `k_conv1d` of width $N_h D_K$, `v_conv1d` of width $N_h D_V$), separate `l2norm`, and separate `allow_neg_eigval` $\beta \in (0, 2)$ and Mamba-decay `g` elementwise kernels; and
     - (v) **Restrictive Context Parallelism (`chunk.py:318-323`)**: upstream `cp_context` forbids `initial_state` and `output_final_state` and serializes state handoff across ranks.
   - Round-21 RSI resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Rectangular Query-Compacted `[B_Q, B_dp]` Chunking ($B_Q = 64 // N_h$ for $N_h \in \{1, 2, 4\}$ and $B_Q = 16, B_{\text{dp}} = 48$ for $N_h = 3$)**: aligning every chunk boundary with an exact integer number $B_Q$ of real tokens guarantees that the cumulative log-decay at virtual step $r = m \cdot N_h + j \in [0, B_{\text{dp}})$ is identically $\bar{g}_m = \bar{g}_{\lfloor r / N_h \rfloor}$ (eliminating `g_interleaved` and the second cumsum) and compacts intra-chunk readout and backward query/output tiles from `[64, 64]` to rectangular `[B_Q, 64]` (`2.0x`–`4.0x` fewer query-side FLOPs and `0` zero-padded `q_new`/`do_new` allocations);
     - (b) **Saved `(w, v_new, h)` Forward States & 3-Pass Split Zero-Spill WY Backward**: eliminating all backward `recompute_w_u_fwd` and `chunk_fwd_h` recomputations and keeping all Round-21 RSI Triton kernels strictly at **`0` SM80 cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`)** across $N_h \in \{1, 2, 3, 4\}$ and $V \in \{128, 256\}$; and
     - (c) **Superfused `GatedDeltaProductBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`)**: fusing `ShortConv1d(W=4)+SiLU`, vectorized `BR=32` `L2Norm`, fused `(beta, g)` gate activation (`allow_neg_eigval` $\beta = 2 \sigma(b)$ + $g = -\exp(A_{\log})\operatorname{softplus}(a + dt_{\text{bias}})$), and `FusedRMSNormGated`.
2. **Exact `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `FP32` and `BF16` forward/state/gradient parity (`o, ht, dq, dk, dv, dg, dbeta, dh0`) against the exact `FP64` recurrent oracle across $N_h \in \{1, 2, 3, 4\}$ and non-chunk-multiple $T = 137$, and kill `3/3` negative-control mutations (`truncate_householder_product_nh1`, `clamp_beta_no_neg_eigval`, `drop_inter_householder_wy_coupling`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Kernel Matrix Across $N_h \in \{2, 3, 4\}, V \in \{128, 256\}, T \in \{2048, 4096\}$ & 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_gated_delta_product_rsi` vs `fla.ops.gated_delta_product.chunk_gated_delta_product` across $N_h \in \{2, 3, 4\}$, $V \in \{128, 256\}$, and $T \in \{2048, 4096\}$, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `GatedDeltaProductBlockRSI` across $N_h \in \{2, 3, 4\}$.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock (`val_bpb`) Autoresearch Loop (`Track 4`)**:
   - Under an identical `30.0s` wall-clock training budget on a multi-layer `GatedDeltaProduct` language model ($N_h = 3$, `allow_neg_eigval=True`), compare `Stage A0` (upstream `GatedDeltaProduct` baseline) vs `Stage A4` (Round-21 RSI zero-spill rectangular `GatedDeltaProductBlockRSI`), measuring optimizer steps, tokens/sec, and final validation bits-per-byte (`val_bpb`).
5. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`GDP-CP = 8`, `Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{16384, 32768, 65536\}$, up to **`262,144` virtual Householder steps** at $N_h = 4$), benchmark Zero-Copy Head-Partitioned Ulysses `GDP-CP = 8` against 8-GPU Sequential State-Passing Ring (`GDP-Ring-CP = 8`), verifying `0.0` max absolute difference vs single-GPU execution.

## 25. Round-22 RSI — E148: `Cyclic Flow Attention (CyFA): Linear Sequence Modeling with Relative-Time-Partitioned Memory` (`arXiv:2609.36259`, Yixiao Chen / FLA Team, `fla/ops/cyfa` & `fla/layers/cyfa.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Cyclic Flow Attention (`CyFA: Linear Sequence Modeling with Relative-Time-Partitioned Memory`, `arXiv:2609.36259`, Yixiao Chen et al.; official repositories `Chyxx/CyclicFlowAttention` and `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/cyfa` & `fla/layers/cyfa.py`)**, which maintains aligned key and value memories $H_t^K \in \mathbb{R}^{D_k \times M}$ and $H_t^V \in \mathbb{R}^{M \times D_v}$ over $M \in \{64, 128\}$ relative-time slots (`period` $C = M - 1$ active Fourier coordinates) transported by a learned monotonic clock $\lambda_t = \lambda_0 + \sum_{s \le t} \delta_s$ ($\delta_t = \sigma(W_d \operatorname{detach}(x_t)) \in (0, 1)^H$) on the cardinal Fourier orbit $\phi_{2p}(\lambda) = \alpha_p \cos(\omega_p \lambda), \phi_{2p+1}(\lambda) = -\alpha_p \sin(\omega_p \lambda)$ ($\omega_p = 2\pi p / (M - 1)$, $w_t = \beta_t \phi(\lambda_t) \in \mathbb{R}^M$), executing a two-pass scalar-decay recurrence coupled by an $SO(2)^{M/2}$ modal rotation + Fourier-basis softmax readout + inverse modal rotation:
$$\begin{aligned}
x_t &= \operatorname{GLA}\!\left(\hat{q}_t, \hat{k}_t, w_t, g_t; H_0^K\right) \in \mathbb{R}^M, \qquad z_t = R(+\lambda_t) x_t, \qquad p_t = \operatorname{softmax}\!\left(z_t W_{\text{table}}^\top\right) \in \Delta^{C-1}, \\
u_t &= p_t W_{\text{table}} \in \mathbb{R}^M, \qquad y_t = R(-\lambda_t) u_t \in \mathbb{R}^M, \qquad o_t = \operatorname{GLA}\!\left(y_t, w_t, v_t, g_t; H_0^V\right) \in \mathbb{R}^{D_v},
\end{aligned}$$
where $W_{\text{table}} = W_{\text{readout}} B_{\text{Fourier}} \in \mathbb{R}^{H \times C \times M}$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, strip-tiled cyclic readout, superfused layer periphery, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/cyfa` & `fla/layers/cyfa.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-22 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/cyfa` (`chunk.py`, `qk_norm.py`, `utils.py`) and `fla/layers/cyfa.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, memory, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Catastrophic SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`69` Spilling Specializations, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 4,932`, `total_spills = 58,354`, up to `147,456` B SMEM)**: `_chunk_cyfa_bwd_dqk_kernel` spills across `19/21` specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 4,932, sum_spills = 20,614, max_smem = 131,104` B) and issues cross-block global `tl.atomic_add` onto `dg_force`; `_chunk_cyfa_fused_readout_bwd_kernel` spills across `24/28` specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 4,248, sum_spills = 31,638, max_smem = 147,456` B) because it holds the entire `[128, 128]` `FP32` readout table `w` (`64 KiB`) in registers alongside 14 `[BT, 64..128]` modal tiles (`raw_even, raw_odd, dw_even, dw_odd, raw_z, dw_z, scores, dp, p, dl, coeff, grad, c, s`); `_chunk_cyfa_fused_readout_fwd_kernel` spills across `2/2` specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 62, sum_spills = 64, max_smem = 143,872` B); `_chunk_cyfa_readout_table_grad_kernel` spills across `19/37` specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 1,440, sum_spills = 5,918, max_smem = 82,944` B); `_chunk_cyfa_write_bwd_kernel` (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 90`), `common.chunk_o.chunk_bwd_kernel_dv` (`4/12` spilling, `max_regs = 255, sum_spills = 30, max_smem = 147,456` B), `_chunk_cyfa_qk_norm_bwd_kernel` (`max_regs = 255`), `chunk_fwd_kernel_h` (`max_regs = 181`), and `chunk_fwd_kernel_o` (`max_regs = 192`) all exceed the SM80 `168`-register multi-CTA occupancy threshold;
     - (ii) **Disabled Fused `dqk/dv` on SM80 (`USE_FUSED_DQK_DV = False` in `chunk.py:60`) & Redundant Forward Recomputations in Backward (`4x chunk_fwd_h`, `2x qk_rmsnorm_fwd`, `2x _point_write`)**: upstream `ChunkCyFAFunction.backward` discards normalized `q`, `write`, `h_k`, and `h_v` even when `checkpoint_level == 0`, forcing backward to re-run `qk_rmsnorm_fwd`, re-run `_point_write`, and re-run `chunk_fwd_h` twice from scratch (`4` total `chunk_fwd_h` launches per training step);
     - (iii) **Redundant Forward Scan Launches (`chunk_local_cumsum` + `cyfa_clock_cumsum` + `add_segment_initial`) & Duplicate Reverse `dg` Cumsum**: upstream launches separate `[B, T, H]` scans for `g` and `delta` in forward and separate `dg_force` atomic accumulations in backward;
     - (iv) **`TF32` Truncation in `FP32` Mode (`o rel_l2 = 4.68e-3, dg rel_l2 = 7.94e-3`)**: upstream omits `input_precision='ieee'` in `FP32` mode; and
     - (v) **Unfused `CyclicFlowAttention` Layer Periphery (`layers/cyfa.py`) & Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`CyFA-CP = 8`)**: `CyclicFlowAttention.forward` launches 3 separate `ShortConvolution` calls (`q_conv1d, k_conv1d, v_conv1d`), separate gate projections (`a_proj, b_proj, d_proj`), eager elementwise `softplus`/`exp`/`sigmoid` chains, and an extra `[B, T, H, V]` tensor allocation for `o * self.num_readout_slots` prior to `self.o_norm`, with no Context Parallel (`CP`) support for the 3-tuple state `(hk, hv, lambda)`.
   - Round-22 RSI resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Strip-Tiled Zero-Spill Cyclic Modal Readout Forward & 2-Pass Backward (`_cyfa_modal_readout_fwd_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_readout_bwd_pass1_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_readout_bwd_pass2_rsi_kernel`)**: streaming $W_{\text{table}} \in \mathbb{R}^{H \times C \times M}$ in `BC = 64` (`BF16`) / `BC = 32` (`FP32`) slot strips at `BT = 16..32` eliminates `[128, 128]` register residency, cutting readout forward/backward registers to `79–146 <= 168` with **`0` spills** and slashing SMEM from `147 KiB` to `4–36 KiB`;
     - (b) **Live-Range-Decoupled `BR = 32` Sub-Tiled Recurrence Kernels (`_cyfa_chunk_fwd_h_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_chunk_fwd_o_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_chunk_bwd_dh_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_chunk_bwd_dqk_rsi_kernel`, `_cyfa_chunk_bwd_dv_rsi_kernel`) + Saved `(q_norm, k_norm, write, h_k, h_v)` Forward States + Atomic-Free Fused Dual `dg` Reverse Cumsum**: decoupling intra-chunk `[BR=32, BT=64]` score accumulation from inter-chunk `[BR=32, BK/BV]` state readout and ordering `dq` and `dk` lifecycles sequentially inside `_cyfa_chunk_bwd_dqk_rsi_kernel` keeps **all `14/14` Round-22 RSI Triton kernels strictly at `0` SM80 register spills (`max_regs <= 168`)** across both `BF16` and `FP32` (`DOT_IEEE = True`), while eliminating `2` backward `chunk_fwd_h` calls, `1` backward `qk_rmsnorm_fwd` call, `1` backward `_point_write` call, and all global `tl.atomic_add` contentions; and
     - (c) **Superfused `CyclicFlowAttentionBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`)**: combining fused QKV `ShortConvolution`, fused `(a, b)` gate GEMM + fused `(g_cumsum, lambdas, beta, delta)` gate-and-clock scan, cached `W_table` readout basis, and fused post-scale `o_norm(o * num_readout_slots, g_proj)`.
2. **Exact `FP32`/`FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-4` (`~1e-5` with `DOT_IEEE = True` vs upstream `7.94e-3`) and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 2e-2` across all outputs, 3-tuple final states, and 9 parameter/input gradients (`o, hk, hv, fl, dq, dk, dv, dg, ddelta, dbeta, dreadout, dqw, dkw`) against `naive_recurrent_cyfa` across $M \in \{64, 128\}$, $K, V \in \{128, 256\}$, and non-chunk-multiple $T = 137$, and kill `3/3` negative-control mutations (`freeze_cyclic_clock_zero_delta`, `omit_inverse_modal_rotation`, `drop_pass1_key_memory_dg`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **12-Shape Kernel Matrix Across $M \in \{64, 128\}, K, V \in \{128, 256\}, T \in \{1024, 2048, 4096, 8192\}$ & 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_cyfa_rsi` vs `fla.ops.cyfa.chunk.chunk_cyfa` across 12 shapes spanning $M \in \{64, 128\}$, $K, V \in \{128, 256\}$, and $T \in \{1024, 2048, 4096, 8192\}$, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `CyclicFlowAttentionBlockRSI` across $(M=64, D=128)$, $(M=128, D=128)$, and $(M=128, D=256)$.
4. **Fixed-Wall-Clock (`val_bpb`) Autoresearch Loop (`Track 4`)**:
   - Under an identical `30.0s` wall-clock training budget on a 4-layer `CyclicFlowAttention` language model ($M = 128, D = 256$), compare `Stage A0` (upstream `CyclicFlowAttention` baseline) vs `Stage A4` (Round-22 RSI zero-spill superfused `CyclicFlowAttentionBlockRSI`), measuring completed optimizer steps, tokens/sec, and final validation bits-per-byte (`val_bpb`).
5. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`CyFA-CP = 8`, `Track 5`)**:

## 26. Round-23 RSI — E149: `Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention` (`arXiv:2605.29157`, Yifei Zuo et al., `fla/ops/parallax` & `fla/layers/parallax.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention (`arXiv:2605.29157`, Yifei Zuo et al.; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/parallax` & `fla/layers/parallax.py`)**, which pairs primary queries $q_i \in \mathbb{R}^D$ (`scale` $\gamma = D^{-1/2}$) with a learned secondary query stream $r_i \in \mathbb{R}^D$ over causal / sliding-window keys and values $(k_j, v_j) \in \mathbb{R}^D \times \mathbb{R}^D$ ($0 \le i - j < W$) to inject a first-order local linear correction onto the softmax-weighted values:
$$\begin{aligned}
p_{ij} &= \frac{\exp(\gamma q_i^\top k_j)}{\sum_{l \in \mathcal{V}(i)} \exp(\gamma q_i^\top k_l)}, \qquad \bar{v}_i = \sum_{j \in \mathcal{V}(i)} p_{ij} v_j = \frac{O^{(1)}_i}{d^{(1)}_i}, \qquad \bar{t}_i = \sum_{j \in \mathcal{V}(i)} p_{ij} (r_i^\top k_j) = \frac{d^{(2)}_i}{d^{(1)}_i}, \\
o_i &= \sum_{j \in \mathcal{V}(i)} p_{ij} \Bigl(1 + \bar{t}_i - r_i^\top k_j\Bigr) v_j = \bar{v}_i \bigl(1 + \bar{t}_i\bigr) - \frac{O^{(2)}_i}{d^{(1)}_i},
\end{aligned}$$
where backward requires coupled first-order corrections $\delta^{(t)}_i = \nabla o_i^\top o_i$, $\delta^{(b)}_i = \nabla o_i^\top \bar{v}_i$, $a_{ij} = \nabla o_i^\top v_j$, $\Delta_{ij} = a_{ij} - \delta^{(b)}_i$, $g^{(L)}_{ij} = p_{ij} \bigl(a_{ij} - \delta^{(t)}_i + (\bar{t}_i - r_i^\top k_j)\Delta_{ij}\bigr)$, and $g^{(U)}_{ij} = -p_{ij} \Delta_{ij}$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, GQA-packed Tensor Core contraction, superfused layer periphery, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/parallax` & `fla/layers/parallax.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-23 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:

     - (i) **255-Register Saturation & Spills Across Training & Decode Kernels (`4/6` Kernels Saturating at `253–255` Registers with `2..22` Spills, and `decode_one_step` at `182` Registers)**: `parallel_parallax_fwd_kernel`, `parallel_parallax_bwd_kernel_dqr`, `parallel_parallax_bwd_kernel_dkv`, and `parallax_decode_kernel` hardcode `[BT=64, BS=64, num_warps=8, num_stages=2]` while holding two query tiles (`b_q, b_r`), two `FP32` output/gradient accumulators (`barv_acc, Rv_acc` or `grad_q_acc, grad_r_acc` or `grad_k_acc, grad_v_acc`), both `b_k` and `b_v` simultaneously, and 3 duplicated loop phases (`Phase 0, Phase A, Phase B` in `fwd`/`dqr`; `Phase A, Phase B, Phase C` in `dkv`), hitting `n_regs = 253–255` with up to `22` register spills/thread (`91,392` B SMEM), while `parallax_decode_one_step_kernel` hits `n_regs = 182 > 168`;
     - (ii) **GQA Redundant HBM Loads (`G`-Way `K, V` Reloads), Separate `bwd_preprocess` Kernel, & Two `einops.reduce` Launches**: under Grouped-Query Attention ($G = H_Q / H > 1$), `parallel_parallax_fwd` and `parallel_parallax_bwd_kernel_dqr` launch separate single-head CTAs that reload identical `K, V` tiles $G$ times from HBM, `parallel_parallax_bwd` launches a separate `parallel_parallax_bwd_kernel_preprocess` kernel (re-reading `grad_o`, `o`, and `barv` from HBM), and `parallel_parallax_bwd_kernel_dkv` writes $G\times$ inflated `[B, T, HQ, D]` `grad_k_buf` and `grad_v_buf` buffers followed by two separate `einops.reduce` kernels;
     - (iii) **`TypeError` Crash on `torch.float32` Inputs (`parallel.py:769`)**: `parallel_parallax` raises `TypeError: parallel_parallax requires bf16 or fp16 inputs` on `torch.float32` tensors;
     - (iv) **Duplicate `self.rotary(r, r)` Bug & Unfused Periphery in `layers/parallax.py` (`14` Periphery Kernels per Step)**: `Parallax.forward` launches 4 separate `q_proj, r_proj, k_proj, v_proj` linear GEMMs, 3 separate `RMSNorm` calls (`q_norm, r_norm, k_norm`), and 2 `RotaryEmbedding` calls where `r, _ = self.rotary(r, r)` rotates `r` **twice** in forward and backpropagates through the discarded second output `_` in backward (`7` `rotary_embedding_kernel` calls + `6` `layer_norm` calls + `1` gradient-add kernel per Fwd+Bwd step); and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`Parallax-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism or associative online-softmax moment merger for `Parallax`'s coupled 5-tuple state $(m, d_1, d_2, O_1, O_2)$.
   - Round-23 RSI resolves all five via:

2. **Exact `FP32` `naive_parallax` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 2`)**:
