## 27. Round-24 RSI — E150: `Wall Attention: Length Generalization With Diagonal Gates` (`Tilde Research`, Timor Averbuch & Dhruv Pai, `https://blog.tilderesearch.com/blog/wall-attn`, `fla/ops/wall_attn` & `fla/layers/wall_attn.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Wall Attention: Length Generalization With Diagonal Gates (`Tilde Research`, Timor Averbuch & Dhruv Pai, June 2026, `https://blog.tilderesearch.com/blog/wall-attn`; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/wall_attn`, `fla/layers/wall_attn.py`, & `fla/models/wall_transformer`)**, which augments causal / sliding-window Grouped-Query Attention ($G = H_Q / H$) with a learned per-channel log-decay gate $g_{t,n} = \log\sigma(W_g x_t) \le 0$ (`[B, T, HQ, K]`), an optional FoX-style per-head scalar log-decay gate $g_{\text{scalar}, t} = \log\sigma(W_{gs} x_t) \le 0$ (`[B, T, HQ]`), and an optional sink bias $b_{\text{sink}} \in \mathbb{R}^{H_Q}$:
$$\begin{aligned}
P_{i,n} &= \frac{1}{\ln 2} \sum_{t=0}^{i} g_{t,n}, \qquad c_i = \frac{1}{\ln 2} \sum_{t=0}^{i} g_{\text{scalar}, t}, \\
s_{ij} &= \frac{\gamma}{\ln 2} \sum_{n=1}^{K} q_{i,n} k_{j,n} 2^{P_{i,n} - P_{j,n}} + (c_i - c_j) = \frac{\gamma}{\ln 2} \sum_{n=1}^{K} \underbrace{\left(q_{i,n} 2^{P_{i,n} - R_n}\right)}_{\tilde{q}_{i,n}(R)} \underbrace{\left(k_{j,n} 2^{R_n - P_{j,n}}\right)}_{\tilde{k}_{j,n}(R)} + (c_i - c_j), \\
p_{ij} &= \frac{2^{s_{ij} - m_i}}{\sum_{l \in \mathcal{V}(i)} 2^{s_{il} - m_i} + 2^{b_{\text{sink}}/\ln 2 - m_i}}, \qquad o_i = \sum_{j \in \mathcal{V}(i)} p_{ij} v_j,
\end{aligned}$$
where backward uses the exact structural log-space identities $\mathrm{d}P^{(q)}_i = (\ln 2) \, q_i \odot \mathrm{d}q_i$, $\mathrm{d}P^{(k)}_j = -(\ln 2) \, k_j \odot \mathrm{d}k_j$, and $\mathrm{d}g_t = \frac{1}{\ln 2} \sum_{i \ge t} (\mathrm{d}P^{(q)}_i + \mathrm{d}P^{(k)}_i)$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, GQA-aware backward & decode cache, superfused layer periphery, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/wall_attn` & `fla/layers/wall_attn.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-24 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/wall_attn` (`parallel.py`, `decode.py`, `naive.py`) and `fla/layers/wall_attn.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, memory, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Catastrophic SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`961 / 1,009` = `95.2%` Spilling Specializations, `max_regs = 255`, `max_stack = 36,808 B/thread`, `5.27 MB` Total Stack, & `71` Specializations Exceeding SM80's `166,912` B Shared Memory Limit up to `295,424` B)**: `parallel_wall_attn_fwd_kernel` spills across `340/360` (`94.4%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_stack = 25,144 B, total_stack = 1,226,328 B, max_smem = 295,424 B`), `parallel_wall_attn_bwd_kernel_dq` spills across `325/325` (`100.0%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_stack = 30,168 B, total_stack = 1,725,232 B, max_smem = 246,272 B`), and `parallel_wall_attn_bwd_kernel_dkv` spills across `296/296` (`100.0%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_stack = 36,808 B, total_stack = 2,315,000 B, max_smem = 199,168 B`), while `parallel_wall_attn_decode_kernel` hits `255` registers (`4/24` configs `> 168` regs). Because `bwd_dq` holds two `[BT, BK]` `FP32` accumulators (`b_dq_til` and `b_dq_diag`) plus `[BT, BK]` `b_pq` and executes `FP32` (`TF32`) `tl.dot` in the diagonal loop, `K = 128` backward latency balloons to `13.503 ms` (`6.76x` slower than `K = 64` `1.997 ms`);
     - (ii) **Broken Autograd in Upstream `naive_wall_attn` (`g.grad = None, g_scalar.grad = None`) & `FP32` `TF32` Truncation Drift (`dg rel_l2 = 2.49e-3`)**: `fla/ops/wall_attn/naive.py:38,65` calls non-differentiable `chunk_global_cumsum(g)` inside `naive_wall_attn`, silently returning `None` for `g.grad` and `g_scalar.grad`, while `parallel_wall_attn` uses default `TF32` `tl.dot` on `FP32` inputs (`2.49e-3` error on `dg`, `1.78e-3` on `dq`/`dk`);
     - (iii) **Unfused Eager `build_wall_kv_cache` (`1.374 ms` vs `0.220 ms` Decode = `86.2%` of Decode Total Time) & Separate `bwd_preprocess` / `einops.reduce` / `dg_cumsum` Launches**: `build_wall_kv_cache` (`decode.py:252-298`) runs 7 eager PyTorch ops (`k.repeat_interleave(G, dim=2)`, `arange`, `clamp`, `g_cumsum[:, chunk_starts]`, `r_cache[:, token_chunk]`, `exp2`, `.to(out_dtype).contiguous()`), taking `6.25x` longer than `parallel_wall_attn_decode_kernel` itself; meanwhile `parallel_wall_attn_bwd` launches a separate `bwd_preprocess` kernel, writes $G\times$ inflated `[B, T, HQ, K/V]` `dk, dv` buffers, runs two `einops.reduce` kernels, and launches separate `chunk_global_cumsum` kernels;
     - (iv) **Unfused `WallAttention.forward` Periphery (`layers/wall_attn.py`)**: launches `4–5` separate linear projections (`q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, g_proj[, gs_proj]`), separate `F.logsigmoid(g.float())` elementwise kernels, 2 separate `GroupNorm` (`q_norm, k_norm`) calls, and separate `o_gate_proj(hidden_states).sigmoid() * o` elementwise multiplication; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`Wall-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism for `WallAttention`'s coupled `[B, T, HQ, K]` channel prefix sum $P$ and online-softmax LSE state $(m, d, O)$.
   - Round-24 RSI resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Anchor-Consistent Unified Single-Loop `BT = 16` Frame (`R_local == R`) + Single `[BT=16, BK]` `b_dq_til` Accumulator + `uint32` Causal/Window Mask**: tiling queries at `BT = 16` (`BS = 32`) makes the diagonal sub-block start `i_s = i_t * 16` coincide identically with the chunk anchor `R = P[i_t * 16]`, eliminating the entire second unrolled diagonal loop in `fwd` and `bwd_dq`, eliminating the second `[BT, BK]` `FP32` accumulator `b_dq_diag`, freeing `b_pq` before the loop, and exploiting `BF16`'s full 8-bit exponent range (`[-126, 127]`, where $2^{110} \ll 2^{127}$ never overflows) so **all `10/10` Round-24 RSI Triton kernels compile with `0` SM80 register spills (`max_regs <= 168`, `max_smem <= 49,152` B)**;

3. **12-Shape Kernel Matrix Across Training (`K, V \in \{64, 128\}, G \in \{1, 4\}, W \in \{512, 1024, \text{None}\}, T \in \{2048, 4096, 8192\}`) & Decode (`build_wall_kv_cache + parallel_wall_attn_decode`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:

## 28. Round-25 RSI — E151: `DeltaFormer: Understanding Transformer from the Perspective of Associative Memory` (`arXiv:2505.19488`, `fla/ops/deltaformer` & `fla/layers/deltaformer.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **DeltaFormer: Understanding Transformer from the Perspective of Associative Memory (`arXiv:2505.19488`, official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/deltaformer` & `fla/layers/deltaformer.py`)**, which reformulates causal attention as a two-stage associative-memory process — Stage 1 solving a global unit-lower-triangular softmax value-decorrelation recurrence $(I + \operatorname{diag}(\beta) P^{(1)}) U = V$ where $P^{(1)}_{t, s} = \frac{\exp(q_t^\top k_s / \sqrt{D})}{\sum_{r < t} \exp(q_t^\top k_r / \sqrt{D})}$ ($s < t$, with $u_0 = v_0$), and Stage 2 applying causal softmax attention $O = \operatorname{CausalSoftmax}(Q K^\top / \sqrt{D}) U$ over the decorrelated values $U$ — five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, Tensor-Core triangular-solver, GQA + single-pass cached decode, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/deltaformer` & `fla/layers/deltaformer.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-25 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/deltaformer` (`parallel.py`, `invcum.py`, `naive.py`) and `fla/layers/deltaformer.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and correctness pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **`torch.linalg.solve_triangular` (`batch_trsm_left_kernel` / `magma_sgemmEx`) Bottleneck (`68.8%` of GPU Time) + Serial Python `for b in range(B)` Loop + Transpose/Copy Storm (`85.5%` of Stage-1 Runtime)**: `ParallelDeltaformerFunction` (`parallel.py:339, 402`) loops serially in Python over batch index `for b in range(B)`, launching single-batch Triton kernels `(C / BLOCK_C, H)` instead of `(C / BLOCK_C, B * H)`, and for every `(b, chunk)` in forward and backward materializes `[512, H, 512]` `w`, runs a separate `w = w * betai.unsqueeze(-1)` kernel, zero-pads and copies `w` into `ws`, calls `.transpose(0, 1).contiguous()` on both `w` and `u` (`160` `aten::copy_` calls = `1.40 ms`), casts to `FP32` (`56` elementwise kernels = `0.61 ms`), and dispatches `torch.linalg.solve_triangular` (`128` `batch_trsm_left_kernel` + `80` `ampere_sgemm` + `32` `magma_sgemmEx` + `224` `offsetPointerArray_kernel` calls = `7.42 ms` out of `11.03 ms` at $B=2, T=2048, H=16, D=64$);
     - (ii) **SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`24 / 104` Spilling Specializations, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 274`, `1,606` Total Spills, `65 / 104` Specs `> 168` Regs, up to `131,072` B SMEM)**: `parallel_deltaformer_bwd_kernel_qk` spills up to `274` registers/thread (`6/8` = `75%` spilling specs, `600` total spills, `118,272` B SMEM) because a single monolithic kernel computes both row-wise `grad_q` (`s < t`) and column-wise `grad_k` (`t > s`) sequentially while holding `k_row`, `k_row_true`, `ut`, `nu`, `da`, `daat`, and `acc` live across 3 loops; meanwhile `parallel_deltaformer_fwd_kernel` spills up to `196` registers (`8/32` spilling specs, `712` total spills, plus scanning all `C = 512` intra-chunk columns even when `s > t`), `parallel_deltaformer_bwd_kernel_u` spills up to `114` registers (`5/32` spilling specs, `258` total spills), and `parallel_deltaformer_bwd_kernel_row_sum` spills up to `20` registers (`131,072` B SMEM);
     - (iii) **Out-of-Bounds Unmasked `tl.load(u_blk_ptr)` Memory Bug, `BF16` `row_dot` Truncation Bug, `-1e6` Softmax Bias Bug, & `FP32` `RuntimeError` Crash**: `parallel_deltaformer_bwd_kernel_qk` (`parallel.py:306, 319`) calls `ut = tl.load(u_blk_ptr)` with no mask (reading out-of-bounds GPU memory when $T$ is not a multiple of `BLOCK_C`), truncates `FP32` `row_dot` to `BF16` (`parallel.py:298, 341`) before computing `dp - row_dot`, masks future entries in `fwd_kernel` with `-1e6` instead of `-inf` with `rowsum` initialized to `1.0` (`lse[0] = -999,994.0`), uses `TF32` `tl.dot` on `FP32` inputs (`rel_l2_dq = 1.88e-3`), and crashes in `deltaformer_attn` on `FP32` inputs (`RuntimeError: FlashAttention only support fp16 and bf16 data type`);
     - (iv) **Broken GQA (`AssertionError` when `num_kv_heads < num_heads`), `NotImplementedError` on Cached Decode (`past_key_values`), & Unfused Periphery in `DeltaFormerAttention` (`layers/deltaformer.py`)**: `DeltaFormerAttention.__init__` accepts `num_kv_heads` and claims GQA support in its docstring, but `parallel_deltaformer_chunk_fwd` asserts `H == _H` (`AssertionError` whenever `num_kv_heads != num_heads`), `DeltaFormerAttention.forward` raises `NotImplementedError("DeltaFormerAttention does not support past_key_values")` on cached inference, and launches 4 separate input GEMMs (`q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, b_proj`) + 2 separate `RMSNorm` calls + `RotaryEmbedding`; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`DeltaFormer-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism for `DeltaFormer`'s two-stage causal triangular recurrence + FlashAttention pipeline.
   - Round-25 RSI (`code/deltaformer_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Batched Zero-Spill Tensor-Core Euler-16 Block-Triangular Solver (`_df_tril_solve_fwd_rsi_kernel` & `_df_tril_solve_bwd_rsi_kernel`) + Causal Intra-Chunk Early-Termination + Split `bwd_dq` / `bwd_dk`**: replacing `torch.linalg.solve_triangular` and all `.transpose(0,1).contiguous()` copies with an in-place Triton block-substitution solver (`(I_{16} + L)^{-1} = (I - L)(I + L^2)(I + L^4)(I + L^8)` on diagonal $16 \times 16$ blocks, saving $A_{m,m}^{-1}$ in forward so backward requires zero matrix inversions), terminating intra-chunk causal loops at `min(Ci, (pid_c + 1) * BLOCK_C)`, and splitting `bwd_kernel_qk` into dedicated `tl.make_block_ptr` kernels (`_df_bwd_dq_rsi_kernel`, `_df_bwd_dk_rsi_kernel`, `_df_bwd_reduce_dkv_rsi_kernel`) so **100% of Round-25 RSI Triton kernels compile with `0` SM80 register spills (`max_regs <= 168`)**;
     - (b) **Diagonal-LSE Stage-2 Collapse Identity ($o_t = (1 - \alpha_t)\bar{u}_{<t} + \alpha_t u_t$) & Single-Pass Cached Decode (`deltaformer_decode_rsi`)**: proving that for $s < t$, $P^{(2)}_{t,s} = (1 - \alpha_t) P^{(1)}_{t,s}$ with $\alpha_t = \sigma(q_t^\top k_t / \sqrt{D} - \text{lse}_t \ln 2)$, enabling a single-pass KV-cache decode kernel (`_df_decode_rsi_kernel`) that updates $U_{\text{new}}$ and computes $O_{\text{new}}$ in one pass over $(K_{\text{cache}}, U_{\text{cache}})$ (`2.0x` lower KV-cache HBM traffic); and
     - (c) **GQA-Native `DeltaFormerBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`) & Fused 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `DeltaFormer-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 2e-5` (`>100x` lower error than upstream `1.88e-3` `TF32` Stage-1 error while resolving upstream's `FP32` `RuntimeError` crash) and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1e-2` across `o, u, dq, dk, dv, dbeta` against the differentiable `FP64` `exact_deltaformer_oracle` across $D \in \{64, 128\}$, $G \in \{1, 4\}$, and non-multiple-of-64 $T = 137$, and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_tril_offdiag_substitution`, `M2_truncate_bwd_row_dot_bf16_or_sign`, `M3_corrupt_stage2_diagonal_alpha`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Cached Decode Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `deltaformer_attn_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) across `B=2, T in {1024, 2048, 4096}, HQ=16, HKV in {16, 4}, D in {64, 128}`, benchmark `deltaformer_decode_rsi` (`T_kv in {2048, 4096}, T_q in {1, 16}, D in {64, 128}`) against two-stage eager KV-cache decode, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `DeltaFormerBlockRSI`.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`DeltaFormer-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{16384, 32768\}, H_Q = 16, H_{KV} = 8, D = 64$), benchmark Zero-Copy Fused Ulysses `deltaformer_cp8_ulysses_rsi` (single `all_to_all_single` before Stage 1, running both Stage 1 and Stage 2 locally on full sequence length with zero inter-stage communication, and single `all_to_all_single` after Stage 2) against Sequence-AllGather/Ring (`deltaformer_cp8_ring_baseline`), and run a fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop comparing `Stage A0` vs `Stage A4` at $D_{\text{model}} \in \{1024, 2048\}$.

## 29. Round-26 RSI — E152: `Stick-Breaking Attention` (`arXiv:2410.17980`, ICLR 2025 — `fla/ops/stickbreaking_attn` & `fla/layers/stickbreaking_attn.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Stick-Breaking Attention (`arXiv:2410.17980`, ICLR 2025 — Shawn Tan, Yikang Shen, Songlin Yang, Aaron Courville, Rameswar Panda; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/stickbreaking_attn` & `fla/layers/stickbreaking_attn.py`)**, which replaces softmax + positional encodings with a sequential nearest-to-farthest stick-breaking process where each key $j \le i$ breaks off a fraction $\beta_{i, j} = \sigma(q_i^\top k_j / \sqrt{K})$ of the remaining stick, yielding attention weights $P_{i, j} = \beta_{i, j} \prod_{j < m \le i} (1 - \beta_{i, m}) = 2^{S_{i, j} + \sum_{j < m \le i} \log_2(1 - \beta_{i, m})}$ with $S_{i, j} = \frac{q_i^\top k_j}{\sqrt{K} \ln 2}$ and $\log_2(1 - \beta_{i, m}) = -\operatorname{softplus}_2(S_{i, m}) = -\log_2(1 + 2^{S_{i, m}})$, remaining stick $\text{rem}_i = \prod_{0 \le m \le i} (1 - \beta_{i, m})$, and output $o^{\text{total}}_i = \sum_{j \le i} P_{i, j} v_j + \text{rem}_i v_i$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, single-pass reverse-stick backward, Tensor-Core GQA decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/stickbreaking_attn` & `fla/layers/stickbreaking_attn.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-26 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/stickbreaking_attn` (`parallel.py`, `decoding.py`, `naive.py`) and `fla/layers/stickbreaking_attn.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Severe SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`36 / 114` = `31.58%` Spilling Specializations, `105 / 114` = `92.11%` with `regs > 168`, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 314`, `2,440` Total Spills, up to `139,264 B` Shared Memory, & `50%` of Winning `BF16` Autotune Configs Spilling)**: `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_fwd_kernel` spills across `12/38` (`31.6%`) specializations (`38/38` = `100%` with `regs > 168`, `max_regs = 255, max_spills = 208, total_spills = 1,240, max_smem = 139,264 B`), `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_bwd_kernel_dq` spills across `10/38` (`26.3%`) specializations (`35/38` = `92.1%` with `regs > 168`, `max_regs = 255, max_spills = 112, total_spills = 264, max_smem = 98,560 B`, with `4/8` = `50%` of winning `BF16` autotune configs spilling to local memory), and `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_bwd_kernel_dkv` spills across `14/38` (`36.8%`) specializations (`32/38` = `84.2%` with `regs > 168`, `max_regs = 255, max_spills = 314, total_spills = 936, max_smem = 82,432 B`);
     - (ii) **Redundant Two-Pass Key Scan in `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_bwd_kernel_dq` + 32-Way Uncoalesced `[B, T, HQ, NS]` `log_rem`/`delta_acc` HBM Scatter/Gather + 2-Tuple `tl.associative_scan` + Sequential `for i_g in range(G)` Loop in `bwd_kernel_dkv`**: because upstream `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_fwd_kernel` discards the final log remaining stick $L_i = \log_2(\text{rem}_i)$, `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_bwd_kernel_dq` walks every causal key block **twice** (`Pass 1` nearest-to-farthest to recompute `log_rem` and scatter it to HBM, then `Pass 2` farthest-to-nearest to reload `log_rem`, recompute $S = Q K^\top$ and $-\operatorname{softplus}_2(S)$ a second time, and compute `delta_acc` and `dq`), doubling `bwd_dq`'s HBM key reads, `tl.dot(b_q, b_k)` Tensor-Core MMAs, and SFU ops; furthermore, `log_rem` and `delta_acc` are allocated as `[B, T, HQ, NS]` where adjacent threads `o_q` are strided by `HQ * NS` floats (`4 KB` 32-way uncoalesced HBM scatter/gather), exclusive cumsum is computed via a 2-tuple `tl.associative_scan((0, b_pdp), 1, _add_f32x2)` instead of `tl.cumsum(b_pdp) - b_pdp`, and `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_bwd_kernel_dkv` loops sequentially `for i_g in range(G)` inside each CTA (`grid = (NS, B * H)`);
     - (iii) **`FP32` `TF32` Truncation Drift (`1.42e-3` on `dq, dk`) & Non-Tensor-Core Scalar SIMT `stickbreaking_attn_decoding` (`decoding.py`)**: upstream `parallel_stickbreaking_attn` uses default `TF32` `tl.dot` on `FP32` inputs (`rel_l2 = 1.42e-3` on `dq, dk`), while `stickbreaking_attn_decoding_fwd_kernel` launches 1 CTA per single query `(i_t, i_hq)` (`grid = (TQ, B * HQ)`), computes attention via scalar `tl.sum(b_q[:, None] * b_k, axis=0)` SIMT reductions without Tensor Cores, and reloads the entire KV cache $T_Q \times G$ times per KV head;
     - (iv) **`StickBreakingAttention.forward` Raises `TypeError` on `FP32` Inputs & Launches Unfused 3-GEMM + 2-RMSNorm + Eager Residual Stick Addition Periphery (`layers/stickbreaking_attn.py`)**: `StickBreakingAttention.forward` unconditionally casts `q = q.to(torch.bfloat16)` before calling `self.q_norm(q)`, crashing with `TypeError` whenever the module has `FP32` weights (`norm_fn` raises `TypeError: Input tensor must have the same dtype as weight tensor`), and launches 3 separate linear GEMMs (`q_proj, k_proj, v_proj`), 2 separate `RMSNorm` calls, and eager `o + rem[..., None] * v`; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`StickBreaking-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism for Stick-Breaking Attention's sequential nearest-to-farthest stick-breaking recurrence.
   - Round-26 RSI (`code/stickbreaking_attn_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Reverse-Stick Subtraction Theorem ($\text{log\_rem}(i, i_k) = L_i - \sum_{m=0}^{i_k} B_{i, m}$) + Single-Pass `_sb_bwd_dq_rsi_kernel` + Coalesced `[B * HQ, NS, T]` Snapshot Layout + Inline PTX `_neg_softplus2_ptx` / `_exp2_ptx` + Sub-Block `BS = 16` `dKV` Decomposition at `K = 128`**: saving the 1D total log remaining stick $L_i = \sum_{m=0}^{i_t} B_{i, m}$ (`[B * HQ, T]`) at the end of `_sb_fwd_rsi_kernel` allows `_sb_bwd_dq_rsi_kernel` to walk causal key blocks **only once** (`i_k = 0 .. max_k_blk`, decrementing `b_rem_run` by `sum(b_log_om_beta)`), cutting `bwd_dq`'s HBM key traffic, `Q K^\top` MMAs, and SFU ops in half (`50%` reduction); transposing snapshots to `[B * HQ, NS, T]` makes every `o_q` vector load/store 100% unit-stride coalesced (`stride = 1`); replacing Triton's branchy `_neg_softplus2` with a branchless predicated PTX SFU sequence (`setp.gt.f32` + `ex2.approx.ftz.f32` + `lg2.approx.ftz.f32`) and decomposing `K = 128` snapshots into two `16`-key sub-blocks (`2 * i_k` and `2 * i_k + 1`) so `_sb_bwd_dkv_rsi_kernel` runs at `BQ = 32, BS = 16` guarantees **`0` SM80 register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across 100% of compiled specializations**;
     - (b) **Tensor-Core GQA-Grouped Decoding (`_sb_decode_gqa_rsi_kernel`)**: tiling all $T_Q \times G$ queries sharing KV head $(b, h)$ into `[BM, K]` Tensor-Core blocks so `K` and `V` are loaded from HBM once per KV head (`2x–11x` faster than upstream scalar SIMT decoding); and
     - (c) **Superfused `StickBreakingAttentionBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`, fixing upstream's `FP32` `TypeError`) & Zero-Copy 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `StickBreaking-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-4` (`~1e-6` via `ALLOW_TF32 = False` IEEE contraction vs upstream `1.42e-3` `TF32` truncation drift) and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1e-2` across `o, rem, dq, dk, dv` against the differentiable `FP64` `exact_stickbreaking_attn_oracle` across $K, V \in \{64, 128\}$, $G \in \{1, 4\}$, `attend_current in {False, True}`, and non-multiple-of-32 $T = 137$, and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_reverse_stick_subtraction_update`, `M2_include_diagonal_pdp_in_exclusive_delta`, `M3_corrupt_softplus2_log2_scale`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Decoding Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `parallel_stickbreaking_attn_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) across `B=2, T in {1024, 2048, 4096}, HQ=16, H in {16, 4}, K=V in {64, 128}`, benchmark `stickbreaking_attn_decoding_rsi` (`B=4, TK in {2048, 4096}, TQ in {1, 16, 64}, HQ=16, H=2, K=V in {64, 128}`) against upstream `stickbreaking_attn_decoding`, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `StickBreakingAttentionBlockRSI`.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`StickBreaking-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{16384, 32768\}, H_Q = 16, H = 8, K = V = 64$), benchmark Zero-Copy Fused Ulysses `stickbreaking_cp8_ulysses_rsi` against Sequence-AllGather/Ring `stickbreaking_cp8_ring_baseline`, and run a fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop comparing `Stage A0` vs `Stage A4` at $D_{\text{model}} \in \{1024, 2048\}$.

## 30. Round-27 RSI — E153: `Test-Time Training (TTT-Linear): Learning to (Learn at Test Time): RNNs with Expressive Hidden States` (`arXiv:2407.04620`, ICML 2024 / JMLR 2025 — `fla/ops/ttt`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Test-Time Training (`TTT-Linear` — *Learning to (Learn at Test Time): RNNs with Expressive Hidden States*, `arXiv:2407.04620`, ICML 2024 / JMLR 2025 — Yu Sun, Xinhao Li, Karan Dalal, Jiarui Xu, Arjun Vikram, Genghan Zhang, Yann Dubois, Xinlei Chen, Xiaolong Wang, Sanmi Koyejo, Tatsunori Hashimoto, Carlos Guestrin; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/ttt`)**, which replaces a static vector hidden state with an online-learned linear model $(W_t, b_t) \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V} \times \mathbb{R}^{1 \times V}$ updated along the sequence by mini-batch gradient descent on the self-supervised reconstruction loss $\ell_t(W_t, b_t; k_t, v_t) = \frac{1}{2} \| \operatorname{LN}(k_t^\top W_t + b_t; w, b) - (v_t - k_t) \|_2^2$, yielding dual chunked update $v_{\text{new}}^{(c)} = \nabla_{z} \ell(z^{(c)}) = \operatorname{rstd}^{(c)} \cdot \frac{V y^{(c)} - \sum_v y^{(c)}_v - \hat{z}^{(c)} \sum_v y^{(c)}_v \hat{z}^{(c)}_v}{V}$ where $z^{(c)} = K^{(c)} W^{(c)} + b^{(c)}$, $\hat{z}^{(c)} = (z^{(c)} - \mu^{(c)}) \operatorname{rstd}^{(c)}$, and $y^{(c)} = (\hat{z}^{(c)} \odot w + b - V^{(c)} + K^{(c)}) \odot w$, followed by intra-chunk dual attention readout $O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)} = (Q^{(c)} / \sqrt{K}) W^{(c)} + b^{(c)} - \operatorname{tril}\!\left(((Q^{(c)} K^{(c)\top}) / \sqrt{K} + \mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^\top) \odot \eta^{(c)}\right) v_{\text{new}}^{(c)}$ and output LayerNorm-residual $O^{(c)} = O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)} + \operatorname{LN}(O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)}; w, b)$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, self-adjoint `LN^2` backward, single-pass online test-time adaptation decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/ttt` (`chunk.py` & `fused_chunk.py`) Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-27 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/ttt` (`chunk.py`, `fused_chunk.py`, `naive.py`) exhibits five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **Catastrophic SM80 Cubin Register Spilling (`40 / 62` = `64.52%` Spilling Specializations + `3` `OutOfResources` Shared-Memory Crashes, `30,146` Total Spills, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 3,392`, up to `151,808 B` Shared Memory)**: upstream `chunk.py` and `fused_chunk.py` hardcode `BK = max(next_power_of_2(K), 16)` and `BV = max(next_power_of_2(V), 16)` (`assert NK == 1` and `assert NV == 1`) with default `num_stages = 3`, forcing a single CTA to hold the entire `[128, 128]` `FP32` hidden-state matrix (`16,384` floats = `65,536 B`, 100% of an SM80 SM's physical register file) as the `DotOperand1` (`B` operand) of `tl.dot(tl.trans(b_k), b_h)` and simultaneously triple-buffering `[128, 128]` `b_h` loads in shared memory (`151,808 B`). As a result, `ttt.chunk` spills across `22 / 43` (`51.16%`) specializations + `2` `OutOfResources` crashes (`9,326` total spills, `max_spills = 2,636` in `chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_kernel_norm`, with even the winning `BF16` autotune configs spilling `122` regs at $K=64$ and `574` regs at $K=128$), while `ttt.fused_chunk` spills across `18 / 19` (`94.74%`) specializations + `1` `OutOfResources` crash (`20,820` total spills, `max_spills = 3,392` in `fused_chunk_ttt_linear_fwd_kernel` and `3,196` in `fused_chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_kernel_dh`);
     - (ii) **Redundant Full Forward Replay in Backward (`chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_h`) + Uncoalesced `[BV, BK]` State Reads + Unfused 3-Clone Eager `norm_residual` + Duplicate Dual-Attention `tl.dot` Calls**: `ChunkTTTLinearFunction.forward` discards `h, hb, v_new, x, rstd`, forcing `chunk_ttt_linear_bwd` to execute `chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_h` (re-running the entire sequential $N_T$-chunk forward recurrence from scratch during backward); `chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_kernel_norm` and `chunk_bwd_kernel_dqke` read `h` and `dh` transposed as `o_v[:, None] + o_k[None, :] * V` (`uncoalesced stride = V across warp threads`); `chunk_ttt_linear_fwd_kernel_o` and `chunk_ttt_linear_bwd_kernel_dv_local` execute two separate `tl.dot` calls on the same `b_v` / `b_do` (`tl.dot(b_A, b_v) + tl.dot(b_Ae, b_v)`) instead of factoring `(b_A + b_Ae) @ b_v`; and `norm_residual(o, w, b, eps)` runs outside `ChunkTTTLinearFunction` with 3 eager `.clone()` allocations + external `group_norm` forward/backward kernel launches;
     - (iii) **Backward `TF32` Mantissa Truncation (`1.36e-3` `FP32` Error vs `1.29e-7` Forward) + Premature `BF16` Inner-Loop `LN^2` Downcasting + Unmasked Tail-Padding Bug on `final_state_bias` (`hbt`) when `T % BT != 0`**: upstream omits `allow_tf32=False` on every `tl.dot` call across all backward kernels (`bwd_kernel_dv_local`, `bwd_kernel_norm`, `bwd_kernel_dqke`), degrading `FP32` backward accuracy by `>3,000x` (`dk rel_l2 = 1.36e-3`); downcasts `b_x, b_y, b_dy, b_dx` to `BF16` before computing the `LN^2` second-derivative projections; and fails to mask `b_v2` by `o_t[:, None] < T` in `chunk_ttt_linear_fwd_kernel_h` and `fused_chunk_ttt_linear_fwd_kernel`, corrupting `final_state_bias` (`hbt`) by `8.7%–14.6%` (`rel_l2 = 1.46e-1`) whenever $T \bmod 16 \ne 0$ (because `b_hb` and `b_b` are nonzero on padded rows `o_t >= T`);
     - (iv) **Missing Single-Pass Online Test-Time Adaptation / Decoding Kernel (`_ttt_decode_adapt_rsi_kernel`) & Unfused Block Periphery**: upstream provides no dedicated kernel for single-step ($T_q = 1$) or mini-batch ($T_q \in \{4, 8, 16\}$) autoregressive test-time adaptation $(h_0, hb_0) \to (o, h_t, hb_t)$, forcing 6 kernel launches + 5 intermediate HBM allocations per decoding step; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`TTT-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism for `TTT-Linear`'s chunked fast-weight gradient-descent recurrence.
   - Round-27 RSI (`code/ttt_linear_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:

## 31. Round-28 RSI — E154: Titans (`Titans: Learning to Memorize at Test Time`, `arXiv:2501.00663`, Google Research — Ali Behrouz, Peilin Zhong, Vahab Mirrokni; `fla/ops/titans`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Titans (`Titans: Learning to Memorize at Test Time`, `arXiv:2501.00663` — Ali Behrouz, Peilin Zhong, Vahab Mirrokni; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/titans`)**, which augments online test-time gradient descent with a coupled **weight-decay forgetting gate $\alpha_t \in (0, 1)$ and momentum memory state $S_t \in \mathbb{R}^{D \times D}$**:
$$S_t = \eta_t S_{t-1} - 2 \theta_t k_t^\top v_{\text{new}, t}, \qquad M_t = (1 - \alpha_t) M_{t-1} + S_t$$
yielding chunked readout $O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)} = (\beta^{(c)} \odot Q^{(c)}) M^{(c)} + (f^{(c)} \odot Q^{(c)}) S^{(c)} - 2 \bigl(\operatorname{tril}(Q^{(c)} K^{(c)\top}) \odot G^{(c)}\bigr) v_{\text{new}}^{(c)}$ followed by output LayerNorm-residual $O^{(c)} = O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)} + \operatorname{LN}(O_{\text{raw}}^{(c)}; w, b)$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, analytical dual-state backward, single-pass online test-time memorization decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

     - (i) **Zero Triton Kernels (`0` Forward & `0` Backward Triton Kernels) + `288` Doubly-Nested Python Loops per 16-Token Chunk (`3,642 ms` at $T=512$, `7,323 ms` at $T=1024$)**: upstream `fla/ops/titans` provides only `naive.py` and `log_impl.py`, where every chunk $i \in \{0, \dots, N_T - 1\}$ calls `combine_params_log(theta_i, alpha_i, eta_i, chunk_size)` containing three doubly/singly-nested Python `for` loops (`cal_n_log`: `136` slice/sum ops, `cal_f_log`: `16` `logsumexp` ops, `cal_G_log`: `136` `logsumexp` ops = `288` CUDA kernel launches per 16-token chunk, or `18,432` launches at $T=1024$) plus `4` dense `[B, H, BT, BT]` `torch.diag_embed` allocations per chunk;
     - (ii) **Broken Tail-Padding Broadcast Causing `20%` `dalpha` `NaN`s, `5%` `deta` `NaN`s, & `5.54%` `ht` Corruption When `T % BT != 0` (`naive.py:290-299`, `@pytest.mark.skipif(True, reason='FIXME')` in `test_titans.py:55`)**: when `pad_len = (BT - T % BT) % BT > 1`, `chunk_titans_linear_ref` zero-pads `theta, alpha, eta` via `F.pad(..., (0, 0, 0, pad_len))` and only overwrites the final index `theta[:, :, -1, :] = theta[:, :, -(pad_len + 1), :]`, leaving indices `-pad_len .. -2` equal to `0.0`. Passing `eta = 0.0` and `theta = 0.0` into `torch.log(...)` produces `-inf`, causing `-inf - (-inf) = NaN` in `dalpha` (`32 / 160` `NaN`s at $T=20$) and `deta` (`8 / 160` `NaN`s) and corrupting `final_state` (`ht rel_l2 = 5.54e-2`);
     - (iii) **Silent `FP32` Default-Dtype Bug in `cal_G_log` (`log_impl.py:65`), Uncast `FP16`/`BF16` Gate Cumulative Sums (`naive.py:301`), & Missing Parentheses in `v_new` LayerNorm VJP (`naive.py:179, 253`)**: `cal_G_log` allocates `log_G = torch.full(..., float("-inf"), device=log_beta.device)` without `dtype=log_beta.dtype` (silently truncating `FP64` inputs to `FP32`), `chunk_titans_linear_ref` casts `q, k, v, w, b` to `FP32` while leaving `theta, alpha, eta` in `FP16`/`BF16` and returning `o.dtype == torch.float32`, and `naive.py:179, 253` writes `v_new = D * grad - grad.sum(-1, keepdim=True) / (rstd * D)` without parentheses around `(D * grad - grad.sum(-1, keepdim=True))`;
     - (iv) **Missing Single-Pass Online Test-Time Memorization Decoding Kernel (`_titans_decode_memorize_rsi_kernel`) & Unfused Block Periphery**: upstream provides no kernel for single-step ($T_q = 1$) or mini-batch ($T_q \in \{4, 16\}$) online dual-state adaptation $(M_0, S_0) \to (o, M_T, S_T)$; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`Titans-CP = 8`)**: upstream provides no multi-GPU context parallelism for `Titans`'s coupled $(M_t, S_t)$ recurrence.
   - Round-28 RSI (`code/titans_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:

     - (b) **SM80 `MMA <-> DotOperand0` Transposed Dual-State Congruence (`M^T, S^T [V, K]`) + Analytical Decoupled Dual-State Adjoint (`_titans_bwd_dh_rsi_kernel`) + Self-Adjoint LayerNorm Backward**: storing both $(M_t^\top, S_t^\top) \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times K}$ transposed in registers and HBM matches SM80's `mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16` accumulator layout to `DotOperand0` (`A` operand of `tl.dot(b_mt, tl.trans(b_k))`) with 100% unit-stride coalesced `[BV, BK]` loads/stores and **`0` SM80 cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across 100% of compiled specializations**; and
     - (c) **Single-Pass Online Test-Time Memorization Decoding (`_titans_decode_memorize_rsi_kernel`), Superfused `TitansBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`), & Zero-Copy 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `Titans-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-5` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1e-2` across all forward/backward tensors (`o, mt, st, dq, dk, dv, dw, db, dtheta, dalpha, deta, dm0, ds0`) against the differentiable `FP64` `titans_fp64_oracle` across $K = V \in \{64, 128\}$, with/without initial & final states, and non-multiple-of-16 $T = 37$ (while proving upstream `chunk_titans_linear_ref`'s `32/160` `dalpha` `NaN`s, `8/160` `deta` `NaN`s, and `5.54%` `ht` tail corruption), and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_momentum_f_readout_and_state`, `M2_drop_bitriangular_G_intra_chunk`, `M3_drop_fused_output_ln_residual`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.

## 32. Round-29 RSI — E155: Comba (`Comba: Improving Bilinear RNNs with Closed-loop Control`, `arXiv:2506.02475` — Jiaxi Hu, Yongqi Pan, Jusen Du, Disen Lan, Xiaozhe Ren, Yibo Wang, Xuezhi Cao, Yunsheng Wu, Yu Zhang, Songlin Yang; `fla/ops/comba` & `fla/layers/comba.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Comba (`Comba: Improving Bilinear RNNs with Closed-loop Control`, `arXiv:2506.02475` — Hu et al.; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/comba` & `fla/layers/comba.py`)**, which generalizes Gated DeltaNet with **closed-loop pre-decay feedback on the state via an inner-decayed auxiliary key $p_t = s_h \hat{k}_t$ ($s_h = \sigma(\text{decay}_h) \in (0, 1)$) and query-key feedthrough correction $\tilde{q}_t = \hat{q}_t - D_h p_t$**:
$$v_{\text{corr}, t} = v_t - S_{t-1}^\top p_t, \qquad S_t = \exp(g_t) S_{t-1} + \beta_t k_t v_{\text{corr}, t}^\top, \qquad o_t = S_t^\top \tilde{q}_t$$
yielding the chunkwise closed-loop WY representation $A = (I + \operatorname{StrictTril}(\beta p k^\top \odot \exp(g_0 - g^\top)))^{-1}$, $u = A (\beta \odot v)$, $w = A (\beta \exp(g_0) \odot p)$, $v_{\text{new}} = u - w S$, and $o = \exp(g) \tilde{q} S + \operatorname{Tril}(\tilde{q} k^\top \odot \exp(g - g^\top)) v_{\text{new}}$, five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, analytical WY backward, single-pass recurrent decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/comba` & `fla/layers/comba.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-29 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/comba` (`chunk.py`, `wy_fast.py`, `utils.py`, `fused_recurrent.py`) and `fla/layers/comba.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **The Inner-Decay L2Norm Erasure Bug in `Comba.forward` (`fla/layers/comba.py:228-250`) + `6x` Separate `l2norm_fwd`/`l2norm_bwd` Kernel Launches (`chunk.py:165-167, 232-234`)**: `Comba.forward` multiplies `p = k * self.decay.sigmoid()` and subtracts `q = q - self.D * p` *before* calling `chunk_comba(..., use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel=True)`, which then calls `l2norm_fwd(q)`, `l2norm_fwd(k)`, `l2norm_fwd(p)`. Because $\mathrm{L2Norm}(s_h k_t) = s_h k_t / \sqrt{s_h^2 \|k_t\|_2^2 + \epsilon} = \mathrm{L2Norm}(k_t)$ for any positive scalar $s_h = \sigma(\text{decay}_h) > 0$, `l2norm_fwd(p)` **completely erases `sigmoid(decay)` from `p` inside the recurrence** (`||l2norm(0.9*k) - l2norm(0.1*k)||_rel = 8.32e-07`, and when `use_output_correction=False`, `self.decay.grad` vanishes to `9.56e-06` numerical noise!), while normalizing `q - D * p` *after* subtracting `D * p` distorts the rank-1 feedthrough $D$;
     - (ii) **`12 / 43` (`27.91%`) Spilling Specializations (`1,494` Total Spills, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 594`, `max_smem = 156,168 B`) & Redundant $64 \times 64$ `b_A` Re-Computation in `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` (`wy_fast.py:155-282`)**: `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` spills across `4/4` (`100%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 594, total_spills = 1,006`), `recompute_w_u_fwd_kernel` spills across `2/4` (`50%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 90, total_spills = 162`), `chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg` spills across `4/4` (`100%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 180, total_spills = 312`), and `chunk_gated_delta_rule_bwd_kernel_dhu_blockdim64` spills across `2/4` (`50%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 12, max_smem = 131,588 B`). In particular, `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` holds two `[64, 64]` `FP32` matrices (`b_A, b_dA`) simultaneously across 3 loops, writes `dp` to HBM in loop 1, reloads `dp` from HBM in loop 3, and recomputes the pre-solve Gram matrix `b_A = tl.dot(b_p_beta, tl.trans(b_k), b_A)` just to compute `b_dg0 += sum(b_dA * b_A, 1)` and `b_dg = -sum(b_dA * b_A, 0)`;
     - (iii) **Unconditional `TF32` Drift (`3.60e-03` `FP32` Error on `dp`, `2.93e-03` on `dg`) & Redundant Forward State Replay in `chunk_comba_bwd` (`chunk.py:86-148`)**: `chunk_scaled_dot_comba_pkt_fwd_kernel`, `recompute_w_u_fwd_kernel` (`b_w = tl.dot(b_A, b_kb)`), and `prepare_wy_repr_bwd_kernel` omit `allow_tf32=False`, and `chunk_comba_bwd` recomputes both `recompute_w_u_fwd` and `chunk_gated_delta_rule_fwd_h` from scratch in backward while launching `4` separate scalar cumsum kernels (`chunk_comba_cumsum_scalar_fwd`, `chunk_local_cumsum`, `chunk_comba_cumsum_scalar_bwd`);
     - (iv) **Uncoalesced 1-Warp `BV=8` `fused_recurrent_comba` Decoding (`fused_recurrent.py:134-141`) & `NotImplementedError` Backward**: upstream `fused_recurrent_comba_fwd` launches `NV = V/8` tiny 1-warp CTAs per head that reload `q, k, p, g, beta` `V/8` times from HBM, asserts `NK == 1`, and raises `NotImplementedError` in backward; and
     - (v) **Unfused `Comba` Layer Periphery (`5..6` Separate `nn.Linear` + `3` `ShortConvolution` + Separate Eager Gating/Norm Passes) & Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`Comba-CP = 8`)**.
   - Round-29 RSI (`code/comba_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Euler Homogeneous Degree-1 Closed-Loop WY Adjoint Identity ($\sum_j \mathrm{d}M_{ij} M_{ij} = \langle \mathrm{d}p_i^{(\text{from } M)}, p_i \rangle,\; -\sum_i \mathrm{d}M_{ij} M_{ij} = -\langle \mathrm{d}k_j^{(\text{from } M)}, k_j \rangle$) + Single-Pass Combined Exclusive/Inclusive Gate Adjoint ($\mathrm{d}\gamma_s = \mathrm{rev\_cumsum}(\mathrm{d}g + \mathrm{d}g_0)_s - \mathrm{d}g_{0,s}$)**: eliminates the redundant $64 \times 64 \times K$ Tensor-Core recomputation of `b_A`, eliminates the double HBM read/write of `dp`, eliminates `recompute_w_u_fwd` and `chunk_gated_delta_rule_fwd_h` replay in backward by saving compact `(w, v_new, h)`, and fuses the exclusive/inclusive gate cumsum and its reverse adjoint in registers (`4.37e-16` `FP64` parity);
     - (b) **Order-Correct Fused In-Kernel `L2Norm(q, k) -> p = s_h k_hat, q_tilde = q_hat - D_h p` + 100% Zero-Spill SM80 Kernels (`max_regs <= 168`, `0` Spills across `100%` of Specializations) with `allow_tf32=False` Precision Parity**: restores exact inner-decay gradient flow (`self.decay.grad` non-zero even when `use_output_correction=False`) and reduces `FP32` error by $>1,000\times$ (`~1e-6` vs `3.60e-3`); and
     - (c) **Single-Pass Multi-Warp Online Decoding (`comba_decode_rsi`), Superfused `CombaBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`), & Zero-Copy 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `Comba-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-5` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1e-2` across all forward/backward tensors (`o, ht, dq, dk, dv, dp, dg, dbeta, dh0`) against the differentiable `FP64` `comba_fp64_oracle` across $K = V \in \{64, 128\}$, GVA ($H=4, H_V=8$), and non-multiple-of-64 $T = 100$ (while proving upstream `Comba.forward`'s inner-decay `L2Norm` erasure bug and `3.60e-03` `TF32` drift), and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_pre_decay_g0_shift_in_wy`, `M2_drop_euler_wy_gate_adjoint_dg0_dg`, `M3_erase_inner_decay_via_post_l2norm_p`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Recurrent Decoding Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_comba_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) against upstream `chunk_comba` across 8 shapes (`B in {2, 4}, T in {512, 1024, 2048}, H in {16, 8}, HV=16, K=V in {64, 128}`), benchmark `comba_decode_rsi` (`B in {4, 8}, T_q in {1, 4, 16}, H=16, K=V in {64, 128}`) against upstream `fused_recurrent_comba` and `chunk_comba`, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `CombaBlockRSI`.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`Comba-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{16384, 32768\}, B = 2, H = 16, K = V \in \{64, 128\}$), benchmark Zero-Copy Fused Ulysses `comba_cp8_ulysses_rsi` against Sequence-AllGather/Ring `comba_cp8_ring_baseline` (calling upstream `chunk_comba`), and run a fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop comparing `Stage A0` (upstream `CombaBlockBaseline`) vs `Stage A4` (`CombaBlockRSI`) at $D_{\text{model}} \in \{1024, 2048\}$.

## 33. Round-30 RSI — E156: Gated KalmaNet (`Gated KalmaNet: A Fading Memory Layer Through Test-Time Ridge Regression`, `arXiv:2511.21016` — `fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Gated KalmaNet (`GKA`, `Gated KalmaNet: A Fading Memory Layer Through Test-Time Ridge Regression`, `arXiv:2511.21016`; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py`)**, which solves an online exponentially-decayed test-time ridge regression at every token $t$:
$$H_t = \exp(gk_t) H_{t-1} + k_t k_t^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times K}, \qquad U_t = \exp(g_t) U_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}, \qquad f_t = \|H_t\|_F, \qquad \lambda_t = \rho_{\text{ridge}} f_t$$
$$x_t = (H_t + \lambda_t I_K)^{-1} q_t \;\;(\text{solved via } L = \text{num\_iter} \text{ Chebyshev iterations on } [\lambda_t, \lambda_t + f_t]), \qquad q_{\text{mix}, t} = q_t + \alpha_t (x_t - q_t), \qquad o_t = \text{scale} \cdot U_t^\top q_{\text{mix}, t}$$
five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, analytical Chebyshev/ridge-adjoint backward, single-pass recurrent decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/gka` & `fla/layers/gka.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-30 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/gka` (`chunk.py`, `chunk_solve_fwd.py`, `chunk_solve_bwd.py`, `fused_recurrent.py`) and `fla/layers/gka.py` exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **`24 / 44` (`54.55%`) Spilling Specializations (`52,528` Total Spills, `max_regs = 255`, `max_spills = 10,028`, `max_smem = 114,688 B`) & Missing Disk Autotune Cache in `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_intra` (`chunk_solve_bwd.py:25-33`)**: `chunk_gka_solve_fwd_kernel` spills across `8/8` (`100%`) specializations (`max_regs = 255, max_spills = 282, total_spills = 988`) because it holds `[64, 128]` `b_q, b_k, b_x, b_x_prev`, `[128, 128]` `b_h`, and `[64, 64]` `b_m, b_kk` simultaneously across an unrolled `NUM_ITER: tl.constexpr = 30` Chebyshev loop; `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_intra` spills across `12/12` (`100%`) specializations (`regs = 32..255, max_spills = 10,028, total_spills = 51,324, max_smem = 114,688 B`) because it loads two `[128, 128]` matrices (`b_h, b_dh`), outputs a third `[128, 128]` matrix (`b_hs`), and holds four `[64, 64]` matrices (`b_m, b_s, b_ds, b_w2`) simultaneously while omitting `**autotune_cache_kwargs` on `@fla_cache_autotune`; `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_inter` spills `26` registers at `255` regs (`smem = 49,152 B`); and `chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg`/`chunk_bwd_kernel_dv` (called via `chunk_simple_gla`) spill `14..120` registers at `255` regs;
     - (ii) **`SimpleGLABackwardGuard` 16-Bit Backward Crash on `K < 64` or `V < 32` (`chunk.py:146-164`)**: upstream `chunk_gka` delegates its value-readout stage to `chunk_simple_gla`, whose backward kernels hit an illegal memory access (`CUDA error: an illegal memory access was encountered`) for `bfloat16`/`float16` inputs with `K < 64` or `V < 32`, forcing upstream `chunk_gka` to wrap outputs in `SimpleGLABackwardGuard` and raise a fatal `RuntimeError` in `.backward()`;
     - (iii) **Unconditional `TF32` Tensor-Core Drift (`3.10e-03` `FP32` Error on `o`, `3.57e-03` on `dk`, `7.02e-03` on `dalpha`) & Redundant 18-Kernel Multi-Pass Dual-State Replay (`chunk.py:42-144`)**: upstream omits `input_precision="ieee"` in all `tl.dot` calls, runs `chunk_local_cumsum` 5 times per training step (3 forward cumsums on identical `g == gk` and 2 reverse cumsums in backward), recomputes both `chunk_fwd_h(k, k)` and `chunk_fwd_h(k, v)` from scratch in backward, and runs two separate backward state scans (`chunk_bwd_dh` for `dh` and `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_inter` for `hs`);
     - (iv) **Low-Occupancy `191`-Register `fused_recurrent_gka_solve_fwd_kernel` (`fused_recurrent.py:16-89`) & Two-Pass Recurrent Decode**: upstream `fused_recurrent_gka` launches only `N * H` CTAs (`16..32` blocks on a `108`-SM A100, leaving `70%-85%` of SMs idle), uses `191 > 168` registers at $K=128$, and launches a second recurrent pass (`fused_recurrent_simple_gla`) that re-reads `k, v, g` from HBM; and
     - (v) **Unfused `GatedKalmaNet` Layer Periphery (`7` Separate Input `nn.Linear` Projections + Separate `l2_norm` + `beta` Scaling + Gate Prep) & Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`GKA-CP = 8`)**.
   - Round-30 RSI (`code/gka_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Unified Adjoint Gramian State Theorem ($\tilde{\mathrm{d}H}_{[c]} = \mathrm{d}H_{[c]} + \frac{1}{2} B_{\text{hs}, [c]}$), Euler Quadratic Gramian Identity ($\sum_d ((K K^\top \odot W_2) K)_{t, d} K_{t, d} = \sum_j (K K^\top \odot W_2 \odot K K^\top)_{t, j}$), & Rank-1 Symmetric Completion of $W_2$ ($W_{2, j, l} = \mathbf{1}[j \le l] 2^{-g_j} w_{1, l} + \mathbf{1}[j > l] w_{1, j} 2^{-g_l}$)**: fuses the `chunk_bwd_dh` and `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_inter` backward recurrences into a single `[K, BV]` column-slabbed recurrence for $\tilde{\mathrm{d}H}_{[c]}$ (eliminating the `[B, NT, H, K, K]` intermediate tensor `hs` and `chunk_gka_bwd_kernel_dk_inter`), eliminates the `[64, 64] x [64, 64]` `tl.dot(b_m * b_w, tl.trans(b_m))` GEMM for $W_2$, and eliminates the redundant `b_t_intra` loop for `b_dg`;
     - (b) **In-Flight Partial Frobenius Norm Projection inside `_gka_fwd_h_kk_fro_part_rsi_kernel` + Runtime-Loop Strip-Tiled Chebyshev Solver (`_gka_solve_fwd_rsi_kernel`) + Self-Contained Zero-Spill GLA Readout/Backward**: computes $\|H_{[c]}\|_F^2$ and $k_j^\top H_{[c]} k_j$ on-the-fly while each `[K, BV]` slab of $H_{[c]}$ is already in registers during the forward state scan, replaces `NUM_ITER: tl.constexpr` loop unrolling with a hardware loop + scalar Chebyshev recurrence ($\rho_{\text{sq4}} = (2(2\rho_{\text{ridge}} + 1))^{-2}$), eliminates `SimpleGLABackwardGuard` crashes at `K < 64` or `V < 32`, and achieves **`0` SM80 cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of compiled specializations** and $>1,000\times$ lower `FP32` error (`~1e-6` vs `7.02e-3`); and
     - (c) **2-Slab Zero-Spill Online Decoding (`gka_decode_rsi`), Superfused `GatedKalmaNetBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`), & Zero-Copy 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `GKA-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-5` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1e-2` across all forward/backward tensors (`o, ht_kk, ht_kv, dq, dk, dv, dg, dalpha, dh0_kk, dh0_kv`) against the differentiable `FP64` `gka_fp64_oracle` across $K = V \in \{64, 128\}$, small head dims $K = V = 32$ (where upstream `SimpleGLABackwardGuard` crashes!), and non-multiple-of-64 $T = 100$, and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_zero_chebyshev_momentum_rho_sq4`, `M2_drop_frobenius_ridge_lambda_in_solve_and_bwd`, `M3_invert_alpha_query_mixing_gate`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Recurrent Decoding Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `chunk_gka_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) against upstream `chunk_gka` across 8 shapes (`B in {2, 4}, T in {512, 1024, 2048}, H in {8, 16}, K=V in {64, 128}`), benchmark `gka_decode_rsi` (`B in {4, 8}, T_q in {1, 4, 16}, H=16, K=V in {64, 128}`) against upstream `fused_recurrent_gka` and `chunk_gka`, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for `GatedKalmaNetBlockRSI`.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`GKA-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
