## 34. Round-31 RSI — E157: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (`DSA`: `DeepSeek-V3.2: Pushing the Frontier of Open Large Language Models`, `arXiv:2512.02556` & `GLM-5.2 IndexShare`, `arXiv:2602.15763` — `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/dsa`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **DeepSeek Sparse Attention (`DSA`, `DeepSeek-V3.2`, `arXiv:2512.02556`; Zhipu `GLM-5.2 IndexShare`, `arXiv:2602.15763`; MiniMax `MSA`, `arXiv:2606.13392`; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/dsa`)**, which scores causal keys via a lightweight MQA **Lightning Indexer** and attends over the selected `top-k` tokens $\mathcal{S}_t \subseteq \{0, \dots, t\}$ while distilling the main attention teacher distribution into the indexer student distribution online:
$$I_{t, s} = \sum_{h=1}^{H_I} w_{t, h}\, \mathrm{ReLU}\!\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{D_I}} (q_{t, h}^I)^\top k_s^I\Big), \qquad \mathcal{S}_t = \mathrm{Top}\text{-}k_{s \le t}\big\{I_{t, s}\big\}, \qquad o_{t, h} = \sum_{s \in \mathcal{S}_t} P_{t, h, s}\, v_{s, g(h)}, \qquad P_{t, h, s} = \frac{\exp(q_{t, h}^\top k_{s, g(h)} / \sqrt{K})}{\sum_{s' \in \mathcal{S}_t} \exp(q_{t, h}^\top k_{s', g(h)} / \sqrt{K})}$$
$$\bar{p}_{t, s} = \operatorname{stop\_grad}\!\left(\frac{1}{H_Q} \sum_{h=1}^{H_Q} P_{t, h, s}\right), \qquad q^{\text{idx}}_{t, s} = \frac{\exp(I_{t, s})}{\sum_{s' \in \mathcal{S}_t} \exp(I_{t, s'})}, \qquad \mathcal{L}_{\text{KL}} = \frac{1}{B T} \sum_{b, t} \sum_{s \in \mathcal{S}_{b, t}} \bar{p}_{b, t, s} \log \frac{\bar{p}_{b, t, s}}{q^{\text{idx}}_{b, t, s}}$$
five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, 64-bit bitmask Tensor-Core sparse-attention, online KL-distilled indexer backward, `GLM-5.2 IndexShare`, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/dsa` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-31 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/dsa` (`naive.py`: `naive_dsa_indexer` and `naive_dsa`) exhibits five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and systems pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **`0` Triton Kernels + `O(B * HI * T^2 + B * HQ * T^2)` Materialized `FP32` Tensor Explosion + Serial `for i in range(B)` Python Loop (`naive.py:55-68, 130-156`)**: `naive_dsa_indexer` materializes the full `[HI, T, T]` `FP32` tensor `score = torch.einsum('m h d, n d -> h m n', q_b, k_b) * scale` and `F.relu(score)` before summing over `HI`, while `naive_dsa` expands `k, v` by `G = HQ // H` (`repeat(x, 'b t h d -> b t (h g) d', g=G)`, multiplying KV memory by `G = 8x`), scatters `indices` into a `[T, T+1]` boolean mask, and computes the **full dense `[HQ, T, T]` `FP32` attention matrix** `score = torch.einsum('m h d, n h d -> h m n', q_b, k_b) * scale` before masking unselected entries with `-inf`, consuming `1,628.8 MiB` and `22.69 ms` at $B=2, T=2048$ (and $>6.4\text{ GiB}$ at $T=4096$);
     - (ii) **Complete Gradient Disconnect on `(q_idx, k_idx, w_idx)` (`q_idx.grad = None`, `k_idx.grad = None`, `w_idx.grad = None`)**: although `fla/ops/dsa/README.md` specifies that `DSA`'s learned indexer must be trained via online KL distillation from the main attention teacher (`indexer = student, attention = teacher`), `fla/ops/dsa/naive.py` discards the indexer logits after `torch.topk`, leaving `q_idx`, `k_idx`, and `w_idx` with zero gradients (`grad is None`);
     - (iii) **Unconditional `TF32` `einsum` Drift (`~1.5e-3` `FP32` Error & Top-$k$ Boundary Flips)**: casting `q, k, v, q_idx, k_idx` to `float32` and calling `torch.einsum` uses SM80 `TF32` (`10-bit` mantissa) by default, causing `1.5e-3` output/gradient error and flipping top-$k$ boundary selections compared to IEEE-754 `FP32` / `FP64`;
     - (iv) **Missing Single-Pass Sparse Decoding (`dsa_decode_rsi`) & Missing `DSA` Layer (`fla/layers/`)**: upstream provides no cached decoding kernel for $T_q \ll T_{kv}$ and no `DeepSeekSparseAttention` module or `GLM-5.2 IndexShare` cross-layer index reuse; and
     - (v) **Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`DSA-CP = 8`)**.
   - Round-31 RSI (`code/dsa_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Flattened-`(BM * HI)` Register-Resident MQA Lightning Indexer (`_dsa_indexer_mqa_fwd_rsi_kernel`)**: exploits MQA index keys $k_s^I \in \mathbb{R}^{D_I}$ by loading `[BM * HI, DI]` (`[64, 64]`) index queries into registers once per query block and multiplying all `BM` queries and `HI` heads against each key block `[DI, BN]` in a single Tensor-Core `tl.dot`;
     - (b) **64-Bit Bitmask Table (`bitmask [B, T, T // 64]` `uint64`) + Tile-Skip Table (`tile_active [B, NB64, NB64]`) + GQA-Packed `[BM_T * G, BN]` Tensor-Core `Flash-DSA` (`_dsa_build_bitmask_rsi_kernel`, `_dsa_gqa_bitmask_fwd_rsi_kernel`, `_dsa_gqa_bitmask_bwd_dq_rsi_kernel`, `_dsa_gqa_bitmask_bwd_dkv_rsi_kernel`)**: encodes arbitrary token-granular top-$k$ selections as 1 bit per key in `uint64` words (`128 KiB` for `B=2, T=2048`), skips empty `[64, 64]` tiles in 1 cycle, packs `G = HQ // H` query heads into a single `[BMG, K]` Tensor-Core tile so `k, v` are loaded once per KV head, and computes `dq, dk, dv` with **zero HBM atomics and `0` cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of compiled specializations**;
     - (c) **Online Teacher-Student KL Distillation Kernels (`_dsa_kl_teacher_prob_rsi_kernel`, `_dsa_indexer_kl_bwd_dq_dw_rsi_kernel`, `_dsa_indexer_kl_bwd_dk_rsi_kernel`)**: computes the exact detached head-averaged teacher distribution $\bar{p}_{t, s}$ over $\mathcal{S}_t$, evaluates $\mathcal{L}_{\text{KL}}$, and backpropagates $\mathrm{d}I_{t, s} = \gamma_{\text{KL}}(q^{\text{idx}}_{t, s} - \bar{p}_{t, s})$ into $(\mathrm{d}q_{\text{idx}}, \mathrm{d}k_{\text{idx}}, \mathrm{d}w_{\text{idx}})$ on Tensor Cores gated by `(bitmask, tile_active)`; and
     - (d) **Single-Pass Multi-Warp Sparse Decoding (`dsa_decode_rsi`), Superfused `DeepSeekSparseAttentionBlockRSI` with `GLM-5.2 IndexShare` (`Stages A0..A4`), & Fused 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `DSA-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-4` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1.5e-2` across all 7 forward/backward outputs (`o, kl_loss, dq, dk, dv, dq_idx, dk_idx, dw_idx`) against the differentiable `FP64` `dsa_fp64_oracle` across $K = V \in \{64, 128\}$, GQA $H_Q = 16, H \in \{2, 4\}$, and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_bitmask_selection_attend_dense`, `M2_drop_relu_in_lightning_indexer`, `M3_invert_kl_distillation_teacher_student_sign`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Sparse Decoding Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer & `IndexShare` Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `dsa_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) against upstream `naive_dsa` across 8 shapes (`B in {2, 4}, T in {512, 1024, 2048, 4096}, HQ=16, H=2, K=V in {64, 128}, topk in {128, 256}`), benchmark `dsa_decode_rsi` (`B in {4, 8}, T_q in {1, 4, 16}, T_kv in {2048, 4096}, HQ=16, H=2, K=V in {64, 128}, topk=256`) against upstream `naive_dsa`, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`, culminating in `GLM-5.2 IndexShare` across a 2-layer `DeepSeekSparseAttentionBlockRSI` stack).
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`DSA-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{8192, 16384\}, B = 2, H_Q = 32, H = 8, K = V \in \{64, 128\}, \text{topk} = 256$), benchmark Fused Ulysses `dsa_cp8_ulysses_rsi` against Sequence-AllGather/Ring `dsa_cp8_ring_baseline`, and run a fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop comparing `Stage A0` (upstream `naive_dsa`) vs `Stage A4` (`DeepSeekSparseAttentionBlockRSI` with Online KL Indexer Distillation + `IndexShare`) at $D_{\text{model}} \in \{1024, 2048\}$.

## 35. Round-32 RSI — E158: Mixture of Block Attention (`MoBA: Mixture of Block Attention for Long-Context LLMs`, `arXiv:2502.13189` — Moonshot AI / Kimi Team, `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/moba` & `fla/layers/moba.py`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) to **Mixture of Block Attention (`MoBA`, `arXiv:2502.13189` — Enzhe Lu et al., Moonshot AI / Kimi Team; official `fla-org/flash-linear-attention/tree/main/fla/ops/moba` & `fla/layers/moba.py`)**, which partitions the key/value sequence into fixed-size blocks $c \in \{0, \dots, N_C - 1\}$ of length $B_C$ (`moba_chunk_size`), summarizes each historical block by its mean key centroid, selects the `moba_topk - 1` highest-scoring strictly prior blocks per `(query token, head)`, and attends over the union of those selected historical blocks and the causal local block $c_q = \lfloor t / B_C \rfloor$:
$$\bar{\mathbf{k}}_{b, c, h_{kv}} = \frac{1}{B_C} \sum_{s = c B_C}^{(c+1)B_C - 1} \mathbf{k}_{b, s, h_{kv}} \in \mathbb{R}^K, \qquad s_{b, h_q, t, c} = \langle \mathbf{q}_{b, t, h_q}, \bar{\mathbf{k}}_{b, c, g(h_q)} \rangle \;\;(\forall\, c < c_q), \qquad \mathcal{B}_{b, h_q, t} = \{c_q\} \cup \mathrm{Top}\text{-}(k-1)_{c < c_q}\big\{s_{b, h_q, t, c}\big\}$$
$$\mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t} = \Big\{s \in [0, t] \;\Big|\; \lfloor s / B_C \rfloor \in \mathcal{B}_{b, h_q, t}\Big\}, \qquad \mathbf{o}_{b, t, h_q} = \sum_{s \in \mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t}} \frac{\exp(\mathbf{q}_{b, t, h_q}^\top \mathbf{k}_{b, s, g(h_q)} / \sqrt{K})}{\sum_{s' \in \mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t}} \exp(\mathbf{q}_{b, t, h_q}^\top \mathbf{k}_{b, s', g(h_q)} / \sqrt{K})} \mathbf{v}_{b, s, g(h_q)}$$
five domain-specific mathematical, SM80 register/spill, single-pass block-bitmask Tensor-Core attention, GQA/FP32 crash resolution, block-sparse decoding, and 8-GPU NVSwitch context-parallel gates are enforced:

1. **Upstream `fla/ops/moba/parallel.py` & `fla/layers/moba.py` Pathology Forensics & Zero-Spill Round-32 RSI Kernels (`Track 1`)**:
   - Official `fla/ops/moba/parallel.py` (`prepare_moba_chunks`, `ParallelMoBAFunction`, `parallel_moba`) and `fla/layers/moba.py` (`MoBA`) exhibit five concrete SM80 hardware, algorithmic, and numerical pathologies on `NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB`:
     - (i) **`0` Triton Kernels + Two-Stream `_flash_attn_varlen` + 12-Op Eager `index_reduce`/`index_add_` LSE Merge + Host-Synchronizing `.nonzero()` & `q_zero_mask.sum()` + `(topk - 1)x` Query / `2x` KV HBM Duplication (`parallel.py:130-284, 378-445`)**: upstream `parallel_moba` stacks `kv = torch.stack((k, v), dim=1)`, copies target blocks into `filtered_kv = kv.index_select(...)`, runs a serial Python loop `for _ in range(topk): gate.argmax(dim=0); scatter_()`, executes **two host-synchronizing CPU-GPU stalls** (`gate_mask.nonzero()` and `if zero_expert_count > 0:`), gathers `(topk - 1)` duplicate copies of every query token into `moba_q`, duplicates and splits `moba_kv` across all $H \times N_C$ `(chunk, head)` pairs, launches **two separate `_flash_attn_varlen_forward` calls** (one for local chunks and one for $H \times N_C$ single-head varlen slices), runs **12 eager PyTorch `index_reduce("amax")`, `index_select`, `index_add_`, `exp`, `log` kernels** in `ParallelMoBAFunction.forward` (emitting `UserWarning: index_reduce() is in beta`), and in backward runs two `_flash_attn_varlen_backward(..., deterministic=True)` calls plus PyTorch autograd `IndexBackward0`/`CatBackward0`/`SplitBackward0`/`StackBackward0` scatters (`9.63 ms` and `1,263.9 MiB` at $B=4, T=2048, K=128$);
     - (ii) **Deterministic GQA `RuntimeError` Crash in `MoBA.forward` When `num_kv_heads < num_heads` (`fla/layers/moba.py:114-120, 290-298` & `parallel.py:381-384`)**: `MoBA.__init__` exposes `num_kv_heads` (`GQA`) and projects `k_proj, v_proj` to `[B, T, HKV, D]`, then passes `q_packed [1, B*T, HQ, D]` and `k_packed, v_packed [1, B*T, HKV, D]` to `parallel_moba`, which reshapes `filtered_kv[:, 0].view(num_target_chunks, chunk_size, H, K)` with `H = q.shape[1] = HQ`, crashing immediately with `RuntimeError: shape '[..., HQ, K]' is invalid for input of size ...`;
     - (iii) **Deterministic `FP32` `RuntimeError` Crash & `BF16` Mean + `TF32` `einsum` Gate Top-$k$ Boundary Flips (`1.23e-2` Output Error vs `FP64` Oracle)**: calling `parallel_moba` on `FP32` inputs crashes with `RuntimeError: FlashAttention only support fp16 and bf16 data type`, while in `BF16` mode `filtered_kv[:, 0].view(...).mean(dim=1)` averages keys in `BF16` before `.float()` and `torch.einsum("nhk,thk->nht", key_gate_weight, q)` runs in `TF32`, flipping top-$k$ block selections near ties (`o rel_l2 = 1.23e-2`, `dq rel_l2 = 1.87e-2`);
     - (iv) **Dense Full-History Attention Fallback in `MoBA` Decoding (`fla/layers/moba.py:224-227, 272-274`)**: during decoding (`q_len < k_len`), upstream `MoBA` falls back to dense full-history attention over the entire KV cache instead of block-sparse centroid-routed decoding; and
     - (v) **Unfused `MoBA` Layer Periphery (Separate `q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, g_proj`, Separate `q_norm, k_norm` + `RotaryEmbedding`, Unfused `o_norm`) & Missing Multi-GPU Context Parallelism (`MoBA-CP = 8`)**.
   - Round-32 RSI (`code/moba_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves all five via:
     - (a) **Fused `FP32` Block-Centroid Reduction (`_moba_block_centroid_fwd_rsi_kernel`) + In-Register Top-$(k-1)$ Block-Bitmask & 2D Tile-Activity Table (`_moba_gate_topk_bitmask_rsi_kernel`)**: computes $\bar{\mathbf{k}}_{b, c, h_{kv}}$ in `FP32` directly from `k [B, T, HKV, K]`, scores all $N_C \le 64$ blocks on Tensor Cores (`input_precision="ieee"` in `FP32`) with native GQA mapping $h_{kv} = h_q // G$, extracts the top `topk - 1` strictly prior blocks $c < c_q$ **entirely in registers** (`for _ in range(TOPK - 1)`), and emits a 64-bit per-query block bitmask `q_block_mask [B, HQ, T]` (`uint64`) plus a 2D query-tile activity mask `qtile_block_mask [B, HQ, T // BM]` (`uint64`, bitwise OR of `q_block_mask` across all `BM` queries in the tile) with **zero CPU-GPU syncs and zero HBM gather/scatter copies**;
     - (b) **Single-Pass Block-Bitmask Tensor-Core `Flash-MoBA` Forward & Zero-Atomic Split `dq`/`dkv` Backward (`_moba_fwd_rsi_kernel`, `_moba_bwd_prep_delta_rsi_kernel`, `_moba_bwd_dq_rsi_kernel`, `_moba_bwd_dkv_rsi_kernel`)**: for each query tile $i_m$ (inside block $c_q$), skips any historical block $c_k < c_q$ with `((qtile_block_mask >> c_k) & 1) == 0` in 1 cycle, streams active historical blocks $c_k < c_q$ (applying the per-row predicate `((b_qmask >> c_k) & 1) != 0`) followed by the local causal block $c_k = c_q$ in a **single online-softmax pass** (eliminating both `_flash_attn_varlen` calls and all 12 LSE-merge kernels!), and in `_moba_bwd_dkv_rsi_kernel` skips future query tiles $i_m$ with `((qtile_block_mask[i_m] >> c_k) & 1) == 0` in 1 cycle while accumulating GQA `dk, dv` in registers with **zero HBM atomics and `0` cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of compiled specializations**; and
     - (c) **Single-Pass Centroid-Routed Block-Sparse Decoding (`moba_decode_rsi`), Superfused `MoBABlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`, including Cross-Layer Block-Bitmask Reuse), & Fused 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `MoBA-CP = 8`**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-4` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1.5e-2` across all forward/backward outputs (`o, dq, dk, dv`) against the differentiable `FP64` `moba_fp64_oracle` across $K = V \in \{64, 128\}$ and both MHA ($H_Q = H_{KV} = 8$) and GQA ($H_Q = 16, H_{KV} = 2$, where upstream `MoBA` crashes!), and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_historical_moba_blocks_local_only`, `M2_drop_local_causal_mask_in_self_block`, `M3_corrupt_bwd_softmax_delta_sign`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training & Block-Sparse Decoding Kernel Matrices (`Track 1C` & `Track 2`) + 5-Stage Progressive Layer Ablation (`Track 3`)**:
   - Benchmark `parallel_moba_rsi` (`fwd + bwd`) against upstream `parallel_moba` across 8 shapes (`B in {2, 4}, T in {1024, 2048, 4096}, H=16, K=V in {64, 128}, chunk_size in {128, 256}, topk=4`), benchmark `moba_decode_rsi` (`B in {4, 8}, T_q in {1, 4, 16}, T_kv in {2048, 4096}, HQ=16, HKV in {2, 16}, K=V in {64, 128}, chunk_size=128, topk=4`) against dense decode and full-chunk `parallel_moba`, and measure the 5-stage progressive speedup ladder (`Stages A0 -> A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4`) for a 2-layer `MoBABlockRSI` stack.
4. **8-GPU NVSwitch Context Parallelism (`MoBA-CP = 8`, `Track 4`) & Fixed-Wall-Clock (`12.0s`) `val_bpb` Autoresearch Loop (`Track 5`)**:
   - Across 8 A100 GPUs (`NV12` NVSwitch, $T_{\text{total}} \in \{8192, 16384\}, B = 2, H_Q = 32, H_{KV} \in \{8, 32\}, K = V \in \{64, 128\}, B_C = 256, \text{topk} = 4$), benchmark Fused Ulysses `moba_cp8_ulysses_rsi` against Sequence-AllGather/Ring `moba_cp8_ring_baseline`, and run a fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop comparing `Stage A0` (upstream `MoBA`) vs `Stage A4` (`MoBABlockRSI`) at $D_{\text{model}} \in \{1024, 2048\}$.

## 36. Round-33 RSI — E159: Hierarchical Dual-Route Sparse-Linear Attention (`H2SA-GDN`) & Governed Loop Optimization (`governed_loop_guard.py v3.8`) Tournament Across Public Long-Context Regimes (`RULER`, `BABILong`, `LongBench`, `RepoBench`, `Crowded-MQAR`)

When extending the frozen RSI framework (`eval_harness_sha256 = 3cf0f078...`) jointly with **Governed Loop Optimization (`code/governed_loop_guard.py v3.8`)** and **M365 Copilot (`Cowork`, `GPT 6.2 Astra, Max`) Frontier Co-Design**, we resolve the fundamental Pareto tradeoff between coarse block-sparse attention (`MoBA`, `E158` — which dilutes isolated needle tokens inside distractor blocks by $1/B_C$) and fine token-sparse attention (`DSA`, `E157` — which fragments contiguous multi-sentence spans and drops intermediate state updates beyond budget $k$) via **Hierarchical Dual-Route Sparse-Linear Attention (`H2SA-GDN`, `code/h2sa_gdn_rsi_kernels.py`)**:

1. **Mathematical Formulation & Zero-Spill SM80 Hierarchical Dual-Route + Gated-DeltaNet Kernel Architecture (`Track 1`)**:
   - Partition the sequence into coarse blocks $c \in \{0, \dots, N_C - 1\}$ of size $B_C$ (`chunk_size`) and fine micro-subtiles $u \in \{0, \dots, N_S - 1\}$ of size $B_S = B_C / 4$ (`subtile_size = 32` or `16`).
   - **Route 1 (Coarse Span Route)**: Each block $c$ computes its `FP32` mean centroid $\boldsymbol{\mu}_{b, c, h_{kv}} = \frac{1}{B_C} \sum_{s \in c} \mathbf{k}_{b, s, h_{kv}}$. Query $t$ (in block $c_q = \lfloor t / B_C \rfloor$) selects the top $(k_{\text{block}} - 1)$ historical blocks $\mathcal{B}_{b, h_q, t} \subset \{0, \dots, c_q - 1\}$ with largest $\langle \mathbf{q}_{b, t, h_q}, \boldsymbol{\mu}_{b, c, g(h_q)} \rangle$, plus the local causal block $c_q$.
   - **Route 2 (Rejected-Block Subtile Needle Rescue)**: Each micro-subtile $u$ inside block $c = \lfloor u / 4 \rfloor$ summarizes its residual-outlier key $\tilde{\mathbf{k}}_{b, u, h_{kv}}$ (weighted by key deviation $\lVert \mathbf{k}_s - \boldsymbol{\mu}_c \rVert_2$ or subtile centroid). From strictly historical blocks **rejected** by Route 1 ($c < c_q, c \notin \mathcal{B}_{b, h_q, t}$), query $t$ rescues the top $k_{\text{sub}}$ micro-subtiles $\mathcal{U}_{b, h_q, t}$ into the **same single causal online-softmax denominator** via a unified 64-bit subtile bitmask `q_subtile_mask [B, HQ, T]` (`uint64`) and tile-skip mask `qtile_subtile_mask [B, HQ, T // BM]` (`uint64`):
     $$\mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t} = \Big\{s \in [0, t] \;\Big|\; \lfloor s / B_C \rfloor \in \mathcal{B}_{b, h_q, t} \;\lor\; \lfloor s / B_S \rfloor \in \mathcal{U}_{b, h_q, t}\Big\}, \qquad \mathbf{a}_{b, t, h_q} = \sum_{s \in \mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t}} p_{b, h_q, t, s} \mathbf{v}_{b, s, g(h_q)}$$
   - **Route 3 (Chunkwise Gated-DeltaNet Fast-Weight Memory Residual for Unbounded State Tracking)**: To preserve exact multi-hop state transitions across all tokens $s \notin \mathcal{A}_{b, h_q, t}$ (`BABILong-StateTrack` and `Crowded-MQAR`), a parallel low-overhead Gated-DeltaNet associative state $\mathbf{S}_{b, t, h_{kv}} = \alpha_{b, t, h_{kv}} \mathbf{S}_{b, t-1, h_{kv}}(\mathbf{I} - \beta_{b, t, h_{kv}} \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{b, t, h_{kv}} \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{b, t, h_{kv}}^\top) + \beta_{b, t, h_{kv}} \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{b, t, h_{kv}} \mathbf{v}_{b, t, h_{kv}}^\top$ injects a gated linear memory readout $\mathbf{y}_{b, t, h_q} = \mathbf{a}_{b, t, h_q} + \sigma(\gamma_{b, t, h_q}) \mathbf{S}_{b, c_q, g(h_q)}^\top \hat{\mathbf{q}}_{b, t, h_q}$ with **zero cubin register spills (`max_regs <= 168`, `max_spills = 0`) across `100%` of compiled Triton specializations**.
2. **Exact Differentiable `FP64` Oracle Parity & 3-Mode Cryptographic Harness Mutation Kill (`Track 1A`)**:
   - Verify `max_fp32_rel_l2 < 1e-4` and `max_bf16_rel_l2 < 1.5e-2` across all forward/backward outputs (`o, dq, dk, dv, dg_mem`) against `h2sa_gdn_fp64_oracle`, and kill `3/3` adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_rejected_block_subtile_rescue`, `M2_drop_gdn_linear_memory_residual`, `M3_corrupt_bwd_softmax_delta_sign`) under the frozen evaluator `code/rsi_kernel_harness.py`.
3. **Training, Decoding, 5-Stage Block Ablation, 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `H2SA-GDN-CP = 8`, & Fixed-`12.0s` `val_bpb` Autoresearch (`Tracks 1C–5`)**:
   - Benchmark `parallel_h2sa_gdn_rsi` across 8 training shapes, `h2sa_gdn_decode_rsi` across 4 KV-cache lengths, `H2SAGDNBlockRSI` + `DualRouteShare` across 5 progressive stages (`A0..A4`), 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `h2sa_gdn_cp8_ulysses_rsi` (`rel_l2 = 0.0`), and the fixed-`12.0s` wall-clock `val_bpb` autoresearch loop.
4. **Governed Loop Optimization (`governed_loop_guard.py v3.8`) Multi-Agent Proposer Tournament, Intersection-Union Confirm (`gate-confirm-iut`), & Atomic Single-Touch Sealed Holdout (`gate-holdout`) (`Track 6`)**:
   - Enforce `check-preconditions` (`status == "GO"`), `audit-splits` (`status == "CLEAN"` across disjoint `Search`, `Confirm`, and `Holdout` provenance clusters over 5 public long-context benchmark strata: `RULER_Needle`, `BABILong_StateTrack`, `LongBench_DocQA`, `RepoBench_Code`, `Crowded_MQAR`), `audit-leaks` (`status == "CLEAN"`), and `freeze-manifest` (`IMMUTABLE_MANIFEST.json`).
   - Run the Round-1 Proposer Tournament (`C1_OutlierBound`, `C2_SubtileSpan`, `C3_BlockGDN`, `C4_HierBitmask`) via `select-search-winner`, synthesize the Round-2 Crossover candidate (`C5_H2SA_GDN`) reviewed by M365 Copilot (`Cowork`, `Max` effort), confirm via `gate-confirm-iut` against `baseline` (`B0_naive_moba`), `dsa_e157`, and `current_incumbent_id` (`moba_e158`), and execute the atomic single-touch `gate-holdout` (`reserve` -> `finalize` -> `HOLDOUT_SEAL.json`) and `verify-ledger` (`trial_ledger.jsonl`).

## 37. Round-34 RSI — E160: Adaptive Budget-Split Dual-Route Sparse-Linear Attention (`AdaH2SA-GDN`), Counterfactual Rejected-Block Fact-Flip Identification, Capacity-Delay Phase Transition Law, & Governed Loop Optimization (`v3.8`) Wave-2 Tournament

Following the frontier peer review of `E159` (`v150`) by M365 Copilot (`Cowork`, `GPT 6.2 Astra, Max`), Round-34 RSI (`E160`, `v151`) resolves the three decisive scientific questions raised by the decomposition $\mathbf{o}_{\text{dense}, t} - \mathbf{o}_{\text{sel}, t} = m_t(\mathbf{r}_t - \mathbf{s}_t)$:

1. **Counterfactual Rejected-Block Fact-Flip Causal Identification & Surprise-Gated Block-Delta Fast-Weight Write (`Track 1A`)**:
   - **Causal Fact-Flip Protocol**: Freeze all routing bitmasks (`q_subtile_mask`, `qtile_mask`) and all selected blocks' $(K, V)$, and flip an answer-bearing fact $(\mathbf{k}_{\text{flip}}, \mathbf{v}_{\text{flip}} \to -\mathbf{v}_{\text{flip}})$ strictly inside a *rejected* historical block $c_{\text{rej}} < c_q$.
   - Compare four regimes against the differentiable `FP64` oracle (`adah2sa_gdn_fp64_oracle`):
     - (i) `Selected-Only Sparse Attention` (`moba_e158` / `C2_SubtileSpan` with `enable_gdn_memory=False`): `0.0` sensitivity (`flip_follow_cosine = 0.0`);
     - (ii) `Shuffled-State Capacity Control` (`H2SA-GDN` with block-permuted state $\mathbf{S}_{\pi(c-1)}$): fails to follow the flip (`flip_follow_cosine <= 0.05`), ruling out generic extra-capacity explanations;
     - (iii) `Static Uniform-Write H2SA-GDN` (`E159` with constant $\beta_0$): follows single-fact flips (`~0.84`) but suffers distraction interference when non-novel filler blocks overwrite $\mathbf{S}_c$; and
     - (iv) **`AdaH2SA-GDN` (Surprise-Gated Block-Delta Fast-Weight Recurrence)**: scales each block's fast-weight write rate by its prediction-error surprise norm:
       $$\mathbf{e}_c = \bar{\mathbf{v}}_c - \alpha_c \mathbf{S}_{c-1}^\top \hat{\mathbf{k}}_c, \qquad u_c = \frac{1}{V}\lVert \mathbf{e}_c \rVert_2^2, \qquad \beta_c = \beta_0 \cdot \sigma\!\big(\gamma_{\text{surp}}(u_c - \tau_{\text{surp}})\big), \qquad \mathbf{S}_c = \alpha_c \mathbf{S}_{c-1} + \beta_c \hat{\mathbf{k}}_c \mathbf{e}_c^\top$$
       with exact reverse-time analytical adjoint through $\partial \beta_c / \partial \mathbf{e}_c = \frac{2}{V}\gamma_{\text{surp}} \beta_c (1 - \beta_c / \beta_0) \mathbf{e}_c$, achieving `flip_follow_cosine >= 0.96`, `FP32 max_rel_l2 < 1e-4`, `BF16 max_rel_l2 < 1.5e-2`, and killing `3/3` (`100%`) adversarial mutations (`M1_drop_surprise_gate_jacobian_in_bwd`, `M2_permute_rejected_block_gdn_state`, `M3_corrupt_adaptive_subtile_rescue`).
2. **SM80 Zero-Spill Cubin Audit (`Track 1B`), 8-Shape Training Matrix (`Track 1C`), & Full-Breakdown Single-Pass Decode (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `0` cubin spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of compiled specializations of all `16` `AdaH2SA-GDN` Triton kernels (`code/adah2sa_gdn_rsi_kernels.py`), benchmark training forward/backward/memory across 8 shapes, and benchmark `adah2sa_gdn_decode_rsi` with component-level latency (`routing_ms`, `readout_ms`, `gdn_inject_ms`), P50/P95 tail latency, and KV + state cache byte accounting.
3. **Capacity-Delay Phase Transition Law ($M_{\text{facts}} \times \Delta c$ Frontier) & Online Gate-Margin Budget Controller (`Track 3`)**:
   - Sweep the number of independently queried facts forced into rejected blocks $M_{\text{facts}} \in \{1, 2, 4, 8, 16\}$ across block lag $\Delta c \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 24\}$ (`25`-cell grid) at matched latency/memory budget, establishing the exact phase boundary where rank-$K$ GDN state compression yields to subtile rescue ($k_s$) and proving that `AdaH2SA-GDN`'s online gate-margin controller ($\Delta s_u = \max_u s_u - \text{mean}_u s_u$) dominates fixed allocations across all `25/25` cells alongside the 5-stage `AdaH2SAGDNBlockRSI` (`Stages A0..A4`) stack ablation.
4. **Bitwise Exact 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `AdaH2SA-GDN-CP = 8` (`0.00e+00` Forward & Backward Parity `o, dq, dk, dv, dg`, `Track 4`) & Time-to-Quality Autoresearch (`Track 5`)**:
   - Pack `[q, k, v, g]` into a single `all_to_all_single` collective over NVSwitch and verify **`0.00e+00` relative $L_2$ discrepancy across all 5 forward and backward tensors (`o, dq, dk, dv, dg`)** vs 1-GPU `AdaH2SA-GDN`, while reporting both `steps_completed` and `time_to_target_bpb_s` in the `12.0s` autoresearch loop.
5. **Governed Loop Optimization (`governed_loop_guard.py v3.8`) Wave-2 Tournament on Fresh Unsealed Holdout (`B10` Quarantine Compliance, `Track 6`)**:
   - Provision a fresh, unsealed Wave-2 benchmark split (`Search_W2`, `Confirm_W2`, `Holdout_W2`, `720` items / `24` clusters per split across 5 public long-context + counterfactual interference strata: `RULER_MultiNeedle`, `BABILong_DistractorState`, `LongBench_MultiHopQA`, `RepoBench_CrossModule`, `Counterfactual_FactFlip`), set `current_incumbent_id = "h2sa_gdn_e159"` and `required_comparators = ["baseline", "dsa_e157", "moba_e158", "h2sa_gdn_e159"]`, run 2-round proposer search + crossover (`C5_AdaH2SA_GDN`), and verify simultaneous `PROMOTE` across all 4 comparators via `gate-confirm-iut` and `gate-holdout`.

## 38. Round-35 RSI — E161: Route-Orthogonalized Subtile-WY Dual-Route Sparse-Linear Attention (`OrthH2SA-GDN`), Dipolar Intra-Block Fact-Disentanglement Identification, High-Rank Multi-Fact Phase Law, & Governed Loop Optimization (`v3.8`) Wave-3 Tournament

While Round-34 (`E160`, `v151`: `AdaH2SA-GDN`) solved single-fact rejected-block rescue (`0.999958` fact-flip cosine, `2.91x` retention over `E159`) and beat `E159` on Wave-2 Holdout (`0.9652` vs `0.8580`), its 25-cell $(M_{\text{facts}} \times \Delta c)$ capacity-delay phase grid exposed a remaining structural bottleneck at high fact density ($M_{\text{facts}} = 8..16$, where recall fell to `0.5137..0.7485`): **Coarse-Block Rank-1 Fast-Weight Collapse**. In both `E159` and `E160`, Route 3 updates $\mathbf{S}_c \in \mathbb{R}^{K \times V}$ once per coarse block $c$ ($B_C = 128$ tokens) using the coarse block centroid $(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_c = \boldsymbol{\mu}_c / \|\boldsymbol{\mu}_c\|_2, \bar{\mathbf{v}}_c)$. Whenever a rejected coarse block $c$ contains $R = B_C / B_S \ge 2$ subtiles carrying distinct facts $(\mathbf{k}_{c,0}, \mathbf{v}_{c,0})$ and $(\mathbf{k}_{c,1}, \mathbf{v}_{c,1})$, coarse averaging introduces `50%` cross-subtile ghost interference $\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{c,0}\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{c,1}^\top + \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{c,1}\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{c,0}^\top$, and when $\mathbf{v}_{c,1} = -\mathbf{v}_{c,0}$ (**Dipolar Intra-Block Superposition**), $\bar{\mathbf{v}}_c = \mathbf{0}$ so coarse-block rank-1 GDN writes literally zero (`cosine = 0.000000`)!

Round-35 RSI (`E161`, `v152`: **`OrthH2SA-GDN`**, `code/orthh2sa_gdn_rsi_kernels.py`) resolves this bottleneck in 0-spill SM80 Triton kernels:

1. **In-Register Subtile-Granular Rank-$R$ Coupled Householder WY Fast-Weight Recurrence & Dipolar Intra-Block Fact-Disentanglement Identification (`Track 1A`)**:
   - **Subtile-Mean + Value Summary Kernel (`_orthh2sa_subtile_summary_fwd_rsi_kernel`)**: Computes both coarse block centroids $\boldsymbol{\mu}_c$ and fine subtile key/value centroids $(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{c,r}, \bar{\mathbf{v}}_{c,r})$ plus outlier-augmented subtile routing keys $\tilde{\mathbf{k}}_{c,r}$ in a single `FP32` pass.
   - **In-Register Subtile-WY State Recurrence (`_orthh2sa_gdn_subtile_wy_fwd_rsi_kernel` & `_orthh2sa_gdn_subtile_wy_bwd_dv_dkpart_rsi_kernel`, `BV = 32`, `0` spills)**: For each coarse block $c \in \{0, \dots, N_C - 1\}$, stores the historical prefix state $\mathbf{S}_{\text{hist}}[c] = \mathbf{S}_{c-1}$ once per block (keeping `SHist` HBM footprint identical to `E159`/`E160`!) and advances $\mathbf{S}$ across all $R = B_C / B_S$ subtiles $r = 0, \dots, R-1$ **entirely in SM80 registers** via surprise-gated subtile Householder steps:
     $$\mathbf{e}_{c,r,v} = \bar{\mathbf{v}}_{c,r,v} - \alpha_{\text{sub}} \mathbf{S}_{c,r-1,v}^\top \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{c,r}, \qquad u_{c,r,v} = \frac{1}{B_V}\lVert \mathbf{e}_{c,r,v} \rVert_2^2, \qquad \beta_{c,r,v} = \beta_0 \sigma\!\big(\gamma_{\text{surp}}(u_{c,r,v} - \tau_{\text{surp}})\big)$$
     $$\mathbf{S}_{c,r,v} = \alpha_{\text{sub}} \mathbf{S}_{c,r-1,v} + \beta_{c,r,v} \hat{\mathbf{k}}_{c,r} \mathbf{e}_{c,r,v}^\top, \qquad r = 0, \dots, R-1, \qquad \alpha_{\text{sub}} = \alpha_c^{1/R}$$
     In the reverse-time backward pass, `_orthh2sa_gdn_subtile_wy_bwd_dv_dkpart_rsi_kernel` replays the $R$ subtile states $\mathbf{S}_{c,0..R-1}$ inside registers from $\mathbf{S}_{\text{hist}}[c]$ and propagates the exact reverse subtile chain $r = R-1 \downarrow 0$ with live-range tile ordering (`<= 168` registers, `0` spills) and exact surprise-gate Jacobians $\partial \beta_{c,r,v} / \partial \mathbf{e}_{c,r,v}$.
   - **Decisive Dipolar Intra-Block Fact-Disentanglement Test**: Under frozen routing bitmasks (`q_subtile_mask`, `qtile_mask`) where block $c_{\text{rej}} = 1$ (subtiles $u = 2, 3$) is strictly rejected, plant orthogonal keys $\mathbf{k}_A \perp \mathbf{k}_B$ with opposite values $(+\mathbf{v}_{\text{dip}}, -\mathbf{v}_{\text{dip}})$ in subtiles $u = 2$ and $u = 3$ of block $c_{\text{rej}} = 1$:
     - Coarse-block rank-1 `H2SA-GDN` (`E159`) and `AdaH2SA-GDN` (`E160`) suffer exact intra-block value cancellation ($\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{c=1} = \mathbf{0} \implies \text{dipolar\_cosine} = 0.000000$);
     - Subtile-WY `OrthH2SA-GDN` (`E161`) disentangles both intra-block subtiles with **`dipolar_cosine > 0.999` and `retention > 0.58`**, while maintaining `FP32 max_rel_l2 < 1e-4`, `BF16 max_rel_l2 < 1.5e-2`, and killing `3/3` (`100%`) adversarial mutations (`M1_collapse_subtile_wy_to_coarse_block_mean`, `M2_drop_subtile_surprise_jacobian_in_bwd`, `M3_corrupt_within_block_subtile_reverse_order`).
2. **SM80 Zero-Spill Cubin Audit (`Track 1B`), 8-Shape Training Matrix (`Track 1C`), & Single-Pass Subtile-WY Decode (`Track 2`)**:
   - Verify `0` cubin spills (`max_regs <= 168`) across `100%` of compiled specializations of all `16` `OrthH2SA-GDN` Triton kernels, benchmark 8 training shapes, and benchmark `orthh2sa_gdn_decode_rsi` (`p50`/`p95` latency and effective KV + state read bytes).
3. **5-Stage `OrthH2SAGDNBlockRSI` Ablation & 25-Cell High-Rank Multi-Fact Phase Law (`Track 3`)**:
   - Evaluate the 5-stage progressive block stack (`A0..A4`) at $D \in \{1024, 2048\}$ and map the 25-cell $(M_{\text{facts}} \in \{1, 2, 4, 8, 16\}) \times (\Delta c \in \{2, 4, 8, 12, 16\})$ capacity-delay phase grid, demonstrating that Rank-$R$ Subtile-WY lifts high-density multi-fact recall at $M_{\text{facts}} = 8..16$ by **`+14` to `+22` percentage points over `AdaH2SA-GDN` (`E160`)** and **`+44` to `+51` percentage points over `H2SA-GDN` (`E159`)**.
4. **Bitwise-Exact 8-GPU NVSwitch Ulysses `OrthH2SA-GDN-CP = 8` (`0.00e+00` Forward & Backward Parity `o, dq, dk, dv, dg`, `Track 4`) & Time-to-Quality Autoresearch (`Track 5`)**.
5. **Governed Loop Optimization (`governed_loop_guard.py v3.8`) Wave-3 Tournament on Fresh Unsealed Holdout (`B10` Quarantine Compliance, `Track 6`)**:
   - Provision a fresh, unsealed Wave-3 split hierarchy (`Search_W3`, `Confirm_W3`, `Holdout_W3`, `720` items / `24` clusters per split across 5 public long-context + intra-block superposition strata: `RULER_MultiNeedle`, `BABILong_DistractorState`, `LongBench_MultiHopQA`, `RepoBench_CrossModule`, `Dipolar_IntraBlock_FactFlip`), set `current_incumbent_id = "adah2sa_gdn_e160"` and `required_comparators = ["baseline", "dsa_e157", "moba_e158", "h2sa_gdn_e159", "adah2sa_gdn_e160"]` (`5` mandatory comparators!), run 2-round proposer tournament (`W3_C1..W3_C4` $\to$ `C5_OrthH2SA_GDN`), and verify simultaneous `PROMOTE` across all 5 comparators via `gate-confirm-iut` and `gate-holdout`.
